One day, a little girl named Sue went to the park. She saw a hidden toy under a big tree. It was a pretty ball. Sue wanted to play with the ball, but she did not know who it belonged to.
Sue saw a boy near the tree. She asked the boy, "Can I borrow this ball? What is your name?" The boy said, "My name is Tom. You can borrow the ball, but it is not mine."
Sue and Tom played with the ball. They had a lot of fun. Then, a small dog ran up to them. The dog took the ball in its mouth and ran away. Sue and Tom were surprised. They did not know the ball belonged to the dog. They laughed and played with the dog, too.

Ben and Lily were playing with their blocks in the living room. They liked to build towers and houses and cars with the blocks. They were having fun and laughing.
But then, Mom came in and said, "Kids, it's time to relax. You have been playing for a long time. Let's go to your room and read a book."
Ben and Lily did not want to stop playing. They wanted to keep building with the blocks. They said, "No, Mom, we don't want to relax. We want to play more."
Mom said, "I know you like to play, but you also need to rest. Relaxing is good for your body and your mind. It helps your stomach feel better and your brain stay alert. Come on, let's go to your room and I'll read you a nice story."
Ben and Lily still did not want to go. They started to cry and whine. They said, "No, no, no, we want to play, we want to play."
Mom was not happy. She said, "Enough, kids. You are being very noisy and rude. You need to listen to me and do what I say. Now, put away your blocks and follow me to your room. Or else, you will have no dessert tonight."
Ben and Lily did not like that. They did not want to miss dessert. They loved dessert. They looked at each other and decided to do what Mom said. They put away their blocks and followed Mom to their room. They hoped Mom would read them a good story and give them dessert later. They thought maybe relaxing was not so bad after all.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Amy. Amy loved to play outside in the sun. One day, she saw a big puddle. She jumped in the puddle and got all wet. Amy's mom told her to go inside and dry off.
Inside, Amy saw her mom making a yummy cake. Her mom said, "If you help me make the cake, you can have a big slice." Amy wanted to help, but she also wanted to play with her friends. She had to make a choice.
Amy decided to help her mom. Together, they made the cake and put it in the oven. When the cake was done, they took it out and let it cool. Then, they cut the cake and Amy got a big slice. The cake was very yummy, and Amy was happy. She learned that helping others can make you feel good and sometimes, you get a nice reward too.

One day, a little dog named Spot wanted to perform a dance for his friends. He practiced and practiced all day long. Spot was very excited to show his dance to everyone.
Spot invited all his friends to a meeting under the big tree. It was a hot day, and the sun was shining bright. All the animals came to watch Spot's dance. "Hello, friends!" Spot said. "I want to show you my dance!"
But just as Spot was about to start, a big cat named Fluffy came to the meeting. Fluffy didn't want to watch Spot dance. "No dancing!" Fluffy said. "I don't like it!" Spot was sad, but he didn't want to make Fluffy mad.
All the other animals wanted to see Spot dance, so they talked to Fluffy. "Please, Fluffy," they said. "Let Spot perform his dance. It will be fun!" Fluffy thought for a moment, and then agreed. "Okay," Fluffy said. "But only if I can dance too!"
So Spot and Fluffy danced together under the big tree. All the animals clapped and cheered. The meeting was fun, and everyone was happy. Spot and Fluffy learned that dancing is more fun when you do it with friends.

When the game was over, the boy showed them his special lollipop. His friends were very happy. They said it looked delicious. The boy smiled and showed it around. Everyone was excited.
The friends decided to divide the lollipop among themselves. They each got their own piece, and each piece was unique. The boy was so happy to share his lollipop with his friends. They all had a great time.

Once upon a time, in a small town, there was a shy boy named Tim. Tim had a big heart, but he was too scared to make new friends. One day, Tim found some money on the ground. He wanted to find the person who lost it, but he was too shy to ask people.
Tim decided to look for clues. He saw a girl named Sue crying by a tree. He thought maybe the money belonged to her. Tim wanted to make her feel better, so he gave her a big hug. Sue stopped crying and smiled at Tim. He asked if the money was hers. Sue said yes, and thanked Tim for finding it.
From that day on, Tim and Sue became best friends. They played together every day. Tim learned that sometimes, all it takes is a hug to make someone feel better. He wasn't shy anymore, and he made many more friends in the town. And they all lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time there was a yellow table. It belonged to a little boy who was three years old. One day he wanted to try something new with the table. So he decided to reverse it!
He grabbed his little hands and pushed on the table. The table started to move backwards and it went so fast! It went so far that it almost tipped over.
The little boy was surprised. He had never seen the table move like that. He thought it was a fun game and started to reverse the table again. He was laughing and having lots of fun!
When he was done playing with the table, he put it back in its original place. He was proud of his work and gave the yellow table a big hug. He knew that they would be good friends forever.

Once upon a time, there was a chubby cat named Kitty. Kitty loved to drink tea with her best friend, a little girl named Sue. They would sit in the garden and have tea parties every day.
One day, while they were having tea, Kitty and Sue heard a sound. They stopped and listened. It was a little bird, singing a beautiful song. Sue said, "Let's listen to the bird sing while we drink our tea."
So, Kitty and Sue listened to the bird sing while they drank their tea. They felt very happy. And from that day on, they always invited the bird to join their tea parties, and they all became best friends.

Lily and Max were playing in the park. They liked to pretend they were knights and princesses. They had a big stick for a sword and a blanket for a cape.
"Look, there is a dragon!" Max said, pointing to a hill. He ran towards it with his stick. "I will save you, princess!"
Lily followed him, but she was nervous. She did not like dragons. They were big and scary and had fire. She hoped it was not a real dragon.
They reached the hill and saw a big green shape. It had scales and wings and a long tail. It was sleeping in the sun.
"Shh, be quiet," Max whispered. "We have to guess how to defeat it."
Lily looked at the dragon. She saw something shiny on its neck. It was a necklace with a key.
"Maybe the key is its weakness," she said. "Maybe it opens a door to its treasure."
Max nodded. He had an idea. He crept closer to the dragon. He tried to grab the key.
But the dragon woke up. It opened its eyes and saw Max. It roared and breathed fire.
"Run!" Max shouted. He dropped the stick and ran back to Lily.
Lily and Max ran away from the hill. They were scared and out of breath.
"That was not a dragon," Max said. "That was a monster!"
Lily agreed. She hugged Max. She was glad they were safe.
They decided to play something else. They did not like dragons anymore.

One day, John had an idea. He got a bucket full of water and put it in front of his sister. She was so fast and hairy, she couldn't stop herself in time! John had let her win the game, but he was the real winner because he had tricked her.

Once upon a time, a dad wanted to do something special for his three year old daughter. He went to the store and got a big, orange pumpkin. When he got home, he called for his daughter and said, "Let's unpack this pumpkin together!"
So the dad and daughter unpacked the pumpkin together. Firstly, they took off the lid to see what was inside. They were surprised to see lots of seeds!
The dad found a spoon and they started scooping out the seeds together. They had so much fun exploring the inside of the pumpkin!
When they were done, they cleaned up together. The dad was impressed by the polite way his daughter put the pumpkin lid back on. They put the seeds in the bin and then put the pumpkin outside.
They hugged and said, "We did a great job unpacking that pumpkin!" It was a special time for them both.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim loved to look up at the sky and see the stars. He wanted to go to a planet far, far away. His mom told him about an incredible planet that would turn around and around.

But then, Tim turned too fast. He fell out of the box and hit his head. He cried and cried. His mom came and hugged him tight. She said, "I'm sorry, Tim. We can't go to the incredible planet today." Tim was very sad, but he knew he could try again another day.

Once upon a time, there was a big room where kids came to learn. In the room, there was a blackboard. A kind teacher, Miss Lily, wrote on the blackboard with pretty colors. All the kids loved to watch her write and draw on the blackboard.
One day, a new kid named Timmy joined the class. Timmy was a bit careless. He liked to run around and make noise. Miss Lily told him to be careful, but Timmy did not listen. He ran towards the blackboard and bumped into it, making all the pretty colors fall down.
The kids were sad about the pretty colors falling down. Miss Lily told Timmy to be more careful next time. Timmy felt bad and said sorry. The next day, Timmy was very careful. He helped Miss Lily pick up the pretty colors and put them back on the blackboard. All the kids were happy and they all applauded Timmy for being careful and helpful.
The moral of the story is to always be careful and listen to others, so we can keep everyone happy and safe.

But the ugly frog was not careful. He splashed too much water. The water spilled on the ground and made a big mess. Lucy was sad.
"Oh no, you spoil it!" Lucy said. The frog felt bad. He tried again and filled the bucket with just the right amount of water. Lucy was happy. She thanked the frog, and they became friends.

Lily was very happy to find the key. She wanted to know what the key could open. She looked all around the house. She tried the key in many doors, but it did not fit. Then, she saw a small door she had never seen before. Lily thought the gold key might fit in this door.
Lily put the gold key into the small door and turned it. The door opened, and Lily entered a new room. Inside the room, there were many more different toys! Lily played with the new toys all day long. She was glad she found the gold key and the new room with different toys.

One day, as they played, Sue made her doll enter the toy house. She said, "I am going to find a new word today." Tom made his toy car enter the house too. He said, "I will help you find the new word."
As they played, they heard a soft voice coming from the toy house. It said, "I know a new word!" Sue and Tom looked at each other, surprised. They did not know where the voice came from. They looked inside the toy house and found a small book. The book was talking!
The talking book told them the new word, "friend." Sue and Tom were happy to learn the new word. They played with the talking book and their toys for the rest of the day. From that day on, the talking book was their new friend and always taught them new words.

Tom: "Hello, who are you?"
Girl: "Hello, Tom. I am Lisa. Would you like to come and play with me?"
Tom smiled and swam over to the bank. He climbed out of the river and started to play with Lisa. They chased each other around and had lots of fun.
Tom enjoyed his time with Lisa and soon it was time to go home. He waved goodbye to Lisa and moved back to his river. Tom was happy to have made a new friend!

One day, a friendly dog named Max went for a walk. He saw a big building. It was a jail. Max was curious and wanted to see what was inside.
As Max walked closer, he saw a man behind the bars. The man looked sad. Max said, "Hi, why are you sad?" The man replied, "I am in jail and miss my dog." Max felt sorry for the man and wanted to help.
Max had an idea. He found a big stick and used it to open the jail door. The man was free! They both ran away from the jail. Max and the man became best friends. Max was happy he could help and gain a new friend.

Lily and Tom were playing in the park. They liked to pick flowers and make crowns. They saw a big bush with many red flowers. They ran to the bush and started to pick the flowers.
But the bush was not in the park. It was in the garden of a house. The house belonged to a grumpy man. He did not like children. He saw Lily and Tom picking his flowers. He came out of his house and shouted at them.
"Go away! Stop picking my flowers! They are mine!" he said. He was very angry. He tried to catch Lily and Tom.
Lily and Tom were scared. They dropped the flowers and ran away. They did not know where to go. They saw a police car on the street. They ran to the police car and knocked on the window.
A police woman opened the window. She saw Lily and Tom. They were crying and shaking. She asked them what happened. They told her about the grumpy man and the flowers.
The police woman was kind. She said, "Don't worry. You are safe now. I will talk to the grumpy man and explain that you did not mean to hurt his flowers. But you should not pick flowers from other people's gardens. They might get upset. You should only pick flowers from the park or ask permission first. Do you understand?"
Lily and Tom nodded. They said they were sorry. They thanked the police woman. She gave them a hug and a sticker. She said, "You are good children. Next time, be careful where you pick flowers. And always stay away from strangers who shout at you. OK?"
Lily and Tom said OK. They learned their lesson. They went back to the park and played with their friends. They were happy and safe.

She called her mom. "Mom, mom, I want the bottle!"
Mom came to the table. She saw the bottle. She shook her head. "No, Sara, you can't have this bottle. It is hot. It has tea. It will hurt you."
Sara was sad. She still wanted some water. She made a face. "But I'm thirsty, mom!"
Mom smiled. She hugged Sara. "I know, sweetie. I care about you. I don't want you to get burned. I have another bottle for you. It is cold. It has juice. It will make you happy."
She took Sara to the fridge. She opened it. She gave Sara a cold bottle. Sara smiled. She drank the juice. It was yummy. She thanked her mom. "Thank you, mom. You are the best!"
Mom kissed Sara. "You're welcome, Sara. You are my love!"

Tom wanted to play with the spear outside. He asked his mom if he could enter the yard. His mom said yes, but be careful with the spear. Tom went outside and started to play with his friends.
Tom and his friends pretended to be warriors with the fake spear. They took turns holding the spear and running around. They had a lot of fun playing with the spear. At the end of the day, Tom and his friends went home, tired but happy.

Lily liked to play in the field with her red ball. She liked to throw it high and catch it, or roll it on the grass. Sometimes she saw animals in the field, like birds and rabbits. But she never saw a cow.
One day, she heard a loud moo from behind a big tree. She wondered what it was. She ran to the tree and looked around. She saw a big, white cow. It had black spots and a long tail. It looked at her with big eyes and mooed again.
Lily was not afraid. She smiled and said hello to the cow. She wanted to play with it. She threw her red ball to the cow and said, "Catch!" The cow did not catch the ball. It did not know how to play. It just watched the ball bounce on the ground and roll away.
Lily ran after the ball and picked it up. She tried again. She threw the ball to the cow and said, "Catch!" The cow did not catch the ball. It did not want to play. It just ate some grass and flicked its tail.
Lily was sad. She wanted the cow to be her friend. She walked to the cow and gave it a hug. She said, "Please play with me. You are a nice cow." The cow did not hug her back. It did not like hugs. It just mooed and pushed her away.
Lily was very sad. She cried and ran back to the tree. She sat under the shade and hugged her red ball. She wished the cow would appear and play with her. But the cow did not appear. It stayed in the field and chewed its cud. Lily felt lonely and bored. She missed her mom and dad. She wanted to go home.

One day, a little boy named Tim was feeling very naughty. He wanted to play and have fun, but his mom told him to relax. "Tim, please relax," she said. "Your dad has an important operation today, and we need to be calm."
Tim did not want to relax. He wanted to jump and run. So, he went outside and played with his ball. He kicked it high in the sky and laughed. But then, the ball went into the street. Tim knew he should not go there, but he wanted his ball back.
Just as Tim was about to get the ball, his mom came out. "Tim, that is naughty! You must stay away from the street," she said. Tim felt sad, but he knew his mom was right. They went inside, and Tim decided to relax with his mom. They sat on the couch and waited for dad to come home from his operation.

Tim wanted to show his friends his mine. They all came to see it. But when they got there, the hole was full of mud. Tim's mine was very dirty. He tried to clean it, but he couldn't. Tim felt embarrass because his mine was dirty.
Tim's friends saw that he was sad. They decided to help him. They all worked together to clean the dirty mine. After they were done, Tim's mine was clean and pretty. Tim was happy and not embarrass anymore. They all played in the mine and had a great time.

One day, two children called Jenny and Billy were playing near the shore. They were very happy, but they were also a bit anxious. All of a sudden, they noticed a huge sack on the sand. It was too big for either of them to carry, so they decided to play a game.
They each took one of the handles, and they each pulled their end as hard as they could. The sack was so heavy they had to strain, but eventually they managed to move it.
Together, they worked to drag the sack onto the shore. They were both so happy and relieved when the job was done!
They opened the sack and found the most amazing present inside – a fantastic toy boat! They were so excited that they ran off to play and sail the boat in the sea. They had a wonderful time!
The anxious children played happily for the rest of the day, until the sun started to set. Then, they packed up the sack and took it home, full of joy.

The good subway was warm and dry. People were happy inside. The rain did not come in. The subway took them home. They were safe and dry. And everyone lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time, there lived a black cat called Party. Party was always very excited to go outside and listen to the birds singing in the trees.
One day, a strange man wearing a black hat showed up to the woods where Party liked to spend his time. The man started making loud noises and didn't seem to be aware of his surroundings. Party tried to stay away, but the man kept getting closer and closer.
Party managed to signal some of the other animals and they came together to plan how to get rid of the dangerous man. "Let's make a party," they suggested. But how would they do it when they could barely make any noise?
The animals decided to listenthe man's footsteps and when they were loudest they made a huge amount of noise with the sticks and stones, scaring the man off. Everyone was very happy and celebrated with a big party.
Party had managed to save the day and all the other animals were so thankful for all of his help.

"Wow!" she said, "It's so unique!"
"I know!" said the other girl, "It's amazing!"
The girls quickly became friends with the artist who had made the sculpture. He showed them how he had made it out of pieces of metal and how it had taken him many hours to perfect. The little girls were amazed and admired the artist's hard work.
The artist then presented them each with a small piece from the sculpture as a gift. The girls were so excited and thanked him for the unique and special gift.
The girls left the park feeling very special, both with their own piece of the unique sculpture to remember the day forever.

One day, a little girl named Lily went to the park with her mom. Lily saw a crazy pony running around. The pony was jumping and running in circles. Lily wanted to play with the pony, so she ran to it.
Lily said, "Hi, pony! Do you want to play with me?" But the pony did not listen. It just kept running and jumping. Lily felt sad because the pony ignored her. She did not know why the pony did not want to play with her.
Lily's mom saw her feeling sad and went to her. She gave Lily a big hug and said, "Don't worry, Lily. Sometimes, animals don't want to play. Maybe the pony is just too excited today. Let's go play on the swings instead." Lily smiled and went to play on the swings with her mom, feeling happy again.

Once upon a time, there were two best friends, Tom and Sarah. They were walking along the beach and watching the sun setting. Suddenly, Tom saw a whale in the sea.
Sarah said “Be careful Tom, the whale might get you!”
Tom replied “No, I think whales are graceful creatures.”
They decided to get closer and look at the whale from the shore. Tom said “Hey whale, how can I help you?”
The whale replied “Thank you for asking. Would you please go fishing and serve me fresh fish?”
Tom and Sarah went fishing and served the whale fresh fish. The whale thanked them and went away.
The moral of this story is that we should do our best to help others, no matter how small the job may be. As Tom and Sarah demonstrated, even a small service like providing fresh fish to a whale can make a big difference.

One sunny day, Tim was playing with his ball and it got stuck on a tree. The tree was very tall, and Tim could not reach it. He tried to stand on his tippy-toes, but he still could not reach the ball. Tim felt sad and bit his lip, thinking about what to do.
Then, Tim had an idea. He asked his friends to help him. They all stood on each other's shoulders, making a tall tower. Tim climbed up and got his heavy ball back. They all laughed and cheered, happy to play together again. And they lived happily ever after.

The man said, "Okay, let's hear you sing." Tom sang a loud and funny song. The people laughed and clapped. Tom was happy. The show went on with Tom as the star. And that's how the nosy cat became an opera singer.

Once upon a time, there was a loyal dog named Buddy. Buddy loved to go to the office with his owner, Sam. Every day, they went to the office together.
One day, Sam told Buddy, "I have to give a big talk in front of many people. I am very scared." Buddy wagged his tail and looked at Sam. Sam knew Buddy would stay by his side no matter what.
The day of the big talk came. Sam was very nervous, but Buddy was there. As Sam started to speak, Buddy sat by his feet. When Sam finished, everyone in the office started to applaud. They clapped their hands and cheered. Buddy barked and wagged his tail, happy that his owner did a great job.
After the big talk, Sam looked at Buddy and said, "Thank you for being with me. You are the best loyal friend I could ever have." Buddy barked and licked Sam's face, showing his love.
From that day on, Sam and Buddy were even closer. They went to the office together every day, and Sam was never scared again.

Lily and Anna were sisters who liked to play with their mom's makeup. One day, when mom was out, they sneaked into her room and opened her makeup box. They saw many pretty colors and shiny things. They wanted to try them on their faces.
"Look, Lily, this is red lipstick. It makes your lips look like a rose," Anna said, and put some on her mouth.
"And this is blue eyeshadow. It makes your eyes look like the sky," Lily said, and rubbed some on her eyelids.
They laughed and admired each other in the mirror. They felt very lucky to have such fun things to play with.
But then, they heard the door open. It was mom, coming back home. They panicked and tried to hide the makeup box under the bed. But they were too late. Mom saw them and their messy faces.
"Girls, what have you done? You have destroyed my makeup! That was very naughty of you. You know you are not allowed to touch my things without asking," mom said, very angry.
Lily and Anna felt very sorry and scared. They started to cry and apologize.
"Mom, we are sorry. We just wanted to look pretty like you. We didn't mean to destroy your makeup," Lily said.
"Mom, we are sorry. We didn't know it was so important to you. We thought it was just for fun," Anna said.
Mom sighed and calmed down. She hugged them and wiped their faces with a wet cloth.
"I know you didn't mean to destroy my makeup, girls. But you have to understand that makeup is not a toy. It is something I use for special occasions. And it is very expensive and hard to replace. You have to respect my things and ask me before you use them. Do you understand?" mom said.
Lily and Anna nodded and said they understood. They promised to never touch mom's makeup again without permission. Mom smiled and kissed them.
"I love you, girls. You are very lucky to have each other. And you are pretty enough without makeup. Now, let's go and have some cookies and milk. And maybe later, we can play dress up with some old clothes and accessories. How does that sound?" mom said.
Lily and Anna cheered up and said that sounded great. They followed mom to the kitchen, holding hands. They learned their lesson and were happy to have a kind mom.

Tommy was walking in the swamp, it was so lonely. He wanted to find somebody to play with. But the swamp was so quiet. He sang out loud and clapped his hands, hoping somebody would come to play. Suddenly he heard clapping back. Excited he ran towards the sound. It was coming from a huge frog.
He looked at the frog and said: "Hi, I'm Tommy. Let's play together!"
But the frog didn't say anything.
It just hopped away.
Tommy felt really lonely again. He continued to walk in the swamp and clapped his hands in the air.
But then he heard a scary noise coming from above.
It was a big bird, staring down at him.
Tommy was so scared, he started to cry.
The bird swooped down and grabbed him in its claws. Then it flew away with Tommy in its claws, never to be seen again.

Once upon a time, in a small village, there was a boy named Tom. Tom had a toy spear. He loved to play with his spear all day long. One day, Tom felt uncomfortable. His tummy hurt a lot. He wondered what was wrong.
Tom went to his mom and asked her a question. "Mom, why does my tummy hurt?" His mom thought for a moment and said, "Maybe you ate too much candy, Tom." Tom knew he ate a lot of candy, so he thought his mom was right.
Tom's mom told him to rest and not play with his spear for a while. Tom listened to his mom and took a nap. When he woke up, he felt much better. Tom learned that eating too much candy can make him feel uncomfortable. He decided to eat less candy and play with his spear more.

"Let's play a game," Tom says. "Who can get closer to the rat? The one who gets closer wins."
Sam is afraid of the rat. He does not want to play. He says, "No, Tom. That is a bad game. The rat can bite us. We should stay away."
But Tom does not listen. He wants to win. He says, "Don't be a baby, Sam. I will go first. Watch me."
Tom walks slowly to the rat. The rat sees Tom. It makes a loud noise. It runs to Tom. It jumps on Tom. It bites Tom on the arm. Tom screams. He falls to the ground. He cries.
Sam runs to Tom. He tries to help him. He says, "Tom, are you okay? I told you it was a bad game. We should have stayed safe."
But Tom does not answer. He is hurt. He is bleeding. He is not okay. He does not win. He loses.

One day, a cute baby bunny was playing in the grass. He saw a big hole in the ground. He was curious and wanted to see what was inside. He crawled to the hole and looked down. He saw water and his own face.
"Hello, bunny!" he said to his face. "Do you want to play with me?"
But his face did not answer. It only copied what he said and did. The baby bunny was confused and sad. He wanted a real friend, not a face in the water.
He heard a noise behind him. He turned around and saw another cute baby bunny. The other bunny had brown fur and long ears. He smiled and hopped to the hole.
"Hi, I'm Coco. What are you doing?" he asked.
"I'm trying to talk to this bunny, but he won't talk back. He just repeats me," the first bunny said.
"That's not a bunny, silly. That's a well. It has water and it shows your face. It can't talk or play," Coco explained.
"Really? I didn't know that. I thought it was a friend," the first bunny said.
"Well, now you know. And you don't have to be sad, because I can be your friend. Do you want to play with me?" Coco asked.
"Yes, I do. You are very nice and cute. What is your name?" the first bunny asked.
"My name is Lulu. And you are very nice and cute, too. Come on, let's play in the grass. We can hop and run and have fun," Lulu said.
"Okay, let's go. Thank you for being my friend, Coco," Lulu said.
They crawled away from the well and played in the grass. They were happy and became the best of friends.

Amber then asked if she can get married when she grows up. Her parents were shocked and asked her why she wanted to get married.
Amber looked guilty and said nothing. After a long silence, she started to talk.
"I want to get married in order to be able to make my own family. So that I can have someone who loves me and someone to love back," she said.
Her parents were so moved by her words that they hugged her tight. They both promised to never let Amber feel alone and to always love her, no matter what.

Tim and Sam get their toys back. They are happy. They play cricket again. They forget about the bug. They have fun.

Sam was a little boy who liked to play with his red cap. He wore it every day, even when it was hot or cold. He felt happy and brave with his cap on his head.
One day, he went to the park with his mom and his dog, Rex. He ran and jumped and threw a ball for Rex to catch. He had a lot of fun. But then, a big wind came and blew his cap away. He saw it fly over the fence and into the street. He felt sad and scared. He wanted his cap back.
He ran to the fence and tried to climb over it, but it was too high. He called for his mom, but she was too far away. He felt helpless. He did not know what to do.
Then, he heard a bark. He looked and saw Rex running after his cap. Rex was fast and smart. He dodged the cars and grabbed the cap in his mouth. He ran back to the fence and jumped over it. He brought the cap to Sam and wagged his tail.
Sam was so happy. He hugged Rex and thanked him. He put his cap back on his head. He felt happy and brave again. He said to Rex, "You are a good dog. You care for me. You are my best friend." Rex licked his face and barked. He was happy too. They went to find mom and play some more.

Once upon a time, there was a little bunny named Bob. Bob loved to hop all day long. One day, while Bob was hopping, he found a big pit. He was very curious about what was inside.
Bob looked into the pit and saw many different things. There were red balls, blue blocks, and yellow bears. Bob wanted to play with all of them. So, he hopped into the pit and started to play.
Bob had lots of fun in the pit. He hopped from one toy to another. He played with the red balls, the blue blocks, and the yellow bears. After a while, Bob was tired. He hopped out of the pit and went home. He was happy he found the pit with all the different toys.

Once upon a time, in a small house, there was a boy named Tim. Tim loved to play outside. One day, he fell down and hurt his leg. Tim was sad because he could not play with his friends.
Tim's mom wanted to help him gain his strength back. She cooked a big pot of vegetable soup. The soup had carrots, peas, and corn. Tim's mom told him, "Eat this soup, and you will feel better soon."
Tim ate the vegetable soup every day. Soon, his leg did not hurt anymore. He was so happy that he could play outside again. Tim's friends were happy too. They all played together and had lots of fun.

Once upon a time, there was a big, scary bear. He lived in a dark forest. He did not permit any animal to come near his home. He did not like trouble.
One sunny day, a small, cute bunny came to the forest. The bunny did not know about the scary bear. The bunny saw the bear's home and thought it was a fun place to play. The bunny went near the bear's home, but the bear saw the bunny and got angry.
The bear came out of his home to scare the bunny away. But when the bear saw the bunny, he did not feel scary anymore. The bunny was not scared of the bear. The bunny smiled at the bear and said, "Let's be friends!" The bear was so surprised. He never had a friend before. The bear and the bunny became best friends, and they played together in the forest every day. The scary bear was not scary anymore, and he permitted all the animals to come near his home.

Once upon a time, there was a little dancer. She loved to dance and play all day. One day, she wanted to leave her home and dance in the big world.
The dancer was frightened, but she was brave too. She left her home and started to dance. She danced in the sun and under the trees. She danced with the butterflies and the birds. She was very happy.
But then, it started to get dark. The dancer was scared. She wanted to go home, but she did not know the way. She tried to find her way back, but she could not. The frightened dancer was lost and sad. She danced no more.

One day, there was a boy named Nate. He was outside playing and he noticed something bright in the west. He ran to get a better look and he was sure it was his friend, Emma.
Nate smiled and waved in the direction of the bright light in the west. It was getting closer. Suddenly, he heard a voice. It was Emma shouting, “Hurry, Nate! Follow me!”
Nate started running towards the light. But then he saw a big, scary monster blocking the path. The monster wanted to stop them from getting to the light.
Nate was scared. He wanted to get away from the monster. But Emma called out, “Don’t be afraid. Just hurry, Nate! We can make it to the light in time.”
Nate didn’t waste any time. He ran as fast as he could towards the bright light in the west. Emma was right, they made it! The monster was gone and they were free.

Once upon a time, there was a swan named Suki. Suki liked to hang out by the pond with her friends. The pond was a fun place to play and swim. They always had a good time.
One day, Suki saw something shiny in the water. She swam over and picked it up. It was a pretty, expensive necklace. Suki's friend, Lily, saw the necklace and said, "Wow, that's so pretty! Where did you find it?"
Suki said, "I found it in the water. I think someone lost it. We should give it back." So, Suki and Lily went around the pond, asking everyone if they lost a necklace. But no one said it was theirs.
Finally, Suki and Lily found a little girl crying by the pond. The little girl said, "I lost my mommy's necklace. It's very expensive and she will be sad." Suki showed her the necklace and the little girl smiled. She thanked Suki and Lily for finding it.
From that day on, Suki, Lily, and the little girl became best friends. They always played together by the pond and had so much fun. And they all lived happily ever after.

One day, a little boy named Tim and his mom went to the store. They needed food for their house. Tim saw a big freezer. He wanted to look inside. His mom said, "Okay, but be careful."
Tim opened the freezer and saw many cold things. He pointed at a big ice cream. "Mom, can we get this?" he asked. His mom said, "That's fair, but we need to share it with your sister."
When they got home, they opened the freezer to get the ice cream. But, to their surprise, they found a small toy car inside the ice cream box! Tim and his sister were so happy. They played with the toy car and ate the ice cream together. It was a fun day for Tim and his family.

One day, a little girl and her daddy went to the store. They wanted to buy a toy. The girl saw a big, pretty doll. She asked her daddy, "Can I have the doll, please?"
Daddy looked at the price. It was not cheap. He said, "Sorry, we can't buy this doll. It's not cheap. Let's find a cheaper toy." The girl was sad, but she understood.
They looked around and found a small, cheap toy. It was a little horse that could stand. The girl was happy with the new toy. She said, "Thank you, daddy!" They went home and played together.
The girl learned that it's okay to not have the most expensive toy. The cheap toy was just as fun to play with. The moral of the story is that you don't need expensive things to be happy.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lily. She was an adventurous girl who loved to explore her big garden. One sunny day, she saw a small tree with prunes on it. The prunes were purple and round. Lily thought they looked yummy.
Lily went to her mom and said, "Mom, can I pick the prunes from the tree?" Her mom smiled and said, "Yes, Lily, but you must pay me with a hug first." Lily hugged her mom tightly, and then she went back to the tree.
Lily picked the prunes carefully and put them in a basket. She felt happy and proud. She took the basket to her mom and they both ate the sweet prunes together. It was a fun and tasty adventure for Lily.

Once upon a time in a far away land, there lived two friends. One was called Bob and the other was called Sam.
Bob was very attractive and was always smiling. Sam was very supportive of Bob and always there to lend a helping hand when needed.
One day, Sam invited Bob over for a barbecue. Sam had set up a very attractive grill in his garden. Bob was so excited and they began to cook various delicious treats.
As they sat down to eat, Bob said to Sam, "thank you for inviting me over. I really appreciate all your support over the years." Sam smiled and replied, "of course, you can always count on me for support".
They enjoyed their barbeque and both went home happy and full.

Tom loved hockey. He liked to skate fast and hit the puck with his stick. He played hockey with his friends every week. They had fun and learned new skills.
But one day, Tom had a bad game. He missed the puck many times. He fell down on the ice. He got angry and sad. He did not want to play hockey anymore. He told his coach he wanted to quit.
His coach was surprised. He asked Tom why he wanted to quit. Tom said hockey was too hard and not fair. He said his friends were better than him and he could not catch up.
His coach listened to Tom. He said he understood how Tom felt. He said hockey was a game that took practice and patience. He said everyone had good and bad days. He said Tom had a lot of talent and potential. He said quitting was not the answer.
He asked Tom to give hockey another chance. He said he would help Tom improve his skills and confidence. He said he would make sure Tom had fun and felt fair. He said he was proud of Tom for trying and not giving up.
Tom thought about what his coach said. He realized his coach was right. He decided to stay on the hockey team. He thanked his coach for his support and advice. He said he was sorry for wanting to quit. He said he loved hockey and wanted to keep playing.
His coach smiled and hugged Tom. He said he was glad Tom changed his mind. He said he was happy to have Tom on his team. He said he was sure Tom would do great and have fun. He said he was ready to help Tom anytime.
Tom felt better and hopeful. He put on his helmet and gloves. He grabbed his stick and puck. He joined his friends on the ice. He skated fast and hit the puck with his stick. He played hockey with his friends. They had fun and learned new skills.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lucy. She loved to play and pretend with her toys. One day, she got a new toy from her mom. It was a cute little cat. Lucy was so happy!
Lucy played with her new cat all day. They had tea parties and played hide and seek. Then, something unexpected happened. The little cat jumped and made a loud sound. Lucy was in shock! She thought her cat was just a toy.
But the cat was not a toy. It was a real cat! The cat had been pretending to be a toy all this time. Lucy was surprised, but she was also happy. Now, she had a real cat friend to play with. They played and had fun together every day.

One day, a cat named Kitty wanted to have a party. She wanted to invite all her friends. She told her friend Dog, "Let's have a big party!"
At the party, Kitty and Dog played a game called catch. They threw a ball and tried to catch it. All the other animals joined in and had fun too. But then, a little mouse named Squeaky showed up. Squeaky wanted to play catch, but Kitty wanted to catch Squeaky instead.
Squeaky said, "Please don't catch me! I just want to play and have fun at the party too!" Kitty thought about it and decided to let Squeaky play. They all played catch together and had a great time.
At the end of the party, they all ate spicy food. Squeaky tried the spicy food and said, "Wow, this is very spicy!" All the animals laughed and enjoyed the party.
From that day on, Kitty, Dog, and Squeaky became good friends. They played catch and had many more parties together.

Once upon a time, there was a big truck named Tim. Tim was very useful. He liked to help people. Tim could go very fast, with lots of speed.
One sunny day, Tim saw a little girl named Sue. Sue was sad. She had a cup but no water to pour in it. Tim wanted to help Sue.
Tim went to the river and filled a big bucket with water. He went back to Sue and poured the water into her cup. Sue was happy. Tim was happy too because he was useful and helped Sue. They both smiled and played together all day.

Lucy felt shy, but her mom said, "Go introduce yourself to the boy." Lucy walked up to the boy and said, "Hi, I am Lucy. What is your name?" The boy smiled and said, "Hi Lucy, I am Tom."
Lucy and Tom played together all day. They had so much fun, they forgot about the gloomy day. When it was time to go home, Lucy and Tom said, "Goodbye, see you soon!"

Once upon a time there were two best friends, Max and Jess. Jess had an orange cat named Orange. They were always playing together and getting up to all sorts of adventures.
One day they were outside playing when they heard a sound. Suddenly an old bell began to ring. Max and Jess were very curious and wanted to find out where the ringing was coming from.
They searched and searched until they saw an old lady. She was shaking a bell that was ringing.
“What is that noise?” asked Max.
The old lady smiled and told them, “It’s my bell. I am ringing it to let people know they can come and help me. I have a mild cold and need someone to get me medicine from the shop.”
Max and Jess decided to help the old lady. So, Orange the cat ran off and got the medicine for the old lady. The old lady thanked them for being so kind and helping her out in her time of need.
The moral of the story is that it’s important to be kind and help people when you can. A little act of kindness, like helping an old lady, can make someone’s day much brighter!

Once upon a time, there was a furry cat named Tom. Tom lived near the big blue sea. He loved to watch the boats with their big sails go by. One day, Tom saw a little girl named Sue. Sue was sad because she lost her pretty red ball.
Tom wanted to help Sue. He promised her, "I will find your red ball." Tom looked everywhere. He looked under the big tree, behind the little house, and even in the tall grass. At last, Tom found the red ball near the water.
Tom brought the red ball back to Sue. She was so happy! She hugged the furry cat and said, "Thank you, Tom!" They sat by the sea and watched the boats with their big sails go by. Tom and Sue became best friends, and they played together every day.

One day, a little girl named Sue went to the mall with her mom. Sue loved the mall because there were many fun things to see and do. They walked around and saw a store with adorable stuffed animals. Sue wanted one, so her mom bought her a cute stuffed bunny.
While walking, Sue saw a man making sharp things. She asked her mom, "What is he doing?" Her mom said, "He is making sharp toys, but they are not for little kids like you. They can hurt you."
Sue hugged her bunny tight and they kept walking. Suddenly, her bunny started to talk! "Hi, Sue!" it said. Sue was so surprised! She didn't know that her bunny could talk. The bunny told Sue that it had magic powers and could make sharp things safe for kids.
Sue asked the bunny to make the sharp toys safe for her. The bunny waved its paws, and all the sharp toys turned into soft, squishy toys! Sue and her mom laughed and played with the squishy toys. They had a fun day at the mall, and Sue couldn't wait to go back with her magic bunny friend.

That night, Tom ate all his beef. His dad helped him repair the toy car. They made it look like new. Tom and his friends played with the toy car in their hidden place. They had lots of fun and lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time, there was a little bug. The bug liked to crawl all day. One day, the bug saw a big boot. The bug wanted to crawl on the boot.
The bug crawled up the boot, but it was very hard. The bug felt bad. The bug tried and tried, but could not crawl up the boot. The bug was very sad.
Then, a nice bird saw the bug. The bird helped the bug crawl up the boot. The bug was happy. The bird and the bug became good friends. They played together every day.

One day, a little red car was working at the tire factory. He was a hard worker, and he had been working all day.
Suddenly, the little red car saw his friend the tire. He stopped what he was doing and said, “Hello tire! What are you doing?”
The tire said, “I’m here to get printed. The factory is going to turn me into an automobile tire!”
The little red car was very excited, but he quickly became sad. He knew that after the tire was printed, he could never see his friend again. He said, “This is so sad. I’ll never see you again.”
The tire replied, “It’s all right, it’s just part of the job. Now I’ve got to go get printed. Goodbye, little red car.”
The little red car watched sadly as his friend went off to get printed. He had a bitter feeling inside. He knew he would never see the tire again.
The little red car never saw the tire again. He was sad and bitter, and he wouldn’t forget that day.

Tim was scared of the dark. He thought there were monsters under his bed and in his closet. He always wanted his mom to stay with him until he fell asleep.
One night, he heard a strange noise from his closet. He was too afraid to look, but he wanted to know what it was. He grabbed his teddy bear and whispered, "Do you hear that, teddy? Is it a monster?"
He decided to peek at his closet. He lifted his blanket and moved his head very slow. He saw a pair of big eyes and a furry nose. He gasped and hid under his blanket again.
"Who are you?" he heard a voice from his closet. It sounded friendly, not scary.
Tim was curious. He peeked again and saw a small creature with horns, wings, and a tail. It looked like a dragon, but smaller and cuter.
"I'm Tim. Who are you?" he asked.
"I'm Zee. I'm a closet monster. But I'm not a bad monster. I'm a good monster. I like to play and make friends. Can I play with you?" Zee said.
Tim thought for a moment. He liked to play and make friends, too. He was not so scared anymore. He smiled and said, "Okay. You can play with me. But you have to be quiet. My mom is sleeping."
Zee nodded and flew out of his closet. He landed on Tim's bed and hugged his teddy bear. Tim and Zee played and talked until they fell asleep. They became best friends. And Tim was never scared of the dark again.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lily. She had a purple top that she loved very much. The top was soft and pretty. Lily wore it all the time.
One day, Lily's mom told her that they needed to sell some old clothes. Lily did not want to sell her purple top. But her mom said, "We need to make room for new clothes."
Lily was sad, but she knew her mom was right. They went to a place where people sell things. Lots of people came to look at the clothes. A little boy saw the purple top and his eyes got big. He liked it a lot.
Lily saw that the little boy was happy. She felt better about selling her purple top. She knew that the top would make the little boy happy, just like it made her happy. And now, there would be room for new clothes in her closet.

One day, a little cat named Tom was very tired. He wanted to take a nap. Tom looked for a smooth place to rest. He saw a big soft pillow on the floor. Tom thought, "This is the perfect place for a nap!"
Tom jumped on the pillow and closed his eyes. He was just about to start his nap when he heard a voice. It was his friend, a little dog named Max. Max said, "Hi Tom! Do you want to play with me?"
Tom opened his eyes and looked at Max. He was still very tired. Tom said, "Not now, Max. I want to take a nap. Can we play later?" Max nodded his head and said, "Sure, Tom. Have a nice nap!"
Tom closed his eyes again and started his nap. The pillow was very smooth and soft. Tom had a good nap and woke up feeling happy. Later, Tom and Max played together and had lots of fun.

Sara and Ben are playing on the beach. They like to build sand castles and dig holes. They also like to watch the big water that moves up and down. Sometimes the water comes close to them and makes their feet wet. They laugh and run away from the water.
"Look, Ben, look!" Sara says. She points to the sky. "There is a big bird in the air. It has white and black feathers and a long beak. It is flying over the water."
Ben looks up and sees the bird. He thinks it is beautiful. He wants to say hello to the bird. He raises his hand and waves to the bird. "Hi, bird, hi!" he shouts.
The bird sees Ben and Sara. It is curious about them. It flies closer to them and makes a loud sound. "Caw, caw!" it says.
Sara and Ben are happy. They think the bird is talking to them. They wave back to the bird and say "Caw, caw!" too.
The bird likes Sara and Ben. It wants to give them a gift. It flies over the water and catches a shiny fish in its beak. It flies back to Sara and Ben and drops the fish on the sand. The fish is still alive and wiggles on the sand.
Sara and Ben are surprised. They see the fish and its shiny scales. They see the image of the sun on the fish. They think the fish is pretty. They say "Thank you, bird, thank you!" to the bird.
The bird nods its head and makes a gentle sound. "Caw, caw!" it says. It flies away and disappears in the sky.
Sara and Ben look at the fish. They feel sorry for the fish. They want the fish to be happy. They pick up the fish and carry it to the water. They put the fish in the water and watch it swim away.
"Bye, fish, bye!" they say. They smile and hug each other. They are happy. They have made a new friend.

Once upon a time, there was a friendly monkey named Bobo. Bobo loved to eat bananas. He lived in a big tree with many other monkeys. They all played together and had fun.
One day, Bobo found a big, yellow banana. He was very happy. He wanted to share it with his friends. So, he called all his friends to come and see the banana. They all came and looked at the banana. They all wanted to eat it.
Bobo wanted to kiss the banana before they ate it. He thought it would bring them good luck. But when he kissed the banana, something bad happened. The banana fell from his hand and rolled down the hill. All the monkeys ran after it, but they could not catch the banana. It rolled into the river and floated away.
The monkeys were sad. They did not get to eat the big, yellow banana. Bobo was very sorry. He promised to find another banana for his friends. But they never found a banana as big and yellow as the one that rolled away.

Arthur and Sammy were out in the garden.
Arthur said, "Let's play a game!"
Sammy replied, "What kind of game?"
Arthur said, "Let's get some squash and see what happens!"
The boys ran to the squash patch and picked four plump, yellow squash. Then, they carefully placed the squash in the empty sandpit.
Arthur said, "Let's see what happens when we jump into the sandpit!"
Sammy was excited and nodded. He jumped in first and Arthur followed.
The squash bounced around, making lots of noise.
Then they heard a quiet voice say, "Be careful, Boys!"
They looked around and saw that their grandma had become a part of their game!
Grandma smiled at the boys, and said, "This game was very fun. Now it's time to take a break and have some lunch."
The boys both cheered, "Yay!"
Arthur and Sammy enjoyed a quiet and fun afternoon playing their new game.

Tim's friend, Sam, saw what happened and wanted to help. Sam had a toy car too. He said, "Let's fix your car together and make it better." They worked hard to fix Tim's car, but it was not easy. The conflict was big, and they had to try many times.
At last, they fixed the car and made it even better than before. Tim was happy and his heart pain was gone. He thanked Sam for helping him. They raced their toy cars together and had lots of fun. Tim learned that adding rocks was not a good idea, but having a good friend was the best thing to have.

One day, a cheerful boy named Tim went to play outside. He saw a big box and said, "I can imagine this box is a boat!" He got inside the box and pretended to sail on the sea.
Tim met a girl named Sue. She saw the box and said, "Can I play too?" Tim said, "Yes! Let's imagine we are on a big adventure!" They both got in the box and sailed away.
As they played, the box started to move! It was not just their imagination. The box was really floating on water! They held hands and said, "We can do this!" They worked together and found their way back home. Tim and Sue felt success and were very happy.

Sara loved to go to the zoo with her mom. She liked to see all the different animals and learn their names. Her favorite animal was the rhinoceros. She thought it was big and strong and had a funny horn on its nose.
One day, Sara and her mom went to the zoo and saw a sign that said "Rhinoceros Feeding Time". Sara was very excited and wanted to see the rhinoceros eat. She asked her mom if they could go closer to the fence. Her mom said yes, but told her to be careful and not touch the fence.
Sara and her mom walked to the fence and saw a big rhinoceros eating some hay. Sara was amazed by how big its mouth was and how loud it chewed. She wanted to say hello to the rhinoceros and make friends with it. She leaned over the fence and waved her hand. She said "Hi, rhinoceros! You are very nice. Can I pet you?"
The rhinoceros heard Sara's voice and looked up. It saw Sara's hand and thought it was food. It opened its mouth and moved its head towards Sara. Sara did not know what the rhinoceros wanted. She thought it wanted to play with her. She smiled and moved her hand closer.
Her mom saw what was happening and got very worried. She knew the rhinoceros could hurt Sara with its teeth or horn. She shouted "Sara, no! Don't touch the rhinoceros! It might bite you!" She grabbed Sara's arm and pulled her back from the fence.
Sara was surprised and scared. She did not understand why her mom was angry and why the rhinoceros was not friendly. She started to cry and said "Mom, why did you do that? I just wanted to pet the rhinoceros. It looked so nice."
Her mom hugged Sara and said "I'm sorry, Sara. I know you like the rhinoceros, but it is not a pet. It is a wild animal. It does not know you and it might think you are food. You have to be careful around wild animals. They can be dangerous. I love you and I don't want you to get hurt."
Sara calmed down and said "I love you too, mom. I'm sorry I made you worried. I didn't know the rhinoceros was wild. Can we still watch it eat?"
Her mom said "Yes, we can. But we have to stay away from the fence and not touch it. OK?"
Sara said "OK, mom. Thank you for letting me see the rhinoceros. It is still my favorite animal. But I will not try to pet it again."
Her mom said "You're welcome, Sara. I'm glad you like the rhinoceros. But remember, some animals are better to look at than to touch."

Then, a kind ladybug saw the sad little fly. She went to comfort him. She said, "You may not be the fastest, but you are still a good friend. We all like you." The little fly felt better, and they all played together happily.

Once upon a time, there was a big dog named Spot and a small cat named Fluffy. They lived in a nice house with their friend, a little girl named Lily. Lily loved to play with Spot and Fluffy every day. They had lots of fun together.
One day, Spot and Fluffy had a little fight. They both wanted a treat that Lily had. They didn't know how to share it. Lily wanted to be fair, so she thought of a good idea. She broke the treat into two pieces. Now, Spot and Fluffy could each have a part of the treat.
Spot and Fluffy were happy with Lily's idea. They took their pieces of the treat and went to their favorite spots in the house. Spot and Fluffy settled down and enjoyed their special treat. They were glad that Lily was fair and helped them share. And from that day on, they always shared their treats and played happily together.

One day, a little baby was in his crib. The crib was near a big window. The baby liked to look out the window and see the birds and trees. But today, the wind was harsh. It made the baby feel cold and sad.
The baby's big sister saw him in the crib. She wanted to help. She thought, "I need to stop the harsh wind from making the baby cold." So, she closed the window. The wind could not come in anymore.
The baby felt warm and happy again. He smiled at his big sister. She felt good because she helped her baby brother. They played together near the crib, safe from the harsh wind outside.

Once upon a time there was a little girl named Jill. She was three years old and absolutely loved wearing dresses.
One day, Jill's mom told her a secret. She said, "I have a surprise for you. I want you to weigh the dress and see if you can guess what it is!" Jill was so excited she couldn't wait to weigh the dress and find out what the surprise was.
The next day, Jill went outside to the yard and saw a big, clear box. She rushed over and before she could even say anything, her mom said, "Open the box and weigh it!" Jill carefully opened the box and found a beautiful sparkly pink dress inside.
She was so excited she couldn't contain herself! She quickly weighed the dress, knowing that it was the surprise her mom had told her about. She was right!
The dress was the perfect size for her. Since that day, Jill always wears her sparkly pink dress. She loves how it feels when she weights it and how clear it is when she wears it.

Lily and Tom like to play in the park. They run and jump and laugh. They see a big stone near a tree. Lily says, "Let's climb on the stone and pretend we are lions." Tom says, "OK, that sounds fun."
They climb on the stone and roar like lions. They make faces and wave their arms. They have a lot of fun. But then they hear their mom calling them. She says, "Lily, Tom, it's time to go home. Come here, please." Lily and Tom do not want to go home. They want to stay on the stone and play.
They say, "No, mom, we want to stay. We are lions. We are fast and strong. We do not want to leave our stone." Mom says, "I know you are having fun, but we have to go home. It's getting dark and cold. You can play on the stone again tomorrow. Come on, let's go."
Lily and Tom look at each other. They do not want to make mom angry. They say, "OK, mom, we will come. But can we say goodbye to our stone?" Mom says, "Yes, you can say goodbye to your stone. But hurry up, please."
Lily and Tom climb down from the stone. They hug the stone and say, "Bye, stone. Thank you for letting us play on you. You are a good stone. We will see you tomorrow." Once upon a time, in a far away planet, there was a girl named Lina. She was a very happy girl who loved to explore and learn new things.
One day, Lina noticed a soft cloth lying on the ground. She picked it up, wondering what it could be. She wiped it on her face and immediately felt refreshed. She curiously wiped it on other things - rocks, trees and even the sky!
Lina was having lots of fun on this strange planet, but then something unexpected happened. She wiped the cloth one last time, and suddenly all around her glittered with starlight! The sky filled with sparkles, and Lina gasped in amazement. She quickly realized that she had magically turned the entire planet into a sparkly place!
Lina was overjoyed, and from that day on, she kept her magical cloth with her all the time - just in case she ever needed to wipe a little magic on the world again!

"Ow, ow, ow!" Ben says. The bird bites his finger.
"Be gentle, Ben," Sara says. "The bird is scared. Talk to it softly."
Sara kneels next to the bird. She sings a song. The bird stops pecking. It listens to Sara.
"Hello, bird, we are Sara and Ben," Sara says. "We are your friends. We want to make your wing better. Can we bathe it for you?"
The bird nods. It lets Sara and Ben lift it gently. They dip the cloth in the water. They clean the bird's wing. The bird feels better. It thanks Sara and Ben.
"Thank you, thank you," the bird says.
"You're welcome, bird," Sara and Ben say. "Can we keep you as a pet?"
The bird shakes its head. It wants to fly again. It asks Sara and Ben to take it to the vet.
"OK, bird, we will take you to the vet," Sara and Ben say. "But can we visit you sometimes?"
The bird smiles. It agrees. Sara and Ben hug the bird. They take it to the vet. The vet fixes the bird's wing. The bird flies away. It waves to Sara and Ben.
"Bye, bye, bird," Sara and Ben say.
"Bye, bye, Sara and Ben," the bird says. "You are very kind. I will always remember you."

When Anna hopped on the drawer, the drawer opened and Mommy's things fell out. There were clothes, and books, and jewelry, and papers. They made a big mess on the floor.
Mommy came into the room and saw the mess. She was very angry. She said to Anna, "Anna, what did you do? You made a big mess with my things. You were very rude. You should not hop on the drawer. You should not hop on anything that is not yours. You should say sorry and help me clean up."
Anna felt bad. She did not mean to make a mess. She did not mean to be rude. She liked to hop, but she did not like to make Mommy angry. She said to Mommy, "I'm sorry, Mommy. I was hopping and I did not see the drawer. I will help you clean up. I will not hop on the drawer again. I will only hop on things that are mine."
Mommy hugged Anna and said, "Thank you, Anna. I love you. I know you like to hop, but you have to be careful and respectful. You can hop on your toys, or your bed, or your mat. But you cannot hop on things that are not yours. Do you understand?"
Anna nodded and said, "I understand, Mommy. I love you too. Can I hop with you after we clean up?"
Mommy smiled and said, "Yes, you can. We can hop together and have fun. But only on things that are ours."

One sunny day, Tim went to the park with his mom. He wore his big red hat and brought his toy sword. He played with his sword, pretending to be a brave knight. Tim saw a big tree and wanted to climb it. His mom told him not to, but Tim was stubborn and tried to climb it anyway.
As Tim was climbing, something unexpected happened. A kind squirrel came down from the tree and gave Tim a real, shiny sword! Tim was so surprised! He climbed down from the tree and showed his mom the new sword. His mom smiled and said, "Sometimes being stubborn can lead to good things." From that day on, Tim wore his big red hat and played with his new sword, always remembering the kind squirrel and the magic tree.

Once upon a time, in a warm summer, there was a little girl named Mia. Mia was upset because she had no friends to play with. She would walk in the park all alone, feeling sad.
One day, while Mia was walking, she saw a small box. She opened the box and got a big surprise! Inside the box was a tiny kitten. The kitten was very cute and fluffy. Mia was so happy to find the kitten and she decided to take it home.
At home, Mia and the kitten played all day. They became best friends. Mia was not upset anymore because she had a new friend. They had a lot of fun together during the summer. And they lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time, there was a bald man named Tom. Tom had a small house. He lived there all alone. Tom had a rag that he liked very much. He used the rag to clean his house every day.
One day, Tom went outside to play. He forgot to close the door. The wind blew the rag out of the house. Tom was sad. He looked for the rag, but he could not find it.
Tom walked down the road. He saw a big tree. The rag was in the tree! Tom was happy. He took the rag and went back home. This time, Tom remembered to close the door.

One day, a little boy named Tim saw a big, hairy dog. The dog was so big and hairy that it was hard to see his eyes. Tim liked the dog and wanted to play with him. They played ball and ran around in the park. They had so much fun together.
Then, something unexpected happened. The big, hairy dog just disappeared! Tim was sad and didn't know what to do. He looked and looked for the dog, but he could not find him. Tim thought the dog was gone forever.
But then, Tim saw a view of something in the bushes. It was the big, hairy dog! The dog had not disappeared after all. He was just hiding to surprise Tim. They both laughed and played some more. Tim was so happy that his new friend was still there.

One day, a cool cat named Sam went for a walk. He saw a rail near the park. Sam thought, "This rail will be fun to walk on." He started to walk on the rail, but he felt it move. Sam didn't know that the rail was part of a train track.
Sam saw a big train coming. He heard the train say, "Choo choo!" The train was going very fast. Sam was scared, but he had an idea. He knew he had to do something to stop the train. He thought, "I will include my friends to help me."
Sam called his friends, a dog named Max and a bird named Lucy. They all worked together to make a big stop sign. The train saw the stop sign and stopped just in time. The train said, "Thank you for stopping me. You saved the day!"
Sam, Max, and Lucy were happy they could help. They learned that it is good to include friends when you need help. They also learned to be careful near train tracks. From then on, they played in the park and stayed away from the rail.

Once upon a time, there was a big, hairy dog named Spot. Spot loved to run and play. One day, he saw a ship near the water. The ship was big and had many colors.
Spot wanted to see the ship up close. So, he ran to the ship and jumped on it. The ship started to move. Spot was happy and wagged his tail. He saw fish, birds, and other animals as the ship sailed.
After a while, the ship stopped. Spot decided it was time to go home. He jumped off the ship and swam back to the land. When he got home, he was tired. Spot ate his food, drank some water, and settled down for a nap. He dreamed of the big, colorful ship and all the fun he had that day.

Once upon a time, in a big green forest, there was a wise old owl. The owl loved to sit in a circle of trees and chew on leaves. One sunny day, the owl was chewing on a leaf when he saw a small mouse.
The mouse was running and playing near the circle of trees. The wise old owl watched the mouse and thought, "I can catch the mouse and eat it." So, the owl flew down to the ground and tried to catch the mouse.
But the mouse was too fast! The owl could not catch the mouse. The mouse stopped running and looked at the owl. "Why are you trying to catch me?" the mouse asked. The wise old owl thought for a moment, and then said, "I wanted to eat you, but you are too fast. Let's be friends instead." And so, the owl and the mouse became the best of friends, and they played together in the circle of trees every day.

After they finished playing with the clay, they showed their paw prints to their friends. All the animals thought it was a good idea and wanted to try it too. The clay with all the paw prints became a special memory for all the friends. They all played happily ever after.

Along came a little bunny who said, "No, mind the bush! Don't pick all the berries. Save some for me too!" The bear listened to the bunny and decided to leave some berries on the bush.
The bear smiled gently and said, "Okay, I will mind the bush and leave some food for you too." He carefully took a few of the brightest berries and thanked the bunny for minding the food.
The bunny smiled back at the bear and said, "Thank you for minding the food! We can both enjoy it." The bear was so happy that he and the bunny were sharing the food and being gentle with one another.

One day, there was a little girl who was so excited to play with her new toy. She ran outside with it and saw a big barrel. She jumped with joy and started pushing the barrel up a hill. The girl was getting tired but she was determined to get the barrel to the top.
At the top of the hill, she realized the barrel was too heavy for her to move. She was sad and frustrated but she was determined to do it. With all her strength, she pushed the barrel until it reached the top.
Once the barrel was at the top, she realized how much she hated it. She had to put so much effort into pushing it and it made her so tired that it wasn't worth it. But then, suddenly, the girl noticed something amazing. The barrel had made a ramp and was the perfect toy for her to play with.
The girl was so excited! She quickly grabbed the barrel and started playing with it. She had so much fun and was so happy she had pushed the big, heavy barrel.

Lily and Ben are playing with their toys in the garden. Lily has a doll and Ben has a car. They are having fun, but they are also very messy. They leave their toys everywhere on the grass.
"Look, Lily, my car can go very fast!" Ben says, making zooming noises.
"That's nice, Ben, but my doll can talk!" Lily says, pressing a button on the doll's back.
Suddenly, they hear a loud bark. It is their dog, Rex, who has come to join them. Rex is a big, brown dog with a long, fluffy tail. He likes to play with Lily and Ben, but he also likes to chase their toys.
"Rex, no!" Lily shouts, as Rex runs towards her doll. "That's my doll, not your toy!"
But Rex does not listen. He grabs the doll with his mouth and shakes it. The doll makes a funny sound and its hair falls off.
"Hey, stop that!" Ben yells, as Rex drops the doll and goes for his car. "That's my car, not your chew!"
But Rex does not care. He bites the car and breaks one of its wheels. The car makes a sad noise and stops moving.
Lily and Ben are very upset. They run to their toys and try to fix them. But they are too broken. They look at Rex, who is wagging his tail and panting. He thinks he has done a good job.
"Bad dog, Rex!" Lily and Ben say. "You ruined our toys! You are not useful at all!"
Rex does not understand. He thinks they are playing with him. He licks their faces and wags his tail more.
"Go away, Rex!" Lily and Ben say. "We don't want to play with you!"
Rex feels sad. He lowers his ears and walks away. He finds a stick and tries to play with it. But it is not fun. He misses Lily and Ben.
He looks at them from a distance. He sees that they are still sad. He wants to make them happy. He thinks of a way to do that.
He picks up the stick with his mouth and runs to them. He drops the stick in front of them and barks.
"Look, Lily, Ben, I found a stick!" he seems to say. "You can play with it! It is not broken! It is useful!"
Lily and Ben look at the stick. They look at Rex. They see that he is trying to be nice. They feel sorry for him.
"Thank you, Rex," Lily says, picking up the stick. "That's very kind of you."
"Yes, thank you, Rex," Ben says, smiling. "You are useful after all."
They pat Rex on the head and scratch his ears. They throw the stick and Rex fetches it. They play with him and the stick. They forget about their broken toys. They are happy again.
Rex is happy too. He wags his tail and barks. He loves Lily and Ben. He reminds them that he is their friend.

Tim got a jazz CD and went home. He put the CD in the player and started to dance. He was so happy! The jazz music made him feel excited. From that day on, Tim loved to dance to jazz music.

Once upon a time, in a small village, there was a dancer. This dancer was very good at dancing. Everyone in the village liked to watch the dancer dance. The dancer walked and walked, searching for the fierce animal. They looked behind trees and under rocks. The dancer was very brave. Suddenly, they found the fierce animal. But it was not what they expected. The fierce animal was a tiny, scared mouse.
The dancer laughed and decided to help the mouse. They took the mouse back to the village, and everyone was happy. The dancer and the mouse became good friends. The villagers learned that sometimes, things are not as scary as they seem. The moral of the story is to not judge something by what you hear, but to see it for yourself.

She went home to get her tray. Sue's mom said, "Be careful with your tray, Sue. It is your favorite one." Sue nodded and took her tray to the park.
At the park, Sue put her tray on the hole. She played with her toys on it. Then, she heard a voice say, "Thank you for the tray!" Sue looked around and saw a little mouse. The mouse said, "I live in the hole. The tray is now my roof. It keeps me dry when it rains." Sue smiled and said, "You're welcome, little mouse. I am happy to help you."

Kali twirled the whip as she took her adventure around the park. All of a sudden, she bumped into another little boy who was trying to catch a bird. The boy had been chasing it with a net and was very upset that Kali was now twirling his whip. He yelled, telling her to give it back!
Kali didn't want to give it back so she ran away, twirling the whip over her head as she went. But the boy was too quick - he caught Kali and took the whip back.
Kali was very upset. She had been so enthusiastic about taking the whip, but now it had been taken away from her. Still, she was determined not to give up. The next day, she went to the park and found another whip. This time she was more careful and made sure no one saw her take it.

Once upon a time, there was a small bird who liked to sing. Everywhere the bird went, it sang with joy. One day the bird came across a crack on the ground. It was a deep crack, and the bird knew it should stay away. But the bird was so excited to sing that it sang louder and closer to the crack.
Suddenly, the ground around the crack started to move. Out of the crack came a speckled snake! The snake was angry with the bird for singing too close to its home. The bird was so scared that it flew away as fast as it could.
The bird learned an important lesson that day - to always be careful around things that look dangerous. The bird was also reminded that sometimes danger can be hidden, and that even something as soft and beautiful as a song can be too loud or too close.

One day, Tom's mom said he had to take a test. Tom was scared because he didn't want to suffer if he didn't do well. He tried to study, but his room was so messy that he couldn't find his books.
Then, something unexpected happened. Tom's little sister, Lily, came to help him. She cleaned his room and found the books. Tom was so happy! They studied together and Tom did well on the test. From that day on, Tom learned to keep his room clean and he never suffered from a messy room again.

Lily and Tom are twins. They like to play in the kitchen. They find a fork and a lock. They want to see what they can do with them.
"Look, Lily, I can put the fork in the lock," Tom says. He tries to turn the fork, but it does not move. "It is stuck. Help me, Lily."
Lily tries to pull the fork out of the lock, but it does not come out. "It is too hard, Tom. We need to ask Mom."
They run to find Mom. She is in the living room. She is reading a book. She sees the fork and the lock in Tom's hand.
"What are you doing with that, Tom?" Mom asks. She is not happy. "That is not a toy. That is a lock for the door. And that is a fork for eating. You could hurt yourself or break something."
"Sorry, Mom," Tom says. "We were just playing. We wanted to see if the fork can open the lock. But it did not work. It is stuck. Can you help us?"
Mom sighs. She takes the fork and the lock from Tom. She uses a key to open the lock. She frees the fork. She gives them back to Tom.
"Here you go, Tom. But please, do not play with these things again. They are not nice. They are dangerous. If you want to play, you can use your toys. They are nice. They are fun. They are safe. Do you understand?"
"Yes, Mom," Tom says. He feels sorry. He hugs Mom. "Thank you, Mom. You are nice. You are the best."
Lily hugs Mom too. "Yes, thank you, Mom. We love you."
Mom smiles. She hugs them back. "I love you too, my sweet twins. Now, go and play nicely. And be careful."

One day, Jack noticed the gate of the yard. It was locked! He couldn't understand why it won't open.
He asked his mother, “Mommy, why is the yard locked?” His mother replied, “Well, that's because it keeps you safe from the world outside, sweetheart. I lock it to keep you safe.”
Jack nodded his head and said, “Ok! I'll be careful not to open it!” His mother smiled and said, “That's my brave boy.”

Once upon a time, there was a big, ancient oak tree in the woods. The tree was very old and wise. One day, a little squirrel named Sam wanted to climb the tree to find nuts to eat.
Sam tried to climb the ancient oak, but it was very hard. He said to the tree, "Please help me, big tree! I want to succeed in climbing you to find nuts." The tree did not say anything, but the wind blew, and a branch moved down to help Sam.
Sam started to climb, but a mean bird named Billy saw him. Billy did not want Sam to succeed. He wanted the nuts for himself. So, Billy flew down and pushed Sam off the branch. Sam fell down and got hurt.
Sam did not succeed in climbing the ancient oak tree. He was sad and hungry. Billy, the mean bird, got all the nuts for himself, and Sam had to find food somewhere else. And so, the story had a bad ending for little Sam the squirrel.

Lola smiled and said, “It sure is!”

Once upon a time, there was a big orange cat. The cat liked to play with his friends. One day, the cat saw a blackboard. He thought it would be fun to carry it to his friends.
