Luke was very weak. His mom said he needed a special ranch to make him strong again. He worried that ranch might not taste good.
On the way to get the ranch, Luke and his mom stopped off at the store. His mom said they needed something else. She grabbed some wipes and put them in her basket. Luke didn't understand why they needed those.
When they got home, his mom helped him sit down at the dinner table. She then opened the wipes and put the ranch on top of them. It smelt a bit funny to Luke. He asked if he could try it, but his mom said he had to wait a bit.
Instead, she wiped her finger over the ranch and put it on Luke's tongue. "Mmmmmm," he said with a big smile. He liked the ranch!
Luke's mom wiped her eyes, happy to see he was feeling better. "That ranch was just what you needed," she said. Luke smiled and finished his ranch, feeling strong and healthy again.

One day, a little girl named Lucy was in the kitchen. She wanted to spread jam on her bread. But she could not find her spoon. She looked and looked, but the spoon was not there. "Oh no!" she said. "Where is my spoon?"
Lucy asked her brother Tom, "Have you seen my spoon?" Tom said, "Yes, I used it to dig in the dirt outside." Lucy was not happy. She knew that her spoon was now dirty. She went outside to get her spoon back.
When she found the spoon, it was very dirty. Lucy was sad. She took the spoon inside and washed it. The water was mild and warm. She cleaned the spoon with soap. Then she spread the jam on her bread.
Lucy was happy now. She had her spoon back, and she could eat her bread with jam. She learned that sometimes we have to fix things when they go wrong. And she knew that she would always keep her spoon clean from now on.

After some days, the stone cracked open. But it was not a chick inside. It was a baby dinosaur! The dinosaur was very small and cute. The hen was happy. She took care of the dinosaur like it was her baby.
Now, the poor hen was not sad anymore. She had a new friend to love and take care of. The hen and the dinosaur lived happily in the big red barn. And they played together every day.

"Oops," Anna says.
"Uh-oh," Ben says.

"Anna! Ben! What have you done?" Mom yells.
"You are in big trouble!" Dad yells.
Anna and Ben are scared. They run to their room and hide under the bed. They cry and hug each other. They wish they never found the box. They wish they never tried to build a crib. They wish they played with blocks instead.

Once upon a time, there was a little fish named Fin. Fin lived in a big ocean. He had many friends. They liked to swim and play together. One day, Fin and his friends saw a big, dangerous shark. They were scared.

Fin had an idea. He found a big leaf and cut it into a long rope. He gave one end of the rope to the shark. Fin said, "You can play with us, but you must hold this rope. "Wow, mom, look at that snake! It is so long and strong!" Anna said.
"Yes, it is, Anna. It is a python. It can squeeze its prey with its body," mom said.
"Does it eat people?" Anna asked, feeling a little scared.
"No, Anna, it doesn't. It eats small animals, like rats and birds. And it is behind the glass, so it can't hurt us. See, it is very calm. It is just resting," mom said.

"Mom, can I display my tongue like the snake?" Anna asked.
"Sure, Anna, you can. But only here, not at home or at school, okay?" mom said.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Mia. Mia had a small, tidy room with lots of toys. She loved to play all day long. One day, Mia found a big box in her room. It was a present from her mom and dad.
Inside the present, Mia found a toy doctor set. She was so happy! Mia decided to play with her new toy and heal her teddy bear. Teddy had a small rip, so Mia put a band-aid on it. Now, Teddy felt much better.
Mia played doctor with all her toys, making sure they were all healthy and happy. When she was done, she put her doctor set back in the box and put her toys back in their places. Mia's room was tidy again, and she was proud of herself for helping her toys.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim loved to play with his toy gun. He would run around and shoot at things, but he never hurt anyone. One day, Tim found a big book with the word "history" on it. He didn't know what it meant, but he liked how the book looked.
Tim took the book to his room and started to look at the pictures inside. He saw people with real guns, and they were shooting too. But sometimes, when they shoot, people got hurt. Tim didn't like that. He didn't want anyone to get hurt when he played with his toy gun.
So, Tim decided to change the way he played. He started to shoot at things that couldn't get hurt, like trees and rocks. One day, while playing, Tim shoot at a big rock. Suddenly, the rock moved! It was not a rock, but a big, friendly turtle! The turtle smiled at Tim and said, "Thank you for not shooting at things that can get hurt. Now, we can be friends." Tim was so happy! From that day on, Tim and the turtle played together, and they never hurt anyone.

Once upon a time, a little girl named Lily loved to paint. She had a big box of paint and many brushes. Lily also had a mitten that she wore when it was cold outside.
One day, Lily was painting a picture of her mitten. She painted and painted until she was tired. She went to bed, leaving her painting to dry. That night, something unexpected happened. The mitten in the painting came to life!
The mitten felt helpless because it was all alone. It jumped out of the painting and went outside to look for a friend. It found a kitten who was cold and shivering. The mitten jumped onto the kitten's paw to keep it warm. The kitten was so happy that it started to purr. When Lily woke up, she saw the mitten and kitten playing together. From that day on, Lily, the mitten, and the kitten were best friends.

Once upon a time, there was a little dog named Buddy. Buddy loved to play in the park. One day, he saw a big cat. The cat was mean, and they started to fight. Buddy was sad.
The next day, Buddy went to the park again. He saw a big water spray. Buddy had an idea. He thought the water spray could help him. He practiced with the water spray and got better at using it.
Buddy saw the mean cat again. This time, he used the water spray. The cat got wet and ran away. Buddy won the fight! After, Buddy found a delicious treat on the ground. He ate it and felt happy. Buddy knew he could be brave and smart.

Once upon a time, a lazy cat named Tom lived near a little stream. He loved to sleep all day and play all night. One day, a new cat moved in close to the stream. Her name was Sally, and she was very active and liked to play all the time.
Tom and Sally became friends. They played together near the stream every day. But one day, while playing, Tom fell into the stream. He felt embarrass because he did not know how to swim. Sally saw Tom in the water and quickly jumped in to help him.
Sally helped Tom out of the stream, and Tom thanked her. From that day on, Tom decided not to be lazy anymore. He learned to swim and played with Sally near the stream every day without fear.

Once upon a time, there was a small girl called Lucy. She was only 3 years old, but she was very brave!
One day, she was walking outside when she saw a fancy cart parked nearby. She was so excited. She walked up to it and gave it a big hug.
Suddenly, Lucy felt a voice beside her. She turned and saw an old woman.
"What a fancy cart you have!" said Lucy with a smile.
The old woman smiled back. "Yes, I've had it for many years. It's served me very well."
Lucy felt so happy. She wanted to explore the cart and discover all its secrets. She asked the old woman if she could take a look inside.
The old woman kindly agreed, so Lucy opened the cart door and peeked inside. It was so much fun! She felt so excited, she couldn't stop smiling.
The old woman noticed her enthusiasm and said, "I think it's time you had your very own fancy cart. What do you say?"
Lucy was so happy, she jumped up and down in excitement.
The old woman chuckled. She had found just the right present for Lucy!

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Rose. Rose loved to be wild and free. One day, she heard a loud noise outside. She put on her mask and went to answer the sound.
When she got outside, she saw two wild animals running together. Rose was amazing and wanted to join in their fun. She ran after them, but the animals were too fast for her. She tried to catch them, but failed.
Rose was sad that she couldn't play with the wild animals. But then, she thought of a plan. She called out to the animals and asked if they'd like to come over to her house to play. The animals stopped and looked at her. They nodded their heads.
The next day, Rose's house was filled with wild music and laughter. Rose and the animals enjoyed playing together. They were all happy together, answering their wild urges with joy.

One day, a little boy named Tim went to play in the park. He saw a brilliant light under a big tree. Tim went closer to see what it was. He found a tiny spark that could talk!
"Hi, I am Sparky," said the spark. "I am hiding from the wind. Can you help me find a safe place to hide?" Tim nodded and they looked for a good spot together. They found a small hole in the ground and Sparky went inside.
Just then, a big gust of wind came and blew the leaves all around. But Sparky was safe in the hole. Tim was happy he could help his new friend. Suddenly, the hole started to shake and Sparky grew bigger and bigger. Tim was surprised!
"Thank you for helping me," said Sparky, now a big bright light. "I am a magic spark and I will grant you a wish for being so kind." Tim wished for a new toy and Sparky made it appear. They played together all day and became the best of friends.

One day, Tom got a big box of toy cars. He was very happy and proud. He wanted to sort them by color. He put the red cars in one pile, the blue cars in another pile, and the yellow cars in a third pile. Tom's friend, Sue, came over to play with him.
"Look at my cars!" Tom said to Sue. "I sorted them by color!"
"Wow!" Sue said. "Can I play too?"
"Yes!" Tom said, and they started to play with the cars. They made roads and had races.
Suddenly, a real car drove into their playroom! The car was red, blue, and yellow, just like Tom's toy cars. Tom and Sue were very surprised. The car stopped, and a clown came out. The clown had a big smile and said, "I heard you like cars! I wanted to show you my car!"
Tom and Sue laughed and clapped. They had never seen a real car like this before. They played with the clown and his car all day. It was the best day ever!

Tim and Sam are friends. They like to play with scissors and paper. They cut shapes and make animals and cars. One day, they find a big hat in Tim's room. It is Tim's dad's hat. He wears it when he goes to work.
"Let's try the hat!" Sam says. He puts it on his head. It is too big for him. It covers his eyes and ears. He laughs. "I look funny!"
Tim wants to try the hat too. He takes the scissors and cuts a hole in the hat. He makes a hole for his eyes. He puts the hat on his head. He looks in the mirror. He laughs. "I look funny too!"
But then, Tim's dad comes home. He hears the boys laughing. He goes to Tim's room. He sees the hat on Tim's head. He sees the hole in the hat. He sees the scissors and the paper. He is very angry.
"Tim! Sam! What have you done to my hat?" he shouts. He takes the hat off Tim's head. He sees Tim's hair. He sees a big cut in Tim's hair. He sees Tim's bald spot. He is very sad.
"Tim! You cut your hair! Why did you do that?" he asks.
Tim and Sam are scared. They panic. They do not know what to say. They are sorry. They did not mean to cut the hat or the hair. They just wanted to play.
"Sorry, dad. Sorry, Tim. We were playing. We did not know. We are sorry." they say.
Tim's dad sighs. He is not angry anymore. He is calm. He hugs Tim and Sam. He knows they are good boys. He knows they made a mistake. He knows they are sorry.
"It's okay, boys. I forgive you. But you have to be careful with scissors. They are sharp. They can cut things. They can hurt you. Do you understand?" he says.
Tim and Sam nod. They understand. They are careful. They do not play with scissors anymore. They play with crayons and stickers. They make new shapes and animals and cars. They are happy. Tim's dad is happy too. He buys a new hat. He likes his new hat. He still loves his old hat. He still loves Tim. He does not mind his bald spot. He thinks it is funny. He laughs.

Once upon a time, there was a wealthy lady who had a parrot. The parrot was very special - it could talk and the lady loved it very much.
One day, the lady went to the shop and she left her parrot at home. While the lady was away, the parrot got very hungry. It looked around for something to eat, but it couldn't find anything. So the parrot knew it needed help.
Just then, a happy little bird flew past. The parrot asked the little bird for help and the little bird was happy to do so. The little bird flew away and came back with a big juicy apple.
The parrot was so happy and it enjoyed the apple very much. After that, the parrot never forgot how much it needed help.

Once upon a time, there was a little dog named Spot. Spot loved to play outside on his street. One day, he saw a big sign near his house. The sign had a picture of a dog like him. Spot wanted to know what the sign said, but he could not read.
Spot felt frustrated. He wanted to know what the sign was about. He asked his friend, a bird named Tweet, to help him. Tweet looked at the sign and told Spot that it said, "No dogs allowed on the grass." Spot was sad because he liked to play on the grass.
But then, Tweet had an idea. He told Spot that they could play on the other side of the street where there was no sign. Spot was happy again. They played together all day, and Spot was not frustrated anymore. And they lived happily ever after.

Tom and Lily were playing with their toy trains on the track. They liked to make the trains go fast and make noises. But Tom wanted to have the red train that Lily had. He said, "Give me the red train. It is mine."
Lily said, "No, it is mine. You have the blue train. It is nice too."
Tom got angry and grabbed the red train from Lily. Lily cried and tried to get it back. They pulled and pushed the train until it broke. The track was also broken. They both felt sad and sorry.
Their mom heard the noise and came to see what happened. She saw the broken train and the broken track. She said, "What did you do? You broke your toys. You were not nice to each other. You have to share and be kind."
Tom and Lily looked at their mom and then at each other. They knew they did something bad. They said, "We are sorry, mom. We are sorry, Lily. We are sorry, Tom. We were not nice. We were mean. Can you forgive us?"
Their mom smiled and hugged them. She said, "I forgive you, Tom. I forgive you, Lily. I love you both. But you have to learn to play together and respect each other's toys. And you have to be careful with your toys. They are not dangerous, but they can break if you are rough with them."
Tom and Lily nodded and hugged their mom. They said, "We love you, mom. We will play together and respect each other's toys. And we will be careful with our toys. We will not break them again."
Their mom helped them fix the train and the track. They thanked her and played with their trains again. They took turns with the red train and the blue train. They made the trains go fast and make noises. But they also made the trains say, "Hello, friend. You are nice. I like you." They were happy and had fun.

Once upon a time, in a small house, there lived a boy named Tim. Tim loved to eat tasty food. One day, his mom made a very tasty cake. Tim was very happy and excited. He could not wait to eat the cake. But his mom told him, "Wait, Tim. We will eat the cake after dinner."
During dinner, Tim could not stop thinking about the tasty cake. He wanted to eat it right away. He thought of a plan to remove the cake from the table when his mom was not looking. He thought he was very smart, like he had a big brain.
When his mom went to the kitchen, Tim quickly removed the cake from the table. He ate the whole cake very fast. But then, his mom came back and saw the empty plate. She knew what Tim did. Tim felt bad for not waiting. His mom said, "Next time, wait for the right time to eat the cake, Tim." Tim learned his lesson and promised to wait next time. And they all lived happily ever after.

Sara and Ben were playing with their crayons in the living room. They liked to draw on the big paper that their mom gave them. They drew flowers, cars, animals and stars. They had many colors to choose from, but Sara's favorite was white. She liked to use the white crayon to make snow and clouds.
"Look, Ben, I made a snowman!" Sara said, showing her drawing to her brother.
"That's nice, Sara, but you can't see it very well. The paper is also white," Ben said.
"I don't care, I like white. It's pretty and clean," Sara said.
She looked around the room and saw a white sofa. It was their mom's new sofa, and she said it was very expensive. Sara had an idea. She took her white crayon and went to the sofa. She started to draw on it, making more snow and clouds.
"Hey, Sara, what are you doing?" Ben asked, following her.
"I'm making the sofa more beautiful. See, it has snow and clouds now," Sara said, smiling.
"No, Sara, that's bad! You can't draw on the sofa. Mom will be very angry. She said we can only draw on the paper. The sofa is not for drawing. It's for sitting," Ben said.
He tried to stop Sara, but she didn't listen. She kept drawing on the sofa, making more and more marks.
"Sara, stop it! You're ruining the sofa. It's not white anymore. It's dirty. Mom will be very sad. She worked hard to buy the sofa. It's very expensive. You have to wipe it off," Ben said.
He grabbed a cloth and tried to wipe off the crayon marks, but they didn't come off. They were stuck on the sofa.
"Oh no, what have I done?" Sara said, realizing her mistake.
She started to cry. She was scared of what mom would say. She didn't mean to make the sofa dirty. She just wanted to make it pretty.
Ben hugged her and said, "It's okay, Sara. Don't cry. Maybe mom can fix it. Maybe she can wash it or cover it. But you have to say sorry to her. You have to tell her the truth. And you have to promise not to draw on the sofa again. You have to be careful with other people's things. They are not for playing. They are for using."
Sara nodded and said, "Okay, Ben. I'm sorry. I'll tell mom. I'll promise. I'll be careful."
They waited for mom to come home. They were nervous, but they hoped she would forgive them. They learned a lesson that day. They learned to respect other people's things and not to draw on them. They learned that white is not always pretty and clean. Sometimes it can be dirty and expensive.

One day, a cat named Jazz went for a walk. Jazz saw a weird-looking ball on the ground. It was big and had many colors. Jazz was curious and wanted to play with it.
Jazz used his paw to rub the ball. Suddenly, a voice came from the ball. "Hello, I am the ball. Do you want to play with me?" it asked. Jazz was surprised but happy to have a new friend.
"Yes, I want to play with you!" Jazz replied. They played together all day long. Jazz would rub the ball, and it would bounce high in the sky. They had so much fun, and Jazz was glad he found the weird ball.

Once upon a time, there was a playful dog named Max. Max loved to play with his pebble all day. He would run, jump, and roll with his pebble. Max was a very happy dog.
One sunny day, Max was playing with his pebble in the park. He was running so fast that he did not see the big tree in front of him. Max hit the tree and his pebble went flying. Max looked and looked, but he could not find his pebble. Max was sad because he lost his pebble.
While Max was looking for his pebble, a little girl named Lily found it. Lily saw Max was sad and she knew that the pebble was his. Lily walked up to Max and gave him his pebble back. Max was so happy! Max and Lily played together with the pebble all day. Max learned that sharing can make you even happier than playing alone.

One day, a little boy named Tim saw an enormous balloon. The balloon was so big and pretty. Tim wanted the balloon very much. He saw a vendor with many balloons like the one he wanted. The vendor was walking away.
Tim decided to follow the vendor to get the balloon. He walked and walked, but the vendor was too fast. Tim got lost and did not know where he was. He looked around and could not see his mom and dad.
Tim started to cry. He wanted his mom and dad. But he could not find them. Tim was sad and scared. He never got the enormous balloon and he was lost. The day ended very bad for Tim.

One day, Jack was walking in the park. Jack saw a tree with a sign that said "Stay". Jack thought it was fun, so he decided to stay.
Jack got very uncomfortable because he had been sitting there for a while. He wanted to move around, but he remembered the sign and stayed.
Soon, Jack saw a bird flying high above him. He watched it fly in the sky until it was gone. Jack smiled, then he got up and moved away from the sign. He had a nice time, but it was time to go home.
He took one last look at the sign and waved goodbye. He turned away and began to walk home. He felt happy, now that he had signed away from the uncomfortable sign.

One day, a little girl named Lisa was looking for something special to do. She looked in her garden and saw a pepper plant. She was very excited and kneeled down to take a closer look.
Suddenly, Lisa heard a funny sound. She turned around and saw a foolish bird standing in the middle of the garden. The bird was bobbing up and down like it was dancing. Lisa giggled and said, "Hi little bird! What are you doing?"
The bird cocked its head and said, "I'm standing here waiting for you to pick the pepper! I love pepper!". Lisa couldn't stop laughing. She quickly picked the pepper and handed it to the bird. The bird thanked her and flew away.
Lisa stood there for a moment, and then she smiled. She had had the most amazing day, and it was all thanks to a silly little bird!

Once upon a time, in a small valley, there lived a humble bird named Tiny. Tiny loved to dive and play with his friends. The valley was full of beautiful flowers and big trees. Tiny and his friends would fly high and then dive down to the ground, laughing and having fun.
One day, Tiny met a new friend named Flappy. Flappy was very shy and did not know how to dive. Tiny said, "Hi Flappy! Do you want to play with us and dive?" Flappy replied, "I don't know how to dive, Tiny. I'm scared."
Tiny wanted to help Flappy. He told Flappy, "Don't be scared. I will teach you how to dive. It's easy and fun!" So, Tiny and Flappy practiced diving together. At first, Flappy was scared, but he soon learned how to dive and started to have fun.
All the birds in the valley were happy to see Flappy and Tiny playing together. They learned that helping friends is a good thing to do. The birds in the valley were kind and humble, always helping each other when they needed it.
And so, Tiny, Flappy, and their friends played and dived together every day. They had lots of fun, and the valley was filled with laughter and happiness. The moral of the story is: always be kind and help your friends when they need it.

Lucy was walking in the park one day when she saw something gray. She ran over to see what it was and found a fluffy gray cat. The cat curled up at Lucy's feet and seemed very friendly.
"Can I keep you?" Lucy asked, excitedly.
The cat meowed and rubbed against Lucy's legs. She decided the cat was her new friend.
"I'll call you Gray," Lucy smiled.
The next day Lucy went back to the park to see Gray, but the cat was gone. She was so sad.
Then a naughty boy appeared. He had a mischievous smile on his face and was carrying a box.
"I stole this cat," the boy said with a laugh.
Lucy was very surprised and asked the boy why he had taken Gray.
The boy shrugged and said, "I made a promise to my little sister that I would get her a pet."
Lucy said, "You should not steal, you should ask first! Here, I will help you take care of Gray."
The boy was very relieved and agreed to never steal again.
So from then on, Lucy and the boy took very good care of Gray and the three of them became very good friends.

One day, Tom and Anna were playing in the park. They saw a huge phone on a bench. It was shiny and had many buttons.
"Wow, look at that phone!" Tom said. "I want to play with it."
"No, Tom, we should not touch it," Anna said. "It belongs to someone else. Maybe they will come back soon."
But Tom did not listen. He ran to the bench and grabbed the phone. He pressed some buttons and made funny noises.
"Ha ha, this is fun!" he said.
Suddenly, the phone rang. It was very loud. Tom was scared. He dropped the phone and ran back to Anna.
"Help, help!" he cried. "The phone is angry at me!"
Anna looked at the phone. She saw a man running towards it. He looked angry too.
"Hey, that's my phone!" the man shouted. "Who touched it? You broke it!"
He picked up the phone and saw that the screen was cracked. He was very sad.
"I'm sorry, sir," Anna said. "My brother did it. He did not know better. He is only three."
The man sighed. He looked at Tom and Anna.
"Listen, kids," he said. "You should never touch things that are not yours. You could hurt them or yourself. And you should always listen to your sister. She is smart and kind. She tried to stop you."
Tom felt ashamed. He nodded.
"I'm sorry, sir," he said. "I'm sorry, Anna. I was wrong. I will not do it again."
The man smiled. He was not so angry anymore.
"It's okay, Tom," he said. "I forgive you. But you have to be more careful next time. And you have to help me fix my phone. It is very important to me."
Tom agreed. He and Anna helped the man fix his phone. They learned a lesson that day. They learned to respect other people's things and to listen to each other. They also made a new friend.

Once upon a time, in a small town, there was a grumpy old cat named Mr. Whiskers. He lived in a little square house with his best friend, a happy dog named Spot. They liked to play together, but sometimes Mr. Whiskers would refuse to play with Spot because he was too grumpy.
One sunny day, Spot wanted to play outside with Mr. Whiskers. He asked, "Mr. Whiskers, will you play with me?" But Mr. Whiskers just said, "No, I am too grumpy today." Spot was sad, but he did not give up. He thought of a plan to make Mr. Whiskers happy.
Spot found a big, soft ball and rolled it to Mr. Whiskers. Mr. Whiskers looked at the ball and then at Spot. He saw how happy Spot was and decided to give it a try. They played with the ball all day, and Mr. Whiskers became less grumpy. From that day on, Mr. Whiskers and Spot played together every day and lived happily in their little square house.

Tom and Lily were twins who liked to play outside. One day, they saw a big factory near their house. It had many windows, pipes and smoke. They wanted to see what was inside.
"Let's climb the fence and go in!" Tom said. He was brave and curious.
"No, Tom, that's bad. Mama said we should not go near the factory. It's dirty and dangerous," Lily said. She was careful and obedient.
But Tom did not listen to Lily. He climbed the fence and ran to the factory door. He tried to open it, but it was locked. He looked for another way in.
Lily followed Tom, but she was scared. She did not want to get in trouble. She saw a sign that said "Keep Out". She tried to stop Tom.
"Tom, come back! This is not a place for us. We should go home and play with our toys," she said.
But Tom did not hear Lily. He found a hole in the wall and crawled inside. He saw many machines, wires and buttons. He wanted to touch them and see what they did.
Lily saw Tom disappear into the hole. She was very worried. She did not want to go in, but she did not want to leave Tom alone. She decided to follow him and try to get him out.
But when she got inside, she saw something that made her scream. There was a big, mean dog guarding the factory. It had sharp teeth and angry eyes. It saw Tom and Lily and started to bark and chase them.
Tom and Lily were very afraid. They ran away from the dog, but they did not know where to go. They got lost in the factory. They wished they had never come.
The dog was faster than them. It caught up with them and jumped on them. It bit Tom's leg and made him cry. It scratched Lily's arm and made her bleed.
Tom and Lily were in pain and in trouble. They wished they had listened to Mama and stayed away from the factory. They wished they had not been so lazy and played with their toys instead. They wished someone would help them.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lily. She loved to sing and dance. One day, Lily found a black microphone in her toy box. She was very happy and wanted to show it to her friends.
Lily had an idea. She wanted to decorate the black microphone to make it look pretty. She got her stickers and put them all over the microphone. There were stickers of stars, hearts, and flowers. It looked so nice.
Lily called her friends to come over and see her pretty black microphone. They all came and loved it. They took turns singing into the microphone and had so much fun. They danced and laughed all day. And they all lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time there was a very weak girl. She always felt alone and was too weak to play with the other kids.
One day the girl went out to explore. She walked through a field and saw the beauty of nature. She was amazed at how green the trees were and how pretty the flowers smell. She felt so happy.
Suddenly she heard something. She hid behind a tree, and saw a baby bird. The bird had fallen out of its nest and couldn't get back. She was so small and weak, but she knew she had to help.
The girl carefully picked up the bird and placed it back in the nest. The other birds were very happy, and thanked her with chirruping noises.
The little girl smiled and was happy she was able to help the baby bird. She thanked nature for giving her the chance to help. She knew that she was weak, but she was also strong in her own way.

Lily and Tom were playing in the garden. They liked to look at the flowers and the bugs. They saw a big yellow and black bee flying near a red rose. Lily wanted to see the bee closer, so she walked to the rose.
"Be careful, Lily!" Tom said. "The bee can sting you and it will hurt!"
Lily did not listen to Tom. She reached out her hand to touch the bee. But the bee did not like that. It buzzed loudly and flew away. Lily followed the bee with her eyes. She saw it land on a big rock.
Lily ran to the rock. She wanted to catch the bee. She climbed on the rock and tried to grab the bee. But the bee was too fast. It flew away again. Lily lost her balance and fell off the rock. She bumped her head and started to cry.
Tom heard Lily cry and ran to her. He saw her lying on the ground with a bump on her head. He felt sorry for her. He hugged her and said, "It's okay, Lily. I will help you."
He helped her stand up and took her to their mom. Their mom saw the bump and put some ice on it. She said, "Lily, you should not chase the bee. It is silly and dangerous. The bee does not want to play with you. It wants to make honey and pollinate the flowers. You should leave the bee alone and play with something else."
Lily nodded and said, "I'm sorry, mom. I was silly. I will not chase the bee again. Can I play with Tom?"
Mom smiled and said, "Yes, you can play with Tom. But be careful and have fun."
Lily and Tom went back to the garden. They decided to play with their ball instead of the bee. They threw the ball to each other and laughed. They had fun and forgot about the bee and the rock.

One day, a grumpy cat named Tom went for a walk. He saw a little girl named Sally. Sally was having a tea party with her dolls. She saw Tom and wanted him to join.
"Come, Tom! Have tea with me," said Sally. Tom did not want to join. He was grumpy and did not like tea parties. But Sally kept asking, so Tom said yes to make her happy.
As they had tea, Tom saw a big cake. Sally said, "Surprise! I made this cake for you, Tom!" Tom was happy and not grumpy anymore. They ate the cake and had fun at the tea party.

Once upon a time, there was a little bird named Tim. Tim lived in a small, fragile nest up in a tree. One day, Tim's mom told him that he would need to search for food on his own for the first time.
Tim was scared but excited to search for food. He remembered watching his mom find tasty worms and juicy berries. So, Tim flew down from the tree and started to look around. He found a big, red berry and picked it up with his beak.
As Tim was flying back to his nest, he saw a big, scary cat waiting near the tree. Tim knew the cat wanted to eat him and his fragile nest. But Tim was smart. He threw the red berry away from the tree. The cat ran after the berry, and Tim quickly flew back to his nest. Tim's mom was so proud of him for finding food and outsmarting the scary cat.

Sara likes to sort her toys. She has many toys of different colors and shapes. She puts the red toys in one box, the blue toys in another box, and so on. She thinks sorting is fun and makes her toys look neat.
One day, Sara hears a noise in the living room. She goes to see what it is. She sees a big cat on the sofa. The cat is black and white and has long fur. The cat looks at Sara with green eyes. Sara thinks the cat is pretty and wants to pet it.
Sara walks slowly to the sofa. She does not want to scare the cat. She is very quiet and gentle. The cat does not run away. It lets Sara touch its fur. The cat purrs and rubs its head on Sara's hand. Sara smiles and feels happy.
The cat is graceful and jumps off the sofa. It follows Sara to her room. Sara shows the cat her toys. She says, "This is my red box. This is my blue box. I sort my toys by color. Do you want to help me?" The cat nods and meows. Sara and the cat sort the toys together. They have a lot of fun and become friends.

Once upon a time there were two children, Jack and Jill. They were very excited because they were going on an adventure together.
The two of them stepped outside and they saw an enormous mountain in the distance.
"Wow, look at that mountain!" said Jack in awe. "Yes, let's go to the top of it," said Jill excitedly.
So they stepped forward and began the journey up the mountain. It was a difficult climb, with lots of rocks and hills, but Jack and Jill kept going and didn't stop. Finally, after a long time, they reached the top of the mountain.
The view was nice. Jack and Jill could see all the trees and the sky from up there. They even saw a bird flying near some pretty flowers.
"This is so nice," said Jill happily.
"Yes, it is!" said Jack.
The two of them stayed on the top of the mountain for a little while, enjoying the moment and then, when it was time to go, they stepped down the mountain together.
"That was a fun adventure!" said Jack.
"Yes, it was fun," echoed Jill.
As they stepped back home, they were excited to tell their friends all about their mountain climb.

One day a young girl named Sarah was playing with her favourite toy, a soft teddy bear. She hugged it tight as it always made her feel happy and safe.
Suddenly her Dad appeared in the room. He smiled and said to Sarah, "I have a surprise for you."
Sarah looked at him with excitement. "What is it?" she asked.
"I need you to trust me," he said.
Sarah looked even more curious. "Why?" she asked.
"Just trust me and close your eyes," said Dad.
Sarah closed her eyes and when she opened them again, there was a shiny new toy car in front of her!
Sarah gasped and said, "Thank you!" She raced around the room with the toy car, smiling from ear to ear.
Dad had given her the surprise to show her how much he trusted her, and in turn Sarah trusted him one hundred percent. Sarah was feeling very lucky to have such a wonderful Dad — even at her young age.

Bob hopped around with joy and said "Yes please!"

Once upon a time there was a flower who was feeling very uncomfortable. He was feeling unhappy and he wanted to feel better.

The butterfly was so excited by this that it flew down and landed on the four-leaf clover. The flower was so happy that he had become a four-leaf clover that he smiled and now they are best friends.
The flower had become a four-leaf clover, made a new friend and was happy now. The end.

Once upon a time, there were two small mice named Max and Tim.
Max and Tim were very good friends who loved to explore their big lab together.
One day, Max said to Tim, “Let’s go explore the lab!”
Max and Tim were so excited that they quickly followed each other around the lab.
They were having loads of fun.
As they explored the lab, they came across a small room that they had never seen before.
Max said to Tim, “Let’s go in!”
Tim said, “No, Max. We shouldn’t go in there. We don’t know what could be inside”.
Max thought for a moment and then agreed with Tim.
Max said, “You’re right Tim. We don’t know what’s in there, so it’s best to be safe and not go in.”
So Max and Tim stuck together and continued exploring the lab.
The moral of this story is to always strive to make safe decisions. Even if it looks exciting or fun, it's important to think about the risks before doing something.

Anna liked to dream. She dreamed of big things and small things, of happy things and scary things, of things that were real and things that were not. Sometimes she dreamed when she was asleep, and sometimes she dreamed when she was awake.
One day, Anna found a big trunk in the attic. It was dull and dusty, and it had a lock on it. Anna wondered what was inside. She tried to open it, but it was too hard. She asked her mom for help, but her mom said no.
"Anna, that trunk is not for you. It belongs to your grandpa. He left it here when he went away. He said it has some old things that he wants to keep. You can't open it, okay?"
Anna felt sad. She wanted to see what was inside the trunk. She wanted to dream about it. She thought maybe there were toys, or books, or clothes, or treasures. She wished her grandpa was here. She missed him a lot.
That night, Anna had a dream. She dreamed that she was in the attic, and the trunk was open. She saw her grandpa sitting next to it, smiling at her. He had a big hat on his head, and a coat on his back, and a bag on his shoulder. He looked like he was ready to go on an adventure.
"Hello, Anna," he said. "Do you want to see what's in the trunk?"
Anna nodded. She ran to the trunk and looked inside. She saw many things that made her eyes wide and her mouth open. She saw a map, and a compass, and a telescope, and a camera, and a knife, and a flashlight, and a whistle, and a medal, and a flag, and a picture, and a letter, and a key.
"These are some of the things I used when I traveled around the world," her grandpa said. "I saw many places, and met many people, and learned many things. I had a lot of fun, and a lot of trouble, and a lot of stories. Do you want to hear some of them?"
Anna nodded again. She sat next to her grandpa and listened to his stories. He told her about the mountains, and the oceans, and the jungles, and the deserts, and the cities, and the villages, and the animals, and the plants, and the stars, and the moon. He made her laugh, and gasp, and wonder, and dream.
Anna was very happy. She hugged her grandpa and thanked him for sharing his things and his stories with her. She told him she loved him and missed him. He hugged her back and told her he loved her and missed her too. He said he was proud of her and hoped she would grow up to be brave and curious and kind. He said he would always be with her in her heart and in her dreams.
Then Anna woke up. She was in her bed, and the sun was shining. She smiled and rubbed her eyes. She remembered her dream and felt warm and cozy. She jumped out of bed and ran to the attic. She wanted to see the trunk again. She wanted to dream some more.

Mum and Dad were in the park with their 3 year old daughter, Lucy. They watched as all the other people around them lit up the sky with beautiful fireworks.
"I don’t understand,” said Lucy. “What are those sparkly lights in the sky?”
Mum and Dad smiled at their daughter's inquisitive mind.
“Those are fireworks, sweetheart," said Dad. "People use them as a way of having fun and celebrating special occasions."
"Wow," said Lucy, her eyes wide with awe.
The adults watched as the sweet lights lit up the night sky, and Lucy's face beamed with excitement. She had never seen anything like it before, and it made her feel happy.
The displays came to an end and Lucy asked her Mum and Dad if she could have some fireworks of her own.
Mum and Dad smiled. “We can arrange that,” said Mum. “But you have to understand that fireworks can be dangerous, so you must always be careful.”
Lucy nodded and smiled. “I understand,” she said with a giggle, eagerly looking forward to her very own fireworks.

One day, a slow turtle named Tim went for a walk in the sun. He saw a big flame in the grass. Tim was scared. He did not know what to do. So, he went to find his friend, a fast rabbit named Rob.
"Rob, I need help!" said Tim. "There is a big flame in the grass. What do we do?" Rob thought for a moment. "I have an idea!" he said. "Let's grab some water and put out the flame."
They both ran to the pond and filled their buckets with water. They went back to the flame and threw the water on it. The flame was gone! But, something unexpected happened. A magic fairy appeared where the flame was.
"Thank you for helping me," said the fairy. "I was stuck in that flame. As a thank you, I will give you both one wish." Tim and Rob were very happy. They made their wishes and went on more fun adventures together.

Lily liked to sing. She sang all the time. She sang in the morning, she sang at lunch, she sang in the bath. She sang loud and she sang soft. She sang happy and she sang sad. She sang songs she knew and songs she made up.
One day, her mom took her to a big house with many seats. She said they were going to see an opera. Lily did not know what an opera was, but she was excited. She liked to see new things. She wore a blue dress and a blue bow in her hair. She felt very pretty.
When the opera started, Lily saw people on a stage. They wore costumes and they sang. But they did not sing like Lily. They sang very high and very low. They sang in a language Lily did not understand. They sang with music and with no music. They sang with faces and with no faces. Lily was confused. She did not like the opera.
She tried to taste the opera. She licked her lips and her teeth. She stuck out her tongue and moved it around. She hoped the opera would taste like candy or cake or ice cream. But the opera did not taste like anything. It only tasted like air. Lily was disappointed. She did not like the opera.
She looked at her mom. Her mom smiled and clapped. She looked at the people around her. They smiled and clapped too. They liked the opera. Lily felt alone. She did not like the opera.
She wanted to go home. She wanted to sing her own songs. She wanted to taste something sweet. She wanted to wear a different color. She did not like the opera. She did not like the opera at all.

One day, a hungry cat went out to look for food. It saw a cooler under a big tree. The cat was very happy and thought there might be food inside the cooler. It tried to open the cooler with its paws, but it was too hard for the cat to do it alone.
Then, the cat saw a strong dog walking by. The cat asked the dog, "Can you help me open this cooler? I am very hungry." The dog was kind and wanted to help the cat. So, they both tried to open the cooler together.
With the dog's strong paws, they split the cooler open. Inside, they found yummy food to eat. The cat and the dog shared the food and became good friends. They were not hungry anymore and played together under the big tree.

One day, a little boy named Tim was worried. He saw a big sack in the shower. He did not know what was in the sack. He asked his mom, "Mom, what is in the sack in the shower?"
His mom said, "Oh, that is a surprise for you, Tim. But you can only open it after you take a bath."
Tim was excited. He took a bath very fast. After the bath, he went to the shower to open the sack. He pulled the sack open and found a big, soft towel. It was not what he thought would be in the sack. Tim was not sad, but he was not happy either.
Just then, his dad came in and said, "Surprise, Tim! That towel is just one part of the surprise. Let's go outside to see the rest of it." They went outside, and Tim saw a big, new toy car waiting for him. He was so happy and could not wait to play with it. The sack in the shower was just the start of a fun day for Tim.

Once upon a time, there was a strong tree. It was very big and tall. The tree had many leaves. They made a nice shade under it. Many animals liked to be under the tree. They felt cool and safe there.
One hot day, a little bird came to the tree. The bird was tired and thirsty. It said, "Can I be in your shade? I am very hot and tired. I need to rest." The tree was kind and said, "Yes, little bird. You can be in my shade. Rest and be cool here."
The little bird was happy. It stayed under the tree and rested. Soon, it felt better. The bird thanked the tree and said, "You are a very strong and kind tree. Thank you for your shade." The tree was happy to help the bird. They became good friends.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tom. Tom loved to play with his toy gear. He would spin it, roll it, and make it go fast. Tom had a dog named Spot. Spot was a very obedient dog. He would sit, stay, and come when Tom asked him to.
One day, Tom and Spot went to the park. They played with the gear and had lots of fun. After a while, Tom wanted to go home. He called Spot to come, but Spot did not listen. Tom did not know what to do, so he just shrugged.
Tom walked home without Spot. He was very sad. His obedient dog did not come back with him. Tom's toy gear was not fun to play with anymore. He missed his friend Spot. The end.

Once upon a time there was a humble skeleton. Whenever it went outside it was scared of being seen by other creatures. So it walked in the shadows, tiptoeing quietly, looking only at the ground.
One day the skeleton was feeling braver and decided to take a walk outside. As it was walking through the grass, it noticed a tiny orange bug sitting by a flower. The skeleton was thrilled! It had never seen a bug before and wanted to get a closer look.
But then it heard a loud noise coming from the other side of the garden! The skeleton got so scared that it ran away into the shadows.
The noise came from a small bunny who was hopping around the garden. The bunny was curious and had been looking for the skeleton. When he didn't find it he felt sad.
The skeleton watched from his hiding place and realised that the bunny was not scary. Slowly it came out of the shadows and came closer to the bunny. They said hello, and the bunny smiled. They were friends.
From then on, the humble skeleton was never scared to go outside, because now he had a special friend. The end.

Tom and Sam are brothers. They like to play with their toys. They have a lot of cars, trains, and planes. They also have a big box of staff. Staff are things that they find in the house or outside. They have buttons, sticks, stones, feathers, and more.
One day, they decide to make a weird staff plane. They take a big piece of cardboard and cut out a shape of a plane. They glue some staff on it to make it look cool. They use buttons for eyes, sticks for wings, stones for wheels, and feathers for a tail. They are very proud of their staff plane.
They want to show their mom and dad their staff plane. They carry it to the living room, where their mom and dad are watching TV. They say, "Look, look, we made a staff plane! It is very weird, but we like it!"
Their mom and dad smile and say, "Wow, that is a very weird staff plane! You are very creative, boys! How did you make it?"
Tom and Sam tell them how they spend a lot of time cutting, gluing, and decorating their staff plane. They say, "We had a lot of fun! We love staff!"
Their mom and dad say, "We love you, boys! And we love your staff plane! Can we play with it too?"
Tom and Sam say, "Yes, yes, you can play with it too! But be careful, it is very weird!"
They all laugh and play with the staff plane. They have a good time together. They are a happy family.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tom. Tom was not feeling well. His mom gave him medicine to help him feel better. But Tom did not want to take it. He said "no" and refused the medicine.
Tom's mom said, "Tom, you need to take your medicine to feel comfortable again." Tom thought about it and decided to listen to his mom. He took the medicine, even though it tasted bad.
After a while, Tom started to feel better. He was comfortable and happy again. He played with his toys and thanked his mom for giving him the medicine. Tom learned that sometimes, we need to do things we don't like to feel better.

Once upon a time, there was a boy named Tim. Tim had a brilliant flashlight. He loved to play with it in the dark. One night, Tim decided to go on an adventure in the woods with his flashlight.
As Tim walked through the woods, he saw many animals. He was not scared because he had his brilliant flashlight. Suddenly, something unexpected happened. Tim tripped on a rock and his flashlight fell into a puddle. The flashlight stopped working.
Tim was very sad and embarrass. He did not know how to find his way back home without his flashlight. As he tried to find his way, he got lost in the woods. Tim wished he had been more careful with his flashlight.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lily. She loved to draw with her pink crayon. One day, she drew a big sun on a piece of paper. She was very proud of her drawing.
Lily showed her drawing to her mom. Her mom said, "Wow, Lily! You drew the sun so well. Look, it's rising in the sky!" Lily looked up and saw the sun rise higher and higher. She felt very happy.
The next day, Lily took her pink crayon and drew a big heart. She showed it to her friend, Sam. Sam said, "I like your heart, Lily! It's so pink and pretty!" They both smiled and played together all day long.

Once upon a time, there was a big, smelly bear. He loved to eat strawberries. One day, he went to the strawberry field to pick some. But when he got there, he saw that the strawberries were very small and not good to eat. The bear was sad.
Then, a little bird came to the bear. The bird said, "I can help you improve the strawberries. You just need to water them and give them some love." So, the bear and the bird worked together. They watered the strawberries and gave them lots of love.
After some days, the strawberries grew bigger and better. The bear and the bird were very happy. They ate the strawberries together and became good friends. And the bear was not smelly anymore, because he learned to take care of the strawberries and himself too.

Sara and Tom were playing in the park. They liked to swing and slide and run. They saw a big white dog near the fence. The dog was barking loud and showing its teeth.
"Look, a dog!" Sara said. "Can we pet it?"
"No, Sara, it is not a nice dog. It is angry and scary. Stay away from it," Tom said.
But Sara did not listen. She wanted to touch the white fur. She walked close to the fence. The dog saw her and jumped. It tried to bite her hand.
Sara screamed. She was fearful. She ran back to Tom. Tom hugged her and said, "Are you okay, Sara? That was a bad dog. You should not go close to it."
Sara nodded. She was sorry. She said, "I am okay, Tom. Thank you for helping me. You are a good friend. I will not go close to bad dogs again."
They went to find their mom. She gave them a snack and a kiss. She said, "I am glad you are safe, Sara and Tom. You should always be careful with dogs you do not know. Some of them are not friendly. You should always ask before you pet them."
Sara and Tom agreed. They learned a lesson. They finished their snack and played some more. They had fun in the park. They did not go close to the white dog again.

Mum and Dad were packing a suitcase full of clothes, ready for their holiday. When they asked Bob, their three-year-old son, what he wanted to take he was so excited he couldn't sit still - he jumped up and down and kicked his little legs.
"I want to take my teddy bear and my helicopter!", he said with a big, excited grin.
Mum smiled at him and said, "That sounds great, but you know what else you should take? Something special, just for the holiday?"
Bob was feeling a bit nervous, but he said, "What is it?".
"A beach ball!", said Dad. "We can spend some time kicking it around together on the beach!"
At this, Bob smiled, hopped up, and ran over to get his beach ball. He grabbed it and gave it a big kick.
"Hooray!" he said, feeling very happy about his special holiday item.

Once upon a time there was an elephant who lived in a jungle. Every day he would look for food and remain by a river. One day, the elephant saw something strange! On the other side of the river was a bundle of bananas. The elephant wanted to eat them, but the river was too wide for him to get to the other side. Suddenly, he noticed a log that was available for him to cross. He happily made his way across and ate the yummy bananas for his lunch. He remained there for a while, happy and content. He returned home when the sun began to set, and he looked forward to coming back the next day, just in case the bananas were still available!

Ben and Lily were playing knights and dragons in the garden. Ben had a stick for a weapon and Lily had a plastic sword. They pretended to fight the evil dragon that lived in the bush.
"Roar!" Lily said, waving her sword. "I am the dragon and I will eat you!"
"No, you won't!" Ben said, swinging his stick. "I am the brave knight and I have a sharp weapon!"
They ran around the garden, making noises and having fun. But then Ben tripped over a rock and fell down. He hurt his knee and it started to bleed.
"Ow, ow, ow!" Ben cried, holding his knee. "It hurts so much!"
Lily stopped playing and ran to him. She saw the blood and felt sorry for him.
"Oh, Ben, I'm sorry!" she said, dropping her sword. "Are you okay?"
She hugged him and tried to make him feel better. She looked at his stick and saw that it had a pointy end.
"Ben, your weapon is too sharp!" she said. "It can hurt you or me. We should not play with it."
She threw the stick away and picked up her sword.
"Look, my weapon is not sharp," she said. "It is only plastic. It is better for playing. And it is fancy, too. See, it has a shiny handle and a red ribbon."
She gave her sword to Ben and smiled.
"Here, you can have my weapon," she said. "You can be the knight and I can be the dragon. But we have to be careful and not hurt each other. Okay?"
Ben nodded and took the sword. He felt a little better and smiled back.
"Okay," he said. "Thank you, Lily. You are a nice dragon and a good friend."
They hugged again and then continued to play, but more gently and safely. They had fun and forgot about the pain.

The little girl asked the square if it wanted to play. The square was so excited that it said yes. The little girl smiled and hugged the square. She said that she would accept the square no matter what.
The two of them played together every day and had so much fun. They became best friends. Now, no matter how dark or how alone the square gets, it knows that someone really cares.

Once upon a time, in a big garden, there was a small flower. The flower wanted to bloom and be pretty like the other flowers. The flower tried very hard to grow, but it was not easy.
One day, a long caterpillar came to the garden. The caterpillar saw the small flower and wanted to help it. The caterpillar told the flower to drink water and get sun. The small flower listened to the caterpillar and did what it said.
Soon, the small flower began to grow and bloom. The flower was very happy and thanked the caterpillar. The flower and the caterpillar became good friends. The flower's success in blooming made the garden even more beautiful. And they all lived happily ever after.

One day, a little boy named Tim wanted to get a toy from the cupboard. He tried to pull the cupboard door, but it was too high. He found a small chair and stood on it. Now he could reach the cupboard door.
When he pulled the door open, a wet ball fell out. It was his lost ball! He was so happy to find it. He picked up the wet ball and played with it in his room.
Later, his mom came into the room and saw the wet ball. She asked, "Why is the ball wet?" Tim told her about the cupboard. They went to look at the cupboard together. When they opened the door, they found a water bottle had spilled inside. The wet ball was not lost after all; it was just hiding in the wet cupboard.

Once upon a time, there was a famous dog named Spot. Spot liked to help people. One day, Spot saw a lot of cars in the street. This was called traffic. The cars were not moving. The people in the cars were sad.
Spot had an idea. He went to a big button near the street. Spot jumped up and pushed the button. The button made the cars stop and the people walk. The people were happy. They could cross the street now.
A man saw Spot and said, "Thank you, Spot! You helped us by raising the button." Spot barked and wagged his tail. The people learned that it is good to help others. And Spot was happy because he made the people smile.

Once upon a time there was a bird. The bird wanted to soar high in the sky. The bird found a worm and started munching on it. After eating the worm, the bird spread out its wings and flew through the air. The bird's wings were smooth like silk. It felt so good to soar! The bird flew higher and higher and felt so free. Soon the bird saw a big tree and flew down to a branch. The bird flapped its wings and landed on the branch. The bird was tired from soaring. It curled up and fell asleep. The bird had a nice dream of soaring through the sky and feeling so free.

Tom and Lily are playing in the park. They see a big brown dog. The dog is sleeping on the grass. Tom likes dogs. He wants to touch the dog. Lily is scared of dogs. She does not want to touch the dog.
"Look, a dog!" Tom says. He runs to the dog. He stares at the dog. He puts his hand on the dog's fur. The dog is soft and warm.
"No, Tom, don't touch the dog!" Lily says. She runs after Tom. She stares at the dog. She sees the dog's teeth. The dog is big and scary.
The dog wakes up. He sees Tom and Lily. He does not like strangers. He barks at Tom and Lily. He shows his teeth. He looks angry.
"Help, help!" Tom and Lily say. They are afraid of the dog. They run away from the dog. They find their mom. They hug their mom.
"Mom, mom, there was a big brown dog. He barked at us. He was angry. We were scared." Tom and Lily say.
"It's okay, it's okay. You are safe now. You should not touch a dog you don't know. He might bite you. You should ask the owner first. Or you should stay away from the dog." Mom says.
"Okay, mom. We are sorry. We will not touch a dog we don't know. We will be careful." Tom and Lily say.
Mom smiles. She kisses Tom and Lily. She takes them to the playground. They play with their toys. They forget about the dog. They are happy.

Once upon a time, in a small house, lived a deaf dog named Max. Max loved to dress up in his red leather collar. Every day, Max would wear his collar and play in the yard.
One sunny day, Max was playing with his ball. He saw a big tree with many birds. The birds were singing, but Max could not hear them. Max was happy, though, because he could see the birds and feel the sun on his face.
As Max played, his friend, a little girl named Lucy, came to visit. Lucy and Max played all day long. They laughed and had fun together. When the sun went down, Max and Lucy went inside the house. Max's red leather collar shined in the light. They were tired but very happy. Max and Lucy knew they would have more fun days together.

Once upon a time, there were two brothers. Alex was the older brother and John was the younger brother. One day, Alex saw an automobile that he wanted to buy. The automobile was very tight and looked like it was in good condition. Alex asked his brother if he could borrow the money to buy the automobile, but John said no. Alex was very unhappy, but he understood why John said no.
John had learned to value money, and he knew automobiles could break down easily. He wanted Alex to save his money and spend it on things that he really needed. Alex thanked his brother and promised to remember the lesson of valuing his money.
The brothers worked hard and saved their money. Eventually, Alex was able to buy the automobile he wanted without having to borrow money from his brother. He valued his money and appreciated having a car, but he never forgot the valuable lesson his brother had taught him.

Once upon a time, in a busy little house, there lived a spider. The spider made a big cobweb in the corner of the room. The spider liked the house and did not want to leave.
One day, a girl saw the cobweb. She did not like it. She wanted the spider to leave. She got a broom and tried to sweep the cobweb away. But the spider was fast and hid from the girl.
The next day, the girl saw that the spider had caught many flies in the cobweb. The girl was happy because she did not like flies. She let the spider stay and they became friends. The spider and the girl lived happily in the busy little house, and the spider never had to leave.

Once upon a time there lived two neighbors. They were named Lilly and Stan. Lilly was a pretty girl and Stan was a strong boy. Every day, the two would play in the garden together and have lots of fun.
One day, Lilly and Stan had a big fight. It was so big that they couldn't agree with each other no matter how hard they tried. "Stop that!", Stan said.
Lilly didn't like what Stan said, so she started to cry. Stan felt very sad because he didn't mean to make his neighbor cry. He thought of an idea. He asked Lilly, "Want to play tag with me? It's fun!"
Lilly was happy again. She smiled and said, "Yes!" So the two neighbors played tag and laughed together until day was night. They were the best of friends again, and it was a very pretty moment.

Once upon a time there lived a little boy named Ben. Ben lived in the city with his parents. One day Ben and his parents went for a walk in the park. While they were talking Ben noticed a hidden crib in the bushes. He asked his parents what it was doing there. His parents told him that inside the crib there was a baby that had been forgotten.
Ben was very sad to see and he didn't want to leave the baby alone but his parents pointed out that they had no way of taking care of the baby. But Ben had an idea. He asked his parents if they could bring the baby home and take care of him. Ben's parents were surprised at the kindheartedness of their son and agreed.
Once they were back home, Ben and his parents took care of the baby. They cut his hair and fed him and soon the baby was healed. Ben was very happy to have helped and his parents were proud of him.
This story of Ben teaches us that it is important to help others in need and that it is possible to heal even the most broken of hearts when we demonstrate kindness.

Tim and his mom were happy again. They went to the store and bought beef. They went home and Tim's mom made him his favorite beef meal. Tim was so happy because he got to eat beef and also because he saw the dog play with the ball. The end.

Once there was a cheerful girl called Elsa. One day she went to find a mirror so she could check if her hair was neat. She looked around but couldn't find one. Suddenly she heard a voice that said, "let me help you".
Elsa was surprised and turned around to see a friendly magic mirror. He said he could help Elsa find a mirror. Elsa was so excited and thanked the magic mirror.

One day, a big lion with a loud roar was walking in the woods. He saw a basket under a tree. The lion was very curious about the basket. He went close to the basket and looked inside. He saw a tiny kitten, who was uncomfortable and scared. The kitten looked up at the lion with big eyes.
The lion felt bad for the kitten. He wanted to help the kitten feel better. So, the lion picked up the basket with his teeth and carried it to his cave. The kitten was still scared, but the lion was gentle and kind. He made a warm bed for the kitten in the cave.
As the days went by, the kitten and the lion became best friends. The kitten was not scared anymore, and the lion did not roar as loud as before. They played together in the woods, and the kitten even learned how to roar like a lion. And the lion, who was once known for his loud roar, became the kindest and gentlest lion in the woods.

Once upon a time there was a red crane. He was standing in a pond and wanted to take a bath.
He said to the bunny who was hopping nearby, "Can you help me take a bath?"
The bunny replied, "I can help you with bathing, but I won't be able to reach in the pond with you."
The crane thought for a while and then said, "Ok, I will climb onto your back, then you can hop around in the pond."
The bunny nodded and the crane hopped onto his back. They slowly hopped around the pond. The water splashed all around them as the bunny hopped and the crane enjoyed his bath.
After a while, the bunny stopped and said, "Ok, you can get off now, you are all clean."
The crane thanked the bunny and hopped off. He was thankful for the bunny's help and was feeling refreshed after his bath. He took off, flying away as red as a ruby.

The family came into the room and saw the rat on the bed. They were not mad. They thought the rat was cute. The family gave the rat some food and let him stay in the house. The rat and the family became good friends.
