Sue went to Tim's house and said, "Tim, come look at my dough cat! I made it with my delicate hands!" Tim came to see the cat and said, "Sue, your cat is so pretty! Can you teach me how to make one too?"
Sue and Tim sat down and started to play with the dough. They talked and laughed as they made more dough animals together. Sue taught Tim how to be delicate with the dough, just like her. They had so much fun playing and talking with each other.

One day, a small bird named Tim was very proud. He lived on a big mountain. Tim liked to fly high and snap little bugs with his beak.
One morning, Tim saw a big cloud near the mountain. He knew it would rain soon. Tim told his friends, "Let's find a safe place to hide." But his friends did not listen. They wanted to play and have fun.
Later, the rain came. It was heavy and the wind blew hard. Tim was safe in his hiding place. His friends were wet and cold. They wished they had listened to Tim. After the rain stopped, Tim's friends said, "You were right, Tim. We should have listened to you." Tim felt proud and happy that he could help his friends.

Once there was a happy boy named Tim. One day he was looking for something to do and he found a switch. It was a big red switch and Tim couldn't wait to push it. He pressed the switch and everything around him began to shake and rumble. Then the switch said "Destroy". Tim was scared and he ran as fast as he could. He ran away until he saw a big cloud of smoke. He was very sad because he had destroyed everything. Later he saw a new switch, only this time it was green. Tim was very curious and he pushed it with all his strength. Suddenly his house and all his toys were restored! Tim was very happy and he ran to tell his parents the good news. They were so happy and they hugged Tim tightly. From then on, Tim was very careful not to touch any switches he didn't know.

One day, a little boy named Tim wanted to change the color of his wall. His wall was blue, but he wanted it to be red. He thought it might be difficult, but he wanted to try. He asked his mom, "Can I change the wall color?"
His mom smiled and said, "Okay, Tim. But you need to be careful and ask for help if it is difficult."
Tim started to paint the wall red. He was very happy. Suddenly, he heard a strange noise. He looked around and saw a little bird near the window. The bird had paint on its wings. The bird had flown into the red paint and was now painting the wall too!
"Wow!" Tim said, "You are helping me change the wall color. Thank you, little bird!" The bird chirped and flew away, leaving a beautiful pattern on the wall. Tim's wall was now red and had a special bird pattern. It was not difficult at all, and he was very proud of his new wall.

One day, a little girl named Emma went for a walk in the park. She saw a tiny fairy near a flower. The fairy was flying in circles and looked dizzy. Emma asked, "Why are you dizzy, fairy?"
The fairy said, "I am dizzy because I fly too fast. I need to slow down." Emma and the fairy sat down to discuss how to fly better. They talked about how it is good to take breaks and not always be in a hurry.
The next day, the fairy tried to fly slower and take breaks. She felt much better and was not dizzy anymore. Emma and the fairy became good friends. They learned that it is important to slow down and take care of themselves.

Joshua was an adventurous boy. One day, he decided to travel far away. He got dressed, grabbed his penny and said goodbye to his parents.
On his way, Joshua came across a big hill. He tried to climb it, but he slipped and hurt his knee. He cried out, “Ouch! I hurt my knee!”
Just then, he heard a voice from the top of the hill. It was a friendly owl who said, “Hello, little one! Don’t worry, I can help. Let me give you a ride.”
Joshua smiled. He hopped on the owl’s back and they flew over the hill. After they landed, Joshua thanked the owl and waved goodbye.
He set off on his way again and kept one eye out for more adventures. Before long, he made it to his destination with the help of his penny and the friendly owl.

One day, a boy named Tim found a ladder in his yard. He wanted to see what was up high. So, Tim put the ladder against the big tree. He started to climb up, step by step. Tim thought it was fun.
When Tim got to the top, he saw a big, red ball. He wanted to play with it. But the ball was stuck in the tree. Tim tried to get the ball. He pulled and pulled. Then, the ball started to tear. Tim felt stupid for tearing the ball.
Just as the ball tore, a bird flew out of it! The ball was not a ball, but a bird's nest. Tim was so surprised. He climbed down the ladder and told his mom about the bird in the tree. They both laughed at the unexpected twist.

Once upon a time, in a small town, there lived a little boy named Tim. Tim loved to eat fruits. His favorite fruit was a lime. One day, Tim went to the market with his mom to buy some fruits.
At the market, Tim saw a man selling limes. He asked his mom, "Mom, can I have a lime, please?" His mom said, "Of course, Tim. But first, let's buy some other fruits." Tim insisted, "No, I want a lime now." His mom agreed and bought him a lime.
Tim took a bite of the lime and made a face. It was not sweet like the other limes he had before, but mild. Tim's mom said, "That's what happens when you don't wait, Tim. If you had waited, I could have picked a sweet lime for you." Tim learned that it is important to be patient and listen to others.

Sam and Ben are friends. They like to play with a basketball. They go to the park and find a big hoop. Sam throws the ball and it goes in. Ben claps and says, "Good job, Sam!"
Ben wants to try too. He picks up the ball and runs to the hoop. But he is careless. He does not look where he is going. He bumps into a man who is walking his dog. The man falls down and the dog barks. The ball flies away and lands on a scale.
The man is angry. He says, "Hey, watch where you are going, kid! You made me fall and hurt my knee. And you messed up the scale. It says I weigh 100 pounds more than I do!"
Ben is scared. He says, "I'm sorry, mister. I did not see you. I was trying to throw the ball in the hoop. Please don't be mad at me."
Sam runs to Ben and helps him get the ball. He says, "Are you okay, Ben? That was a bad accident. You should be more careful next time. Come on, let's go play somewhere else."
They leave the park and go to Sam's house. They play with the basketball in the backyard. They are more careful and have fun. They are still friends.

"Mom, can I have some toast, please?" asked Amy. Her mom smiled and said, "Of course, my dear. I know you missed breakfast, so I am making toast just for you."
Amy was so happy. She sat at the table and waited for her toast. Her mom gave her a plate with two yummy pieces of toast. They were warm and smelled so good. Amy ate her toast and felt better. She was not tired anymore.
After eating, Amy and her mom went outside to play. They had lots of fun together in the sun. Amy was glad she had the toast and that she didn't miss any more fun.

The lawyer was also sad. He said, "I will miss you too. But I have to go." So, he left his big house and his friends. He went far away. He did his work and helped many people. But he was confused. He missed his friends and his big house.
One day, the lawyer came back home. He was very happy. But when he got to his big house, he saw something unexpected. His friends were all there, waiting for him! They had a big party. The lawyer was not confused anymore. He was happy to be home with his friends. They all said, "We missed you!" And they lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time, there was a door. The door was very attractive. Everyone liked it. But one day the door broke down. Nobody knew how to fix it.
The people went to the shop and bought a new door. But the new door didn't look as nice as the old one.
The people asked a wise old man to help them. He said, "This is an important lesson for you. You should always fix things when they are broken, instead of buying new ones. It is better for the environment, and your wallet!"
The wise old man fixed the door. Everyone was happy and thanked him.
The moral of the story is: fix things instead of throwing them away.

Once upon a time, there was a rich king who had a big castle. He had a lot of nice things and tasty food. The king loved to enjoy his big castle and all his toys.
One day, the king met a poor boy who lived in a small house. The poor boy had an urge to play with the king's toys. So he asked, "Can I play with your toys, please?" The king thought for a moment and said, "Yes, you can play with my toys."
The poor boy and the king played together and had a lot of fun. But then, something unexpected happened. The king saw that the poor boy was much happier than him, even though he didn't have many things. The king learned that being rich is not just about having a lot of things, but also about having friends and being happy. From that day on, the king shared his toys with all the kids in his kingdom, and they all enjoyed playing together.

Lila and Ben were twins who liked to go on adventures. One day, they saw a big mountain near their house. They wanted to climb it and see what was on top.
"Can we go, Mom?" they asked. "Please, please, please?"
Mom smiled and said, "Yes, you can go, but be careful and come back soon. And take this basket of food with you. You might get hungry on the way."
Lila and Ben were happy and thanked Mom. They took the basket and started to walk to the mountain. They saw many things on the way: birds, flowers, rocks, and bugs. They had fun and sang songs.
When they reached the top of the mountain, they were tired and hungry. They opened the basket and saw what Mom had packed for them: bread, cheese, apples, and cookies. They also saw a small jar of red sauce.
"What is this?" Lila asked.
"I don't know," Ben said. "Let's try it."
They dipped a piece of bread in the sauce and took a bite. They made a funny face and spit it out.
"Yuck!" they said. "This is spicy! It hurts our mouths!"
They drank some water and ate the other food. They decided to leave the sauce alone. They looked around and saw a beautiful view of the land. They felt happy and proud.
"Wow!" they said. "This is amazing! We did it! We climbed the mountain!"
They hugged each other and smiled. Then they packed the basket and started to go back home. They couldn't wait to tell Mom about their adventure.

Tim and Sam were playing in the woods. They liked to pretend they were animals and run and hide. Tim was a deer and Sam was a hunter. Sam had a stick for a gun and a hat for a cap.
"Bang! Bang!" Sam shouted, pointing his stick at Tim. "I got you, deer! You have to surrender!"
Tim shook his head and ran away. He did not want to surrender. He wanted to keep playing. He ran behind a big tree and waited for Sam to find him.
Sam looked around and saw the tree. He smiled and crept closer. He thought he had Tim trapped. He was ready to shoot again.
But Tim had a plan. He saw a dry branch on the ground. He picked it up and threw it to the other side of the tree. It made a loud noise.
Sam heard the noise and turned his head. He thought Tim was there. He ran to the other side of the tree, leaving his stick behind.
Tim saw his chance. He jumped out of his hiding place and grabbed Sam's stick. He pointed it at Sam and shouted, "Bang! Bang! Now I got you, hunter! You have to surrender!"
Sam was surprised and laughed. He put his hands up and said, "OK, OK, you got me, deer! You are very smart and fast. I surrender!"
Tim and Sam hugged and smiled. They were happy. They liked to play together. They decided to switch roles and play again.

Once upon a time there was a lady who had a cane. One day was very dry, so she took her cane and went out.
The lady showed her cane to all the animals in the forest. She showed the birds, and the dogs, and all the other animals. Even the ants crawled along the cane!
The lady laughed as she watched all the animals looking at her cane. Then she went to the river, and showed the cane to the fish. She waited and watched, but the fish were not very interested in the cane.
Soon it started to get dark, so the lady took her cane and went home. The lady was very happy, because she had a lot of fun showing her cane.

Once upon a time, there was a little fish named Fin. Fin loved to swim all day long. One day, he saw a road near the water. It was a big, expensive road with many cars. Fin was curious and wanted to see the road up close.
"Be careful, Fin!" said his mom. "The road is not safe for little fish like you."
But Fin didn't listen. He swam closer to the road. He saw the big cars going very fast. Fin thought it was fun to watch them. He wanted to swim even closer.
As Fin swam closer, a big wave from a car splashed him onto the road. Fin was scared and could not swim back to the water. He tried to call for help, but no one could hear him. In the end, poor Fin could not get back to the water and he was very sad.

One day, a big swan went to the lake. It was a nice day. The sun was bright, and the sky was blue. The swan wanted to play with the fish in the lake. So, it swam around and around, looking for fish to play with.
As the swan swam, it saw some food on the ground. The food was all scatter. The swan was hungry, so it ate the food. Suddenly, the swan began to feel funny. It felt like it was growing bigger and bigger. The swan was scared.
Then, the swan saw a little girl. She was holding a magic wand. She said, "Don't worry, big swan. I made you big with my magic. I wanted to see a big swan. But now, I will make you small again." She waved her wand, and the swan became small. The swan was happy and swam away, ready to play with the fish.

One day, Leo had a big meal. He used a napkin to clean his face. After eating, he felt sleepy. So, he took a nap under a tree. While he was sleeping, a little mouse named Max came by. Max saw the napkin and thought it was a fun toy. He played with it and made a mess.
When Leo woke up, he saw the messy napkin. He was not happy. He asked, "Who did this?" Max was scared, but he said, "I did it, I am sorry." Leo saw that Max was small and said, "It is okay. But remember, we must take care of our things." Max learned that it is important to respect others' belongings. From that day on, Max and Leo became good friends.

Tim looked into the hole and saw a small, shiny box. He pulled the box out of the hole and opened it. Inside the box, there was a map and a note. The note said, "Go on an adventure and find the hidden treasure!" Tim was so excited! He put the object back in the hole, took the map, and started his new, unexpected adventure.

Once upon a time, there was a cow that lived in an orange field. He was very happy in his orange field and enjoyed eating the grass all day long.
One day, a farmer came to visit the cow. The farmer said, "I need your help. Can you please behave nicely? There are some sheep that I need to take back home."
The cow nodded and listened patiently. He wanted to help, so he decided to behave and stay calm. He was a good cow.
The farmer was so grateful. He told the cow, "Thank you for behaving so well. You really helped me out today."
The cow was glad that he had been a good cow. He knew he should always behave nicely. Even though the orange field was his home, he still wanted to help others.

One day, a charming dog named Max found a big red jeep. Max wanted to go for a ride, so he jumped in. The jeep had a big button that said "Go." Max pressed the button and the jeep started to move. Max was very happy and barked, "Woof! This is fun!"
As Max was driving, he saw his friend, a cat named Lily. Lily looked sad, so Max stopped the jeep and asked, "Why are you sad?" Lily said, "I lost my toy and can't find it." Max wanted to help, so he told Lily to get in the jeep. Together, they looked for Lily's toy.
They looked and looked but could not find the toy. Max had an idea. "Let's lie down and look under the bushes," he said. They both lay down and looked under the bushes. To their surprise, they found not only Lily's toy but also a big box of treats! Max and Lily were very happy. They shared the treats and played with the toy all day.

One day, there was a bald man named Bob. Bob wanted to get some nails to fix his broken window, so he went to the store. Bob saw a friendly shopkeeper there.
The shopkeeper said "What can I do for you, Bob?"
Bob answered, "I need some nails."
The shopkeeper said, "Sure thing! How many do you need?"
Bob said, "Six please."
The shopkeeper gave him six nails and Bob thanked him. Bob said goodbye to the shopkeeper and went home to fix his window with the nails.
The end.

One day, a little ball named Bounce went for a walk. Bounce was a stubborn ball who always wanted to go left. So, he bounced left and left again. He loved to bounce and see where he would land.
As Bounce was bouncing left, he saw a big tree. He thought it would be fun to bounce into the tree. So, he did. But something unexpected happened! Instead of bouncing back, he got stuck in the tree. Bounce was very sad and scared.
Just then, a friendly bird saw Bounce and wanted to help. The bird flew up to the tree and gently pushed Bounce out. Bounce was so happy to be free! From that day on, Bounce learned to bounce in other directions too, and he made many new friends along the way.

Once upon a time, there was a girl named Lily. She liked to prepare a special gift for her mom's birthday. She thought her mom would love a perfume. So, she went to the store to find one.
At the store, Lily found a perfume that smelled very strong. She thought her mom would like it. She bought the perfume and went home to wrap the gift. She was excited to give it to her mom.
On her mom's birthday, Lily gave the gift to her mom. Her mom opened it and saw the strong perfume. But then, something unexpected happened. Her mom said, "Thank you, Lily, but I don't like strong smells. Let's give this perfume to grandma. She loves strong perfume." Lily was surprised, but she was happy that her grandma would like the gift.

In a big sea, there was a gray ship. The ship had many friends. They liked to play all day. They did not know what was far away.
One day, the gray ship saw a big fish. The big fish was lost. The ship and its friends wanted to help. They did not know where the fish's home was.
Then, a little bird came. The little bird knew where the big fish lived. The gray ship and its friends were happy. They went with the little bird to take the big fish home. The big fish was not lost anymore.

One day, a little girl named Sue went to the store with her mom. They needed to buy milk for their home. Sue liked to help her mom, so she held the milk. The milk felt cold in her hands.
On their way home, they saw a man with a big, unusual hat. The man said, "Hello, do you want to see a trick?" Sue and her mom said, "Yes, please!" The man took a long, twisty stick from his pocket. He twisted the stick in the air and said, "Watch the milk!"
Sue looked at the milk in her hands. It started to twist and shake. Suddenly, the milk changed into a big, twisty snake! Sue and her mom screamed and ran away. They were so scared. They never saw the man or the snake again, but they also never got their milk back.

One day, a kind girl named Lucy was troubled. She did not know what to do. She wanted to add a new law to help her friends. Lucy thought hard and had an idea.
She went to her friend, Tom. "Tom," she said, "I want to add a law to be nice to everyone. Can you help me?" Tom smiled and said, "Yes, Lucy! I will help you."
Lucy and Tom told all their friends about the new law. Everyone liked the idea. They all promised to be nice to each other. Lucy was not troubled anymore. She was happy that she could help her friends.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim was a clumsy boy who always fell and hurt himself. One day, Tim went to the park to play with his friends.
While playing, Tim tripped and fell. He hurt his lip and started to cry. His friends tried to help him, but they didn't know what to do. Then, a kind old man saw Tim and his friends. The old man kneeled down to see Tim's lip.
The old man said, "Don't cry, little one. I will help you." He took out a magic wand and waved it over Tim's lip. Suddenly, Tim's lip was all better! Tim and his friends were so surprised and happy. They thanked the old man and promised to be more careful when they play.
From that day on, Tim learned to be less clumsy and more careful while playing. The moral of the story is that we should always be careful and help each other when someone is hurt.

One day, a boy named Tim went to the park to play. He saw a cheap, old rake on the ground. He picked it up and started to rake the leaves. Tim thought it was fun to make a big pile of leaves.
A girl named Sue came to the park too. She saw Tim and said, "I want to play with the rake too!" Tim didn't want to share, so he ran away with the rake. Sue was sad and started to cry.
A big wind came and blew the leaves all around. The leaves covered Tim, and he couldn't see. Sue heard Tim's cry for help and ran to him. She used the rake to move the leaves away. They both laughed and decided to share the rake and play together.

Once upon a time, in a messy swamp, there lived a big frog named Fred. Fred loved to hop and play in the mud all day long. He had many friends who lived in the swamp too, like ducks, fish, and bugs.
One sunny day, Fred saw a little lost bird. The bird was sad and scared. Fred wanted to help the bird find its family. So, Fred called to his friends in the swamp. "Come, help the little bird!" he said.
All of Fred's friends came to help. They looked high and low for the bird's family. At last, they found the bird's family in a tree near the swamp. The little bird was so happy to see its family again. Fred and his friends cheered, and they all played together in the messy swamp.

Once upon a time there was a tree. It was big and green and very tall. All around the tree there were colorful things.
Every day, a man and a boy would come to the tree. The boy was only three years old and he wanted to gather the colorful things. He would take all he could find and put them in his pockets.
The man and the boy went back to the tree almost every day. Each day, the boy was able to find more colorful things.
The man was very proud of the boy and he would give him a hug each time they had gathered all the things. The boy was happy and he knew he had made his dad proud.
So, from that day on, the man and the boy kept gathering around the tree every day until there were no more colorful things to find.

Once upon a time, there was a pale cat named Mill. Mill lived in a little house near a big tree. Mill was a happy cat, but sometimes he would refuse to play with the other animals.
One day, a small bird came to visit Mill. The bird said, "Hello Mill, do you want to play with me?" Mill looked at the bird and said, "No, I refuse to play today." The bird was sad and flew away.
Later, Mill saw the bird playing with other animals and having fun. Mill felt lonely and decided to join them. He said, "I am sorry for refusing to play before. Can I play with you now?" The bird smiled and said, "Of course, Mill! Let's have fun together." And they all played happily ever after.

One morning, Tim saw a delicious cake on the table. He wanted to eat it but his cap fell into the cake. The cake was all messy. Tim was sad.
Tim's mom saw what happened. She said, "Don't worry, Tim. I will replace the cake with a new one." Tim was happy. He got a new delicious cake and a clean cap.

Once upon a time, in a big forest, there was a fearful little dog named Spot. Spot was always scared of everything. One sunny day, Spot decided to go for a walk in the forest.
As Spot was walking, he met a big, strong bear. The bear looked at Spot and said, "Why are you so scared, little dog? There's nothing to be afraid of in this forest." Spot looked at the bear and said, "But I'm always scared. I don't know how to be brave."
The bear insisted, "Come with me, Spot. I will show you how to be brave." So Spot and the bear walked together in the forest. They saw many things and met many friends. Spot started to feel less scared with the bear by his side.
By the end of the day, Spot was not fearful anymore. He was a brave little dog, all thanks to his big, strong bear friend. And from that day on, Spot and the bear were the best of friends, always playing and having fun in the big forest.

Once upon a time, there was a good angel named Ella. She lived in a big, beautiful sky. Ella liked to help all the little animals and people. She would fly down and give them hugs and smiles.
One day, Ella saw a small bird who was very tired. The bird tried to fly, but it could not. Ella knew the bird needed to rest. So, she made a soft, warm nest for the bird. The bird said, "Thank you, Ella!" and it went to sleep.
Ella was happy that she could help the bird. She flew back up to the sky and told all her angel friends about her good deed. They all clapped and cheered for Ella. Then, they all went to rest on the fluffy clouds. And they all lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time there was a little girl named Katie. She was three years old and very curious. One day, she was playing in the living room when she noticed an ashtray. It was dirty and overflowing with ashes.
Katie got closer and wanted to touch it to see what it felt like. Just as she was about to pick it up, she heard a loud voice.
"No, no, no!", said her mom. "That ashtray is dirty, so you must separate yourself from it."
Katie obeyed and stepped away from the ashtray. After a few moments, her mom said, "That's better. Now you know how important it is to keep your hands away from dirty things."
Katie nodded and solemnly promised she would never touch the dirty ashtray again. From then on, she remembered to always stay away from anything that was not clean.

The little girl went out for a walk in the woods. She saw an old rock. It was a big one and she was excited to see it. She tried to pick it up but it was too heavy. She pointed to it and said, “Rock.”
Suddenly, a spider came out from behind the rock. It was busy spinning a cobweb. The little girl was curious. She reached out her hand to touch the cobweb.
Suddenly, a voice came from behind her.
“Be careful, my dear,” the voice said. “The spider might bite you if you touch his cobweb. But if you are careful, I have something yummy that you can have.”
The little girl smiled with anticipation as she followed the voice. It led her to a berry bush. The voice told her that she could pick the ripe berries and eat them. She was delighted and picked the yummy berries one by one.
Later, when the little girl went back to the rock, she realized that the voice belonged to a kind old woman who had lived in the woods for a long time. She thanked the woman for her kindness and went home with a full tummy.

Once upon a time, there was a helpful boy named Tom. He liked to play chess with his friends. One day, Tom met a new friend named Sam.
"Hi, Sam! Do you want to play chess with me?" Tom asked.
Sam looked at the chess game and said, "I don't know how to play chess. Can you teach me?"
Tom was happy to help. "Of course, Sam! I hope you will like it!" Tom then showed Sam how to move the chess pieces. They played together and had lots of fun.
In the end, Sam learned how to play chess. They both became good friends and played chess every day. They always hoped to be helpful to each other and have fun together.

Once upon a time, in a big forest, there lived a cute bunny and a tiny mouse. They were best friends. They played together every day. They loved to share their food and eat together.
One day, the cute bunny found a big pot of soup. The soup smelled so good that it made his stomach stir. The tiny mouse saw the soup too and felt envious. He wanted the soup all for himself. So, the tiny mouse told the cute bunny that there was a big carrot far away. The cute bunny loved carrots, so he went to find it.
While the cute bunny was away, the tiny mouse tried to eat all the soup. But the soup was too hot for him. He burned his tongue and cried. The cute bunny heard the tiny mouse cry and came back. He saw that his friend was hurt and felt sad. The cute bunny helped the tiny mouse feel better by giving him some cool water.
The tiny mouse learned that being envious and not sharing with friends is not good. He said sorry to the cute bunny, and they shared the soup together when it was not too hot. From that day on, they always shared their food and played together, and they were happy.

One day, a boy named Tim went to the park with his mom. They saw a big pitch where kids were playing ball. Tim was happy and wanted to play too.
Tim's mom said, "Okay, but don't go too far." Tim went to play with the kids. They were having fun, but then an angry dog came. The dog wanted the ball. The kids got scared and paused. They did not know what to do.
Then, Tim had an idea. He said, "Let's give the dog the ball!" The kids threw the ball far away. The angry dog ran after it. The dog was not angry anymore, and the kids went back to playing. Tim's mom was proud of him.

Once upon a time, there was a popular dog named Spot. Spot was a good dog who liked to help people. One day, Spot was playing in the park with his friends.
Suddenly, Spot saw a man who was trying to succeed in getting a cat out of a tree. The cat was scared and did not want to come down. Spot wanted to help the man and the cat. So, he ran to the tree and started to climb up.
As Spot got closer to the cat, he saw that the cat was not just a cat, but a lion cub! Spot was scared, but he knew he had to help. Spot helped the lion cub come down from the tree. The man was very happy and thanked Spot for his help. Spot became even more popular, and everyone in the town loved him. They all knew that Spot was a brave dog who could even save a lion cub from jail in a tree.

Jack was out in the garden looking at the flowerbeds. He saw a daisy and he just had to stare at it. It was so pretty! He wanted one of his own, so he went to the garden centre. The shopkeeper showed him lots of daisies in different colors. Jack noticed that the yellow daisies were the cheapest.
He said, "How much is one of these?" The shopkeeper replied, "These yellow daisies are only a dollar." Jack smiled and said, "I'll take one." He paid the shopkeeper and then he went back to the garden.
He planted the daisy and stared at it admiringly. It was so bright and beautiful! Jack was so happy that he had such a cheap and pretty daisy in his garden. He said to himself, "The daisy is my favorite flower forever!"

Once upon a time, in a small town, there was a boy named Tim. Tim was not very smart and did not know much. People in the town called him ignorant. One day, Tim saw a big truck. The truck had fuel in it. The fuel made the truck go fast.
Tim wanted to make the truck go faster, so he thought he should remove the fuel. He didn't know that taking out the fuel would make the truck stop. He went to the truck driver and said, "I want to help you. Let's remove the fuel to make the truck go faster!"
The truck driver laughed and said, "No, Tim! If we remove the fuel, the truck will not go at all. The fuel is important for the truck to move." Tim was confused, but he listened to the truck driver.
From that day, Tim learned that he should not try to change things he did not understand. He started to ask people for help and learn from them. The people in the town stopped calling him ignorant. The moral of the story is to ask for help when you don't know something and learn from others.

One day, a little bird was in its nest in a tree. It started to rain. The bird felt helpless because it could not fly yet. It wanted to find a place to stay dry. The bird noticed a big leaf nearby.
"Can I stay under your leaf to stay dry?" the bird asked the leaf. The leaf said, "Yes, come under me. You will stay dry here."
The rain stopped after a while. The bird said, "Thank you, leaf, for keeping me dry." The leaf was happy to help the little bird. They became friends and played together every day.

One day, a little girl named Lucy went to the park. She saw her friend Tom playing with a toy. Lucy was tired, but she wanted to play too. She walked to Tom and said, "Hi Tom, can I play with you?"
Tom looked at Lucy and said, "Yes, let's play with my toy!" They played together and had lots of fun. After a while, they heard music coming from a big tree. They went to the tree to see where the music was coming from.
Under the tree, they found a small box. When they opened the box, the music played louder. Lucy and Tom danced and laughed. They were not tired anymore. They played and danced until it was time to go home.

Lily and Ben were friends who liked to play with sheep. They had many sheep toys of different colors and sizes. They liked to make them jump, run, and sleep in their room.
One day, Lily had an idea. She said to Ben, "Let's organize our sheep. We can put them in groups by color and size. It will be fun and neat."
Ben agreed. They started to sort their sheep into piles. They had a pile for white sheep, a pile for black sheep, a pile for big sheep, and a pile for small sheep. They were happy with their work.
But then, they heard a loud noise. It was Lily's mom. She came into the room and saw the sheep everywhere. She was angry. She said, "What are you doing? This is a mess! You have to clean up your toys right now!"
Lily and Ben were scared. They tried to explain that they were organizing their sheep, but Lily's mom did not listen. She grabbed all the sheep and threw them into a big box. She said, "You can't play with these anymore. You have to learn to be tidy and respectful."
Lily and Ben were sad. They watched as their sheep were taken away. They felt uncomfortable and sorry. They did not understand why their mom was so mad. They did not have fun anymore. They wished they had never organized their sheep.

Once upon a time, there was a big whale. The whale was very charming. He had a big smile and big eyes. He lived in the deep blue sea. All the fish and sea animals loved the charming whale.
One day, the charming whale met a little fish. The little fish was very scared. He was trembling. The charming whale asked, "Why are you trembling, little fish?" The little fish said, "There is a big storm coming. I am scared."
The charming whale wanted to help the little fish. He said, "Do not be scared, little fish. I will keep you safe." The little fish felt better. They swam together in the deep blue sea.
But the big storm came. The water got dark and the waves got big. The charming whale and the little fish tried to swim away. They swam and swam, but the storm was too strong. The charming whale and the little fish got tired. They could not swim anymore. The storm took them away, and they were never seen again.

She saw two cars speeding on a street near the store. They were going too fast and it seemed like they were going to crash. The little girl was scared and thought it was so dangerous. She wished that the cars would slow down.
Just then, a man wearing a blue uniform ran past her. He seemed to be running very fast in the same direction as the cars. The little girl figured he was going to help stop them.
Soon she saw the cars stop, just in time and the man was standing in between them. She felt so relieved and excited. The man had prevented a crash, and was incredible. The little girl was glad that no one got hurt.  She quickly went back into the store with a smile to finish getting everything she needed.

One day, a little bird was sitting on a branch. The branch was very high up in the tree. The bird felt uncomfortable because the wind was strong. The bird was scared it would fall off the branch.
A kind squirrel saw the bird and wanted to help. The squirrel said, "Hi, bird! I can help you. Come to a lower branch with me." The bird was happy and said, "Yes, please help me."
The squirrel helped the bird find a lower branch. They both sat on the new branch, and the bird felt much better. The bird and squirrel became good friends. They played and ate nuts together. The bird was thankful for the squirrel's help.

Once upon a time, an elderly dog named Max lived near a creek. Max loved to chew on sticks all day. One sunny day, Max went to the creek to find a new stick to chew.
As Max walked by the creek, he saw a big fish. The fish looked at Max and said, "Hello, Max! Do you want to be my friend?" Max was very surprised because fish don't usually talk.
Max and the fish became good friends. They played and talked every day. One day, Max found out that the fish was a magic fish. The fish said, "Max, I can make one wish come true for you." Max wished for a never-ending supply of sticks to chew. The magic fish made Max's wish come true, and Max was the happiest dog in the world.

Ben and Mia liked to visit the farm with their mom and dad. They saw many animals there, like cows, pigs, sheep and chickens. But their favorite animal was the big ostrich. The ostrich had long legs, a long neck and black and white feathers. It could run very fast and make funny noises.
One day, the farmer told them that the ostrich had laid some eggs. He said they could go and see them, but they had to be careful and quiet. The ostrich was very protective of her eggs and did not like strangers. Ben and Mia nodded and followed the farmer to the ostrich pen.
They saw the ostrich sitting on a big pile of hay. She had gathered many eggs under her. The eggs were huge and white, with brown spots. Ben and Mia thought they looked impressive. They wanted to touch them, but they remembered what the farmer said. They stayed behind the fence and watched the ostrich.
Suddenly, the ostrich lifted her head and looked at them. She saw Ben and Mia and made a loud noise. She got up and ran towards them. She looked angry and scary. Ben and Mia screamed and ran away. The farmer came and calmed the ostrich down. He said she was just trying to protect her eggs. He said they should not go near her again.
Ben and Mia learned their lesson. They decided to admire the ostrich and her eggs from a distance. They still liked the ostrich, but they respected her space. They hoped that one day they would see the baby ostriches hatch from the impressive eggs.

Bobo and the big horse played all day. They were very happy. But then, something unexpected happened. As they played, they found a big, shiny stone behind the stable. They looked at it and wondered what it could be.

They wished for a big party with all their friends. And just like that, the farm became full of happy animals, having fun and playing together.

Once upon a time, in a small town, there was a boy named Tim. Tim loved ice-cream very much. He wanted to eat ice-cream every day. But his mom said, "No, Tim. You can't eat ice-cream every day. You must eat good food too."
One hot day, Tim saw a man selling ice-cream. Tim asked, "Can I have some ice-cream, please?" The man said, "I will give you ice-cream if you help me work." Tim was happy to help. They worked together all day long. They sold many ice-creams to kids in the town.
At the end of the day, the man gave Tim a big ice-cream. Tim said, "Thank you!" and went home. When Tim's mom saw him, she asked, "Where did you get the ice-cream?" Tim told her about his work. His mom smiled and said, "Good job, Tim. You learned that working hard can bring you treats."
The moral of the story is that working hard can bring rewards, and it's important to help others.

One day, a girl named Mia went for a walk in the fog. She wanted to find something fun to play with. Mia saw a big box and it said "include fun things inside". She was very happy and opened the box.
Inside the box, Mia found a spicy pepper, a ball, and a book. She thought the spicy pepper was a toy, so she took a bite. Her mouth was very hot! Mia did not know what to do. She ran to find some water to drink.
As Mia was drinking the water, the fog started to go away. She saw a magic cat standing there. The cat said, "I made a mistake. I put the spicy pepper in the box by accident." The cat waved its tail and the spicy feeling went away. Mia and the magic cat played with the ball and read the book together. They had a great day and became good friends.

One day, a little girl named Lucy went to the kitchen. She saw a big jar of sugar on the table. Lucy was very impatient. She wanted to taste the sweet sugar.
Lucy opened the jar and put some sugar on her hand. She looked at it and examined the tiny white pieces. They were so shiny and pretty.
Lucy tasted the sugar and it was so sweet. She was happy and put the sugar back on the table. Then, she went to play with her toys, feeling excited about her sweet discovery.

Lila and Ben were playing in the park when it started to rain. They ran to the big tree to stay dry, but the rain was too heavy and they got wet.
"Let's go to the shelter!" Ben said. He pointed to the small house near the swings, where the park workers kept their tools and things. "It has a roof and a door and we can be warm and dry there."
But Lila did not want to go. She liked the big tree and she was not afraid of the rain. She was stubborn and said no.
"I want to stay here!" she said. "The tree is my friend and it protects me. The shelter is dark and scary and it has spiders and rats. I don't like it there."
Ben tried to make her change her mind. He said the tree was not a good friend, because it let the rain fall on them. He said the shelter was not dark and scary, because it had a window and a light. He said there were no spiders and rats, because the park workers cleaned it every day. He insisted that they should go, because they would catch a cold if they stayed wet.
But Lila did not listen. She shook her head and hugged the tree. She said she was fine and she did not care about the cold. She insisted that they should stay, because the tree was the best place in the park.
They argued and argued, until they heard a loud noise. It was thunder, and it made them both jump. Then they saw a bright flash. It was lightning, and it struck the big tree. The tree cracked and fell, and Lila and Ben screamed.
They ran away from the tree, and saw that it was on fire. They were scared and sad, and they wished they had listened to Ben. They ran to the shelter, and knocked on the door. The park worker opened it, and let them in. He gave them towels and blankets, and made them hot chocolate. He said they were lucky, and they should always be careful in a storm.
Lila and Ben nodded, and thanked him. They looked at each other, and said they were sorry. They hugged, and promised to be less stubborn and more smart. They learned their lesson, and they never argued about the shelter again.

Once upon a time, there was a grumpy cat named Tom. Tom did not like to play with other cats. He liked to be alone. One day, Tom saw a big door. He thought it was a great escape from the other cats.
Behind the door, Tom found a big room with many tables. The tables had a lot of yummy food. Tom saw a dog named Max. Max was there to serve the food to the animals. Tom thought Max was nice, but he did not want to be friends.
Tom ate the food and felt happy. Then, something unexpected happened. The door closed, and Tom could not go back. He was sad and wanted to escape. Max saw Tom and helped him open the door. Tom was surprised that Max helped him.
Tom and Max went outside, and Tom said thank you. Tom learned that having friends was nice. He was not grumpy anymore. Tom and Max played together and had fun. They became best friends, and Tom was happy.

Once upon a time, there was a little duck named Ducky. Ducky loved to swim in the pond near his home. One sunny day, Ducky went to the pond to play with his friends.
While they were swimming, Ducky saw a frog named Freddy. Freddy was wet from jumping in the water. "Hi, Freddy!" said Ducky. "Do you want to swim with us?" Freddy smiled and said, "Yes, I love to swim!"
As they swam and played, they saw a big fish named Fin. Fin liked to clap his fins to make music. Ducky, Freddy, and their friends swam, clapped, and danced in the water. They had so much fun together.
At the end of the day, Ducky, Freddy, and their friends were tired but happy. They said goodbye and went back to their homes. And they knew they would have more fun days at the pond together.

Lily and Ben are twins. They like to play with their toys and books. But sometimes they get bored and want to do something new. One day, they see their mom lying on the couch. She looks tired and sad.
"Mom, why are you on the couch?" Lily asks.
"I have a headache, sweetie. I need some rest," mom says.
"Can we help you, mom?" Ben asks.
Mom smiles and says, "You can help me by being quiet and gentle. Maybe you can give me a massage. That means you rub my back and shoulders with your hands. It feels nice and makes me relax."
Lily and Ben nod and climb on the couch. They take turns to massage mom's back and shoulders. They are careful not to hurt her or tickle her. Mom closes her eyes and sighs.
"Thank you, my loves. You are very good at massage. You make me feel better," mom says.
Lily and Ben hug mom and say, "You're welcome, mom. We love you."
Mom hugs them back and says, "I love you too. You are very kind and sweet. You know what? I think I can allow you to watch a movie with me. How does that sound?"
Lily and Ben cheer and say, "Yay! That sounds great, mom. Can we watch the one with the animals?"
Mom says, "Sure, why not? But remember, some animals are dangerous. You should never touch them or go near them in real life. They can bite or scratch or hurt you. You should only watch them on the screen."
Lily and Ben say, "OK, mom. We understand. We will be safe and smart."
Mom says, "Good. Now, let's find the remote and enjoy the movie."
Lily and Ben cuddle with mom on the couch. They watch the movie and laugh and learn. They are happy and cozy. Mom is happy and cozy too. She forgets her headache and enjoys her massage and her movie. She thinks, "I have the best twins in the world."

One day, it was rainy and Tim was sad. He could not play outside. He asked his mom, "What can I do to make the sun come out?" His mom said, "We can pray for sunshine, Tim."
So, Tim and his mom closed their eyes and prayed together. They asked for the sun to come out so Tim could play outside. After they prayed, they waited.
The next morning, Tim woke up and saw sunshine through his window. He was very happy. He ran outside to play and thanked his mom for praying with him. From that day on, whenever it was rainy, Tim and his mom would pray for sunshine together.

Once upon a time, there was a cute dog named Bob. He lived in a lab with his friend, a kind man named Tim. Tim liked to play with Bob, and they had a lot of fun together.
One day, Tim said to Bob, "Let's go outside and play with a ball!" Bob was very happy and wagged his tail. They went outside, and Tim set the ball on the ground. Bob ran fast and picked up the ball in his mouth.
They played all day until the sun went down. Bob and Tim were tired but happy. They went back to the lab and had a big meal. Then, they went to bed, ready for another fun day together.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lucy. Lucy had a big, attractive bottle. The bottle was very pretty, with lots of colors. Lucy loved her bottle very much.
One day, Lucy's bottle was lost. She looked everywhere for it. Lucy was very sad. She asked her mom and dad to help her find her bottle. They all looked together, but they could not find it.
Then, Lucy had an idea. She thought about where she last saw her bottle. Lucy went to her room and found her bottle under her bed. She was so happy that she solved the problem. Lucy, her mom, and her dad all smiled and hugged. Lucy was very happy to have her attractive bottle back.

One day, a little girl named Ann wanted to bake a cake. She asked her mom, "Can I bake a cake?" Her mom said, "Yes, you can. Let's bake a cake together."
They went to the store on their street to buy things to make the cake. The street was dull and not many people were there. They bought eggs, flour, and sugar. Then, they went back home to start baking.
Ann and her mom mixed all the things in a big bowl. They put the mix in the oven to bake. After a while, the cake was ready. It smelled yummy! Ann and her mom ate the cake together and it was very tasty. They had a fun day baking the cake.

Once upon a time, in a big tree, there was a chubby dove. The dove loved to fly around and play with its friends. One day, the dove met a little boy named Tim.
"Hello, little boy," the dove said to Tim. "Would you like to play with me?"
Tim was happy and said, "Yes! I want to play with you, chubby dove."
As they played, the dove would fly up high and then come back down. Tim would laugh and clap his hands. Tim would then say, "Repeat!" and the dove would do it again. They played this game for a long time, and they both had so much fun.
When it was time for Tim to go home, he said, "Goodbye, chubby dove. I had fun playing with you today." The dove said, "Goodbye, Tim. I had fun, too. Let's play again soon!" And so, they became the best of friends, and they played together every day.

Once upon a time, there was a big red barn. In the barn, there were many animals. There was a hairy cow, a big pig, and a small duck. They all liked to play together.
One day, the animals decided to sort their toys. The hairy cow sorted the big toys, the big pig sorted the small toys, and the small duck sorted the tiny toys. They were having a lot of fun.
But then, something unexpected happened. A big wind came and blew all the toys away! The animals were sad. But then, they saw a big rainbow in the sky. The wind had made a beautiful rainbow with their toys. The animals were happy again and they all played under the rainbow.

Ben and Lily were twins who liked to play outside. One day, they saw a big puddle of mud near their house. They wanted to jump in it and make a splash. But their mom said, "No, you can't do that. You will get your boots and clothes dirty. And you will catch a cold. Go and play with your toys instead."
Ben and Lily were sad. They thought their mom was mean. They waited until their mom was busy in the kitchen. Then they sneaked out of the house and ran to the puddle. They put on their boots and jumped in the mud. They laughed and shouted, "This is fun! This is fun!"
But soon they felt cold and wet. The mud was sticky and smelly. It got on their faces and hair. It stained their clothes and socks. Their boots were heavy and full of mud. They did not feel like laughing anymore. They felt sorry and scared. They wanted to go back to their mom.
They ran to the house and knocked on the door. Their mom opened it and saw them. She was angry and shocked. She said, "What have you done? Look at you! You are filthy and messy. You disobeyed me. You are in big trouble."
Ben and Lily started to cry. They said, "We are sorry, mom. We regret jumping in the mud. We thought it would be fun, but it was not. We made a mistake. Please forgive us."
Their mom sighed and hugged them. She said, "I love you, but you have to listen to me. I know what is good for you. You have to clean up and change your clothes. And you have to throw away your boots. They are ruined."
Ben and Lily nodded and said, "Yes, mom. We will do that. We will not jump in the mud again. We will be good."
Their mom smiled and said, "You are generous kids. You share and help each other. You make me happy. But you also have to be careful and smart. You have to learn from your mistakes. Now go and wash up. I will make you some hot chocolate."
Ben and Lily said, "Thank you, mom. You are the best. We love you." They went to the bathroom and washed up. They threw away their boots and changed their clothes. They drank the hot chocolate and felt warm and cozy. They played with their toys and forgot about the mud. They were happy again.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lucy. She was nervous because she had never made toast before. Her mom said it was easy, so Lucy wanted to try.
Lucy put the bread in the toaster and pushed the button. She waited and waited. Finally, the toast popped up! Lucy was so happy. But she did not want to eat the toast plain. She wanted to put something on it.
Lucy looked in the fridge and found some old jam. It was very old and had started to rot. Lucy's mom saw the jam and said, "No, Lucy, we can't use that. It's bad." Lucy was sad, but her mom found some new, yummy jam for her toast. Lucy ate her toast and it was so good. She was not nervous anymore.

One day, a boy named Tim went to play outside. He saw his neighbor, a tidy girl named Sue. Sue was playing with a big red ball. Tim wanted to play with Sue and the ball.
"Can I play with you?" Tim asked Sue. Sue said yes, and they started to play. They threw the ball back and forth, laughing and having fun. But then, something unexpected happened. The ball hit a tree and split in two!
"Oh no! My ball!" said Sue, feeling sad. Tim wanted to help Sue feel better, so he had an idea. He went to his house and got a new ball. It was even bigger and more colorful.
"Here, we can play with this ball," Tim said, giving the new ball to Sue. Sue smiled, and they played together until the sun went down. From that day on, Tim and Sue became best friends.

Once upon a time, in a big forest, there was a deep pit. The pit was very filthy. It was full of mud, dirt, and smelly things. No one liked the pit because it was not nice.
One day, a small cat was walking in the forest. The cat did not see the pit and was about to enter it. But, a big bird saw the cat and wanted to help. The bird flew down and said, "Stop, cat! Don't enter the pit! It's filthy and you will get dirty."
The cat listened to the bird and did not enter the pit. The cat said, "Thank you, bird, for helping me." The bird was happy to help the cat. From that day, the cat and the bird became good friends. They played together in the forest and stayed away from the filthy pit.

Once upon a time, there was a gray cat. The cat liked to watch the town. Every day, the cat sat on a hill and watched the town below.
One day, while the cat was watching, it saw a big red ball. The red ball was rolling down the street. The cat was surprised. It had never seen a big red ball in the town before.
The cat ran down the hill to see the big red ball up close. But when the cat got close to the ball, it saw that it was not a ball at all! It was a big, round, red house. The cat was very happy to find a new friend, the red house, in the gray town.

Lily decided to scatter the balls all over the floor. She giggled as the balls bounced and rolled everywhere. She had so much fun chasing them and picking them up. After playing for a while, Lily felt thirsty and wanted a drink.
Lily went to the sink to get a cup of water. She carefully turned on the tap and filled her cup. She took a big sip and felt so refreshed. Then, she went back to play with her colorful balls, feeling happy and content.

One day, Tim asked his mom, "What is in the sky?" His mom said, "That is the universe. It has lots of stars and planets." Tim thought the universe was very big and pretty.
Tim asked more questions about the universe. He wanted to learn all about it. His mom and dad helped him find answers. They read books and looked at pictures. Tim was happy to learn about the universe.

Once upon a time, there was a big dog named Max. Max loved to lead his friends on a journey. One day, Max and his friends, a cat named Lily and a bird named Sam, went on a journey to find a big tree.
Max said, "We will stay safe if we stay together." Lily and Sam agreed. They walked and walked until they saw the big tree. Max, Lily, and Sam were very happy.
But then, they saw a big river. Max knew that he could swim, but he wasn't sure about Lily and Sam. Max said, "Let's find a safe way to cross the river." They looked and looked, and finally found a bridge. They crossed the bridge and reached the big tree. Max, Lily, and Sam were very happy that they had stayed safe on their journey. The moral of the story is to always stay together and help each other to stay safe.

Once upon a time, in a small village, there was an adorable little girl named Lily. Lily loved to play with her friends and look for shiny things. One day, she found a pretty gem while playing in the park. She was so happy and showed her friends her new treasure.
Lily's mommy told her to be careful with her gem, and to always say "please" when she wanted something. Lily promised to be good and always use her manners. She kept her gem in a special box in her room, and every night, she would look at it before going to bed.
One day, Lily's friend Timmy came over to play. Timmy saw the gem and wanted to hold it, but he didn't say "please." Lily remembered what her mommy said and told Timmy to say "please" first. Timmy said "please," and Lily let him hold her gem. They both smiled and played together, knowing that using good manners made them better friends.

Once upon a time, there was a brown dog. The brown dog liked to dig in the dirt. One day, the brown dog found a file in the dirt. He did not know what it was, but he thought it was a toy.
The brown dog played with the file. He would throw it in the air and catch it. One day, the brown dog left the file outside in the rain. The file got wet and started to rot.
The brown dog's friend, a little bird, saw the rotting file. The bird thought it was food. The bird ate the rotting file and got very sick. The brown dog was sad because he lost his toy and his friend was sick.

Sam and Lily were bored. They wanted to play, but it was raining outside. They looked around the house for something fun to do.
"Let's play hide and seek!" Sam said. "You count to ten and I will hide."
"Okay!" Lily said. She closed her eyes and started to count. "One, two, three..."
Sam ran to the kitchen. He saw an enormous cupboard. It was full of pots and pans and plates and cups. He thought it was a good place to hide. He opened the door and climbed inside. He moved some things to make room for himself. He closed the door and waited for Lily to find him.
Lily finished counting. "Ten! Ready or not, here I come!" She opened her eyes and looked around. She went to the living room, the bedroom, the bathroom, but she could not find Sam. She wondered where he was.
She went to the kitchen. She saw the enormous cupboard. She thought maybe Sam was hiding there. She opened the door and saw Sam. He was lying on the floor. He was not moving. He was not breathing. He had a big cut on his head. He had hit it on a sharp knife when he climbed inside. He was bleeding a lot.
"Sam! Sam!" Lily screamed. She ran to him and tried to wake him up. But he did not wake up. He was dead.
Lily cried and cried. She did not know what to do. She wished she had never played hide and seek. She wished she had never opened the cupboard. She wished Sam was still alive. She was very sad and scared. She was alone.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim was a bit frightened of the dark. One night, he saw a big shadow on his bedroom wall. Tim thought it was a monster, so he hid under his bed.
His mom came into the room and said, "Tim, don't be frightened. It's just a shadow." She gave Tim a small camera and told him to take a picture of the shadow. "When you see the picture, you'll know it's not a monster," she said.
Tim took a picture with the camera. When he looked at the picture, he saw that the shadow was just a chair with a coat on it. Tim felt better and wasn't frightened anymore. He thanked his mom for the camera and went to sleep with a big smile on his face.

One day, a man named Tom went for a drive in his red car. He was very patient as he drove slowly down the road. He saw a big tree and a blue bird. The sun was shining, and Tom was happy.
Tom had a jar in his car. The jar was full of sweet candy. He wanted to give the candy to his friend, Sam. Sam lived in a small house near the park. Tom liked to visit Sam and play with him.
When Tom got to Sam's house, he opened the jar to give Sam the candy. But, surprise! The jar was full of frogs, not candy! Tom and Sam laughed and laughed. The frogs jumped out of the jar and went to play in the park. Tom and Sam had fun watching the frogs and playing together.

Lila and Tom were playing in the forest. They had a bow and some arrows that they made from sticks and string. They liked to pretend they were mighty hunters.
"Look, a deer!" Tom said, pointing to a brown shape behind a bush. He took an arrow and aimed at it.
"No, don't!" Lila said. "It is not a real deer. It is a toy deer. It belongs to Sam. He will be sad if you tear it."
But Tom did not listen. He let go of the string and the arrow flew through the air. It hit the toy deer and made a hole in it.
"Oh no!" Lila said. "You tore Sam's toy deer. He will be very angry. He will not play with us anymore."
Tom felt bad. He did not mean to tear the toy deer. He ran to the bush and picked up the arrow and the toy deer. He saw the hole and the stuffing coming out.
"I'm sorry, I'm sorry," he said. "I did not know it was a toy deer. I thought it was a real deer. I wanted to be a mighty hunter."
Lila followed him. She saw the toy deer and the arrow. She felt sorry for Tom and Sam.
"Maybe we can fix it," she said. "Maybe we can sew the hole and put the stuffing back in. Maybe Sam will not be so angry if we say we are sorry and give him the toy deer back."
Tom nodded. He liked Lila's idea. He hoped Sam would forgive him. He learned a lesson. He learned to be careful with his arrows and not to tear other people's toys. He learned that being a mighty hunter was not as important as being a good friend.

Once upon a time, there was a bright sun in the sky. It made everything look happy. In a small house, there lived a little girl named Amy. She had a toy called Top. Top was her best friend. They played together every day.
One day, Amy had an idea. She wanted to serve tea to Top. Amy said, "Top, would you like some tea?" Top didn't say anything because it was a toy, but Amy knew Top would love it. She set up a small table with cups and a teapot. She poured pretend tea into the cups.
Amy and Top had a great tea party. They laughed and played together. The bright sun smiled down on them, making it a perfect day.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl called Millie. She was always very busy playing outside. One day, she heard a loud thunder rolling in. Millie looked up in the sky and was amazed by the bright flashes of lightning.
"What is it, mummy?" Millie asked.
Mummy smiled, "That's the sound of thunder and the light we can see is the lightning."
"Can I go and see it?" Millie pleaded, jumping up and down.
"Okay, but be careful," mummy allowed.
Millie scooted off to the garden and watched the beautiful lightening in awe. She found the thunder and lightening so exciting, she wanted to show everyone.
Mummy called out, "It's time to come inside now Millie!"
Millie was sad to leave the lightning, but she was a good girl and said goodbye before coming back into the house.
The next day, Millie was very busy helping mummy. But when the powerful thunder came again, Mama allowed Millie to go outside for a few minutes and she joyfully watched the lightning, feeling very lucky.

One day, a lovely girl named Mia went to the park with her mom. Mia saw a big tree with small purple things on it. Mia asked her mom, "What are those?" Her mom said, "They are prunes, Mia."
Mia wanted to eat a prune, but they were too high up in the tree. So, Mia's mom found a long stick and got one for her. Mia took a bite and said, "Yum!" But then, Mia's face felt sticky. She did not like that. Mia's mom had a cloth and she used it to wipe Mia's face. Mia was happy again.
Just then, a little bird flew down from the tree. The bird took a prune and dropped it in Mia's hand. Mia was so surprised! She did not know birds could do that. Mia shared the prune with the little bird, and they became friends. From that day on, Mia always looked for her bird friend when she went to the park.

One day, a little fish named Fin was swimming in the sea. He loved to dive deep and play with his friends. The sun was shining, and the water was warm.
Fin saw a big fish named Bob. Bob was sad. Fin asked, "Why are you sad, Bob?" Bob said, "I want butter for my bread, but I can't find any in the sea." Fin had an idea. "I will help you find butter, Bob!" he said.
Fin and Bob swam and swam. They asked other fish if they had seen butter. One fish said, "I saw butter on the land!" Fin and Bob went to the land. They found a little jar of butter. They were very happy. They took the butter back to the sea and had a big feast with all their friends.

Once upon a time, there was a great big bear named Ben. He had a soft, warm coat that he loved to wear. One day, Ben went for a walk in the woods. He felt so happy and warm in his coat.
As Ben walked, he saw a little rabbit crying. Ben wanted to comfort the rabbit, so he asked, "Why are you crying, little rabbit?" The rabbit said, "I am cold and lost." Ben knew what to do. He took off his coat and gave it to the rabbit. The rabbit felt warm and stopped crying.
But then, something unexpected happened. The rabbit took off the coat and said, "Surprise! I am not a rabbit, I am a fairy!" She waved her magic wand, and Ben's coat turned into a big, warm house. Ben was so happy to have a new house. He thanked the fairy and they became great friends.

As the days went by, the shoot grew bigger and stronger. One day, a beautiful flower bloomed on the plant. It had soft petals and smooth skin. Lily loved her new flower very much. She knew that with love and care, even the humblest seed could bloom into something beautiful.

One day, Lily finds a new toy in her room. It is a bird that can talk. Lily presses a button and the bird says "Hello, I am Polly". Lily is happy and surprised. She calls Ben to show him her new toy.
"Look, Ben, this is Polly. She can talk!" Lily says.
"Wow, Polly is cool. Can I play with her?" Ben asks.
"Sure, Ben. Press the button and she will talk to you." Lily says.
Ben presses the button and the bird says "Hello, I am Polly". Ben smiles and says "Hello, Polly, I am Ben". But the bird does not answer. Ben presses the button again and the bird says "Hello, I am Polly". Ben is confused.
"Why does Polly only say the same thing? Why does she not answer me?" Ben asks.
Lily laughs and says "Polly is not a real bird, Ben. She is a toy bird. She can only repeat what we say. She does not understand us."
"Oh, I see. Polly is a toy bird. She can only repeat what we say. She does not understand us." Ben says.
The bird says "She does not understand us."
Lily and Ben laugh and play with Polly. They say funny things and make her repeat them. They have fun with their new toy.

Tim sat down next to Lily and together, they observed the birds. They were very quiet and listened to the birds sing. They watched the birds fly high in the sky and land in the trees.

Once upon a time, there was a woman who lived in a small house. She had a big chair that she liked to rest in. She would sit in the chair every day because it was easy to rest there. She had a little dog who liked to play with a red ball.
One day, the woman could not find her red ball. She looked and looked but the ball was not there. The little dog was sad. The woman thought, "Maybe the ball is under the chair." She had a feeling it might be there.
The woman moved the big chair to look for the red ball. And there it was! The little dog was very happy. The woman was happy too. She put the chair back and they both rested in their favorite spots. The woman knew that her big chair was not just easy to rest in, but also a good place to find lost things.

Once upon a time, there was a calm little dog named Max. Max loved to play in the sun and listen with his big ears. One day, Max went to the park with his best friend, a girl named Lily.
At the park, Max and Lily saw a big tree. Under the tree, they found a small jug. Max put his ear close to the jug and heard a soft sound. Lily had an idea. She took the jug and started to pour water from it onto the flowers near the tree.
As they poured the water, the flowers began to grow and dance. Max and Lily laughed and clapped their hands. They had so much fun pouring water on the flowers and watching them grow. From that day on, Max and Lily always played with the magic jug in the park, making the flowers grow and dance. And Max's big ears always listened to the soft sound of the water pouring from the jug.

As Will went that way, he found a big tree with lots of fruits. He was very happy and started to eat the fruits. Suddenly, a big wind came and blew Will away. Will was scared and wished he had listened to the sign. His friends found him and helped him get back home.
From that day, Will always listened to signs and did not go where he should not. He learned that signs are there to help and keep everyone safe.

Paul ran to the store and bought an island model with a tiny pool and a tiny tree. He sold it to his neighbour and made enough money to buy an island.
The next day, Paul took his boat and sailed out to the open sea. After a few hours, he found a beautiful island. He sailed around it and saw that the island was very deep.
Paul was so excited - he had finally bought his own island! He jumped off the boat, ran up the beach and claimed his new island as his own.
From that day on, Paul was the proud owner of his very own island. He never stopped dreaming of all the things he could do with his island.

One day, Melissa noticed that Louise had more wool on her back than usual. "Louise you have so much wool on your back now! What must I do to take care of it?", Melissa asked.

Melissa was so proud of Louise and the way she remembered to take care of her own wool. She gave Louise a big hug and promised to always take care of Louise.
The moral of the story is that taking care of yourself is important. Even when things may seem difficult, it's important to remember to take care and look after yourself.

Once upon a time, there was a tall tree in a big park. The tree was very old and wise. It had seen many things in its long life. The tree had many friends, like the birds, the squirrels, and the little bugs that lived in the ground.

The tree was surprised that Tim could hear it, but it was happy to have a new friend. So the tree said, "Hello, Tim! Yes, I can speak."
Tim and the tall tree talked for a long time. They talked about the birds that lived in the tree's branches and the bugs that lived in the ground. Tim told the tree about his family and his friends. The tree told Tim about all the things it had seen in the park.
As the sun began to set, Tim knew it was time to go home. He said to the tree, "I have to go now, tall tree. But I will come back to see you soon!" The tree was happy to have a new friend and said, "Goodbye, Tim! I will be here waiting for you."

Ben nodded. He liked symbols too. He said, "OK, let's make a symbol. What symbol do you want to make?"
Lily thought for a moment. She said, "I want to make a star. A star means wish. I wish for a new doll."
Ben smiled. He said, "I want to make a circle. A circle means friend. You are my friend."
They started to pick up the leaves and arrange them on the ground. They made a star and a circle with the leaves. They were very proud of their symbols.
A lady walked by and saw their symbols. She was charming. She had a nice dress and a big hat. She said to Lily and Ben, "What a lovely symbol you have made. Can I take a picture of it?"
Lily and Ben looked at each other. They were shy. They did not know the lady. But she was charming and kind. They decided to accept her request. They said, "Yes, you can take a picture. But please don't touch our symbol. We worked hard on it."
The lady nodded. She took out her phone and snapped a picture of the star and the circle. She said, "Thank you, Lily and Ben. You are very creative and smart. I hope your wishes and friendship come true."
She waved goodbye and walked away. Lily and Ben waved back. They were happy. They hugged each other and said, "We are the best friends ever." They continued to play with their symbol until it was time to go home.

The sun was bright in the sky as Auntie Stacy and her nephew Joey went for a walk. Joey liked to walk fast so Auntie Stacy asked him, “ Joey, can you slow down? I don't want you to walk too fast.”
Joey replied, “But why, Auntie Stacy? I like to move fast.”
Auntie Stacy smiled and said, “I know it's fun to run and play, but you know it's also important to move slowly and pay attention to the world around you. That way you can stay clean and safe.”
Joey nodded and agreed, “Yes, that makes sense.”
Auntie Stacy went on, “When you walk too fast, it's called speed. And speed can prevent you from seeing things. That can be bad because then you can miss important things, like a dirt path or an unsafe puddle.”
Joey was following the conversation and said, “I'll be careful and move slow. That way I can stay clean and safe.”
Auntie Stacy was proud of him and said, “That's a great idea! Let's keep walking, but not too fast.”
Joey smiled and together they walked at a slow, clean pace.
