From that day on, Lucy and Fluffy learned that sharing was a good thing. The moral of the story is that sharing with others makes everyone happy.

Once upon a time, in a charming little town, there lived a barber named Sam. Sam loved to twist hair into fun shapes for the kids who came to his shop. One day, a little girl named Lily came to visit Sam.
"Hi, Sam!" said Lily. "Can you twist my hair into a pretty shape?"
"Of course, Lily!" said Sam. He twisted Lily's hair into a beautiful flower. All the kids in the town loved Sam's fun hair twists.
One day, a big wind came and blew all the hair twists away. The kids were sad, but Sam had an idea. He invited all the kids back to his shop and twisted their hair into new, fun shapes. The kids were happy again, and they all loved their charming barber, Sam.

Once upon a time, there was a ball that loved to roll. One day, it rolled into a kitchen. In the kitchen, it saw a big cold box called a freezer. The ball wanted to see what was inside.
The ball rolled up to the freezer and pushed the door open. Inside, it found a tasty treat. The treat was cold and sweet. The ball wanted to share the treat with its friends.
The ball rolled outside with the tasty treat. It found its friends and they all shared the cold, sweet treat. They laughed and played, enjoying the treat from the freezer. They all had a great day together.

Once upon a time, there was an adorable little cat named Kitty. Kitty loved to play with water. One day, Kitty found a faucet in the kitchen. She jumped up and turned it on. Water came out and Kitty was very happy.
Kitty played with the water for a long time. But soon, the water started to fill up the kitchen. Kitty began to panic. She did not know how to turn off the faucet. The water kept coming out and the kitchen was getting very wet.
Just then, Kitty's friend, a clever dog named Buddy, came in. He saw the water and knew what to do. Buddy turned off the faucet and the water stopped. Kitty was so thankful for Buddy's help. The unexpected twist was that Kitty and Buddy became best friends, even though cats and dogs usually don't get along. And they played together happily ever after.

One day, a little girl named Mia was playing by the sea. She saw a big octopus. The octopus was sad. Mia asked, "Why are you sad, Mr. Octopus?"
The octopus said, "I am sad because I hear that people think I am delicious. I am scared they will eat me." Mia wanted to help the octopus. She had an idea.
Mia told the octopus, "Let's make a big cake. People will think the cake is delicious and not want to eat you." The octopus liked the idea. They made a big, yummy cake.
People came and saw the cake. They ate the cake and said, "This cake is so delicious!" The octopus was happy because people did not want to eat him. Mia and the octopus became best friends.

One day, a little girl named Lily was very happy. She wanted to watch a film on the big TV. It was a film about a funny dog. Lily loved the funny dog and would laugh and clap her hands when he did tricks.
Lily's mom said, "Before you watch the film, you have to study your ABCs." Lily did not want to study, but she knew she had to. So she opened her book and started to study. She looked at the letters and said them out loud.
As Lily was studying, something unexpected happened. The funny dog from the film jumped out of the TV! The dog said, "Hi Lily, I will help you study your ABCs!" Lily was so surprised and happy. The funny dog and Lily studied together, and they had lots of fun. After they finished studying, they watched the film together and laughed at all the funny tricks.

Once upon a time, in a small house, there was a noisy tap. It would make loud sounds all day and all night. The people who lived in the house did not like the noisy tap, but they did not know how to fix it.
One day, a little girl named Lucy came to visit the house. She heard the noisy tap and thought it was funny. Lucy wanted to make the tap quiet, so she tried to turn it off. But instead of turning it off, she made the tap louder! Lucy felt embarrassed because she wanted to help, not make it worse.
Then, something unexpected happened. A little bird flew in through the window and landed on the noisy tap. The bird started to sing, and its song was so beautiful that the tap stopped making noise. Everyone in the house was happy and thanked the little bird. The bird had fixed the noisy tap, and now the house was quiet and peaceful.

Once upon a time, there was a lazy dog named Spot. Spot lived in a little house with a leak in the roof. When it rained, water would drip inside the house.
One day, Spot saw a sign that said, "Fix your leak." Spot thought, "I don't want water in my house anymore." So, he decided to fix the leak.
Spot worked hard to fix the leak in his house. When he was done, the rain stayed outside. Spot was happy and not lazy anymore. He learned that fixing things can be fun.

One day, a little girl named Lily went to the park. She saw her friend, Tim, sitting on a bench. Tim had a bandage on his leg. Lily walked over to him and noticed that the bandage was very fancy. It had bright colors and fun shapes on it.
"Tim, where did you get that fancy bandage?" Lily asked. Tim smiled and said, "My mom put it on my leg after I fell down. It makes me feel better."
Lily thought that was a good idea. She wanted to help Tim feel even better. So, they played together in the park all day long. They went on the swings, down the slide, and even played in the sandbox. Tim forgot all about his fancy bandage and had lots of fun with Lily.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim loved to play hockey. He had a white hockey stick that he took with him everywhere. Tim liked to listen to his dad tell stories about when he played hockey too.
One day, Tim wanted to play hockey with his friends. He took his white hockey stick and went outside. The sun was shining and the sky was blue. Tim and his friends had lots of fun playing hockey together.
But then, a big dog came running into the yard. The dog saw Tim's white hockey stick and thought it was a toy. The dog grabbed the hockey stick and ran away. Tim was very sad. He tried to get his hockey stick back, but the dog was too fast. Tim went home with no hockey stick and a bad ending to his day.

Once upon a time, there was a big fish named Fin. Fin had a wide tail that helped him move fast in the water. He loved to swim and play with his friends all day. One day, Fin saw a little fish named Tiny who was sad. Tiny had a small tail and could not move as fast as the others.
Fin wanted to help Tiny, so he decided to teach him how to move faster in the water. They practiced every day, and Tiny got better and better. Tiny was happy because he could now play with his friends and keep up with them.
The other fish saw how Fin helped Tiny and they learned a lesson. They learned that it is good to help others who need it. Fin and Tiny became best friends, and all the fish in the sea lived happily ever after. The moral of the story is to always help others and be kind to your friends.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lily. She had a pretty bicycle that she loved to ride. One day, she went to the park with her bicycle to play.
While playing, Lily saw a hidden path. She was curious and decided to follow it. The path led to a beautiful garden with flowers and butterflies. In the garden, she saw a frog sitting on a rock.
Lily remembered a story her mom told her about a frog turning into a prince when kissed. She thought, "Maybe if I kiss this frog, something magical will happen!" So, she closed her eyes and gave the frog a big kiss. But instead of turning into a prince, the frog turned into a big, shiny balloon! Lily was surprised but happy. She took the balloon, rode her bicycle back home, and told her mom about the unexpected adventure.

Once upon a time, in a small house, there was a colorful blanket. This blanket was very special because it could talk. One day, a needle came to visit the blanket.
"Hello, blanket," said the needle. "Can I lie on you for a bit?"
"Of course, needle," said the blanket. "I like it when friends lie on me."
So the needle lay on the colorful blanket. They talked and laughed all day. The needle liked how soft the blanket was, and the blanket liked how shiny the needle was. They were very happy together.

One day, a big dog named Sam went to the park. He saw a grill. Sam was very hungry. He wanted to eat something from the grill. But Sam was deaf, so he could not hear very well.
A man was cooking on the grill. The man was nice. He saw Sam and wanted to help him. The man cooked a big hot dog for Sam. Sam was very happy. He could not hear the man, but he could see the hot dog.
Then, something unexpected happened. A bird flew down and took the hot dog from the grill! Sam was sad. But the nice man cooked another hot dog for Sam. This time, Sam ate the hot dog very fast. The bird could not take it. Sam and the man were happy.

Once upon a time, there was a tiny mouse who lived in a big house. The mouse was very brave and loved to explore the house. One day, the mouse heard something strange coming from the kitchen. He carefully peeked into the room and saw a big monster! The monster was big and had lots of teeth - he was ready to bite! But the mouse was too quick for him. He quickly ran away and hid in a cupboard.
The monster was so angry and made a loud noise. He shouted in an order, "I want that mouse now!" He began to search the house, looking for the brave mouse. The mouse heard the order and was very scared. He didn't know what to do.
Suddenly, the mouse remembered something he had heard before - if you stay very still, the monster can't find you! So the mouse stayed very still, but he began to feel dizzy. The monster searched and searched, but he never found the mouse.
After a while, the monster stopped searching and gave up. The mouse was so relieved, and he knew he was safe. He was no longer scared - the brave mouse had won!

Once upon a time, there was a wise old man who lived in a small house. He loved to help people in his town. One day, he found an ashtray on the ground. He knew that it was not good for kids to play with it.
He saw a little boy playing near the ashtray. The wise old man went to the boy and said, "Please do not play with the ashtray. It is not safe for you." The boy listened to the wise old man and went to play with his toys instead.
The boy's mom saw what happened and thanked the wise old man. She said, "Thank you for helping my son. You are very wise and we love you." From that day on, the little boy always listened to the wise old man and stayed away from things that were not safe. The moral of the story is to listen to wise people and stay away from things that can harm you.

Lily and Ben were hungry. They wanted to eat steak for dinner. But steak was too expensive. Mom and Dad did not have much money.
They saw a sign in the shop. It said: "Cheap steak. Only five coins." They ran to the shop. They saw a big pile of steak. It looked good. They wanted to buy some.
But the shopkeeper was mean. He said: "This steak is not for you. It is for my dog. You can have some bread and cheese. That is cheap too."
Lily and Ben did not like bread and cheese. They liked steak. They had an idea. They whispered to each other. They said: "Let's wait until the shopkeeper is busy. Then we can take some steak and run away."
They waited for a long time. They saw the shopkeeper talking to a lady. He did not look at the steak. Lily and Ben sneaked to the pile. They grabbed some steak. They ran out of the shop.
They were happy. They had steak for dinner. But the steak was not good. It was hard and chewy. It tasted bad. They did not like it.
They learned a lesson. They said: "Cheap steak is not good steak. It is for dogs. We should not take things that are not ours. We are sorry."
They went back to the shop. They gave back the steak. They said sorry to the shopkeeper. He was angry. He said: "You are naughty. You should not steal. You should work hard and save money. Then you can buy good steak."
Lily and Ben felt bad. They said: "We are sorry. We will work hard and save money. We will not steal again."
The shopkeeper saw that they were sorry. He said: "OK. I forgive you. But you have to do something for me. You have to help me in the shop. You have to sweep the floor and stack the shelves. Then I will give you some bread and cheese. And maybe some steak. But only good steak. Not cheap steak."
Lily and Ben agreed. They helped the shopkeeper. They ate bread and cheese. And sometimes steak. But only good steak. They liked it. They were happy.

Once upon a time, there was a curious man. He had a wife. They lived in a small house near a big tree.
One day, the wife was in the garden. She saw a big cat. The cat wanted to eat her. She was scared and called for her husband.
The curious man ran to save his wife. He chased the big cat away. They were both happy and safe. They hugged and went back to their small house. And they lived happily ever after.

Lila and Sam were playing with their blocks. They liked to make different patterns with the colors and shapes. Lila made a delicate tower with small blocks. Sam made a big wall with large blocks.
"Look at my tower!" Lila said. "It is so pretty and tall!"
"Look at my wall!" Sam said. "It is so strong and wide!"
They were happy with their patterns. But then, a gust of wind came through the window. It blew Lila's tower down. The blocks scattered on the floor.
"Oh no!" Lila cried. "My tower is gone! It vanished!"
Sam saw what happened. He felt sorry for Lila. He wanted to help her.
"Don't cry, Lila," he said. "We can make another tower. Or we can make something else. We have many blocks."
He picked up some blocks and gave them to Lila. Lila smiled. She wiped her tears.
"Thank you, Sam," she said. "You are a good friend. Let's make a pattern together."
They hugged and started to build again. They made a new pattern with their blocks. It was colorful and fun. They were happy again. The wind did not bother them anymore.

One day, Tom and Sue went to the shore. They saw the big, open water. They were very happy.
Tom said, "Sue, let's go close to the water!" Sue said, "Yes, let's go!" They ran to the water and played in the sand.
Then, something unexpected happened. A big fish jumped out of the water! It said, "Hi, kids!" Tom and Sue were very surprised. They had fun with the big fish and played all day at the shore.

Once upon a time, in a small cheerful town, there lived a happy bunny named Bob. Bob loved to eat carrots. One day, while hopping around, he found a big carrot in the ground. But, the carrot was stuck!
Bob tried to pull the carrot out, but he couldn't do it alone. He asked his friend, a helpful bird named Sally, to help him. "Sally, can you help me pull this carrot out, please?" Bob asked.
Sally flew down and said, "Sure, Bob! Let's pull it out together!" They both pulled and pulled. With their teamwork, they got the big carrot out of the ground. Bob and Sally were so happy. They shared the carrot and had a great snack together.

Once upon a time, a little girl named Sue had a small plant. She loved her plant so much. Every day, she gave it water and lots of love. The plant was very happy with Sue.
One day, the plant started to grow. It grew and grew, until it was very high. Sue was so happy to see her plant grow so tall. She said, "Thank you, plant, for growing so big and strong."
Sue and her high plant played together every day. They had so much fun in the sun. And every night, Sue would thank her plant for being her friend. They were best friends forever.

Lily and Anna were having a tea party with their dolls. They poured water from a pink pot into tiny cups and pretended it was tea. They gave their dolls names and voices and made them talk to each other.
"Hello, Miss Daisy, how are you today?" Lily said in a high-pitched voice, holding her doll with curly hair.
"I'm very well, thank you, Miss Rose. You have such a lovely dress," Anna said in a low voice, holding her doll with a blue dress.
They giggled and sipped their water. They felt very grown-up and happy.
But then, Lily's big brother Tom came into the room. He was six years old and thought he was very smart. He saw the girls playing with their dolls and water and laughed.
"What are you doing, you silly girls? That's not tea, that's water. And those are not real dolls, they are just toys. You are so ignorant, you don't know anything," he said in a mean voice.
He grabbed the pink pot and poured the water over Lily's head. Lily gasped and felt the water dripping down her face and clothes. She felt very cold and wet and sad.
"Tom, stop it! That's not nice, that's mean. You are so rude, you don't care about anyone," Anna said in an angry voice.
She stood up and pushed Tom away. She hugged Lily and tried to comfort her.
"Are you okay, Lily? Don't listen to him, he's just a bully. You are not ignorant, you are smart and creative. And you are not silly, you are fun and sweet. And we are not playing with water and toys, we are having a tea party with our dolls. And we are having a lot of fun," she said in a kind voice.
Lily nodded and smiled. She felt a little better. She wiped her face and hugged Anna back.
"Thank you, Anna. You are a good friend. And you are right, we are having a lot of fun. And he is a bully. And he is the ignorant one, he doesn't know how to play or have fun. And he is the silly one, he just embarrassed himself," she said in a brave voice.
They looked at Tom, who was standing there with a red face and a wet shirt. He looked very silly and embarrassed. He realized he had made a mistake and felt sorry.
"I'm sorry, Lily. I'm sorry, Anna. I was just joking, I didn't mean to hurt you. Can I join your tea party?" he said in a small voice.
Lily and Anna looked at each other and nodded. They decided to forgive him and let him play with them. They gave him a doll and a cup and taught him how to have a tea party. He learned how to be nice and have fun. They all became friends and had a lot of fun.

Once upon a time, in a lively garden, there was a big peach tree. The tree had many sweet peaches on it. The peaches were very yummy.
One day, a little bird saw the peach tree. The bird wanted to eat a peach. But the bird was too small to get a peach. The bird was sad.
Then, a kind squirrel came to save the day. The squirrel climbed the tree and got a peach for the bird. The bird was very happy. They both ate the peach and played together in the lively garden.

Once upon a time, there was a house. It was the original house. Every night, a family lived in it. They would pray to keep it safe and happy. One day, something bad happened. Fire spread around the house! Everyone had to leave quickly. They tried to pray, but the fire just kept getting bigger. Nothing could be done. In the end, the house was no more. It was sad, but that was the bad ending. The family had to find a new place to live.

The dolphin helped Tim surf on the waves, and they all had a great time. The unexpected visit from the dolphin made Tim's birthday even more special. Everyone laughed and played together, making it a day they would never forget.

Once upon a time, there was a poor little dog named Max. Max was very hungry, so he went to the park to find some food. He saw a big sandwich on a bench. Max was so happy and ran to eat it.
As Max got close, a big bird flew down and tried to scare him away. The bird wanted the sandwich, too! Max was very scared, but he really wanted that sandwich. He thought of a plan to get the sandwich back.
Max barked very loud at the bird, and the bird got scared. The bird dropped the sandwich and flew away. Max was so happy to have the sandwich again. But then, something unexpected happened. A little girl came to the park and saw Max. She was sad and hungry too. Max decided to share his sandwich with the girl. They both ate and were not hungry anymore. They became best friends and were never poor or scared again.

Sara was very tired after a long day of playing with her toys and friends. She wanted to go to bed and have sweet dreams. But she could not relax. She was thinking about the big test she had to take tomorrow at school. She wanted to do well and make her parents proud.
She saw her teddy bear on the pillow next to her. She hugged him and whispered, "Teddy, I'm scared. What if I don't know the answers? What if I fail?"
Teddy did not say anything, but he smiled and cuddled with Sara. He made her feel warm and safe. He reminded her of all the things she had learned and practiced with her teacher and her mom. He told her, "Sara, you are smart and brave. You can do anything you set your mind to. You will be successful. I believe in you."
Sara felt better. She thanked Teddy and gave him a kiss. She closed her eyes and took a deep breath. She relaxed and fell asleep. She dreamed of getting a gold star on her test and seeing her parents smile. She was happy.

Once upon a time there was a 3 year old girl. She loved colouring and drawing. One day, she asked her mummy if she could print her picture. Her mummy said yes and she went to get the special printing machine.
The girl was so excited and quickly started to print her picture. Suddenly, the printing machine ran out of juice and stopped working. The girl was very confused and didn't know what to do.
She went to her mummy and asked: "Mummy, why did it stop printing? What can I do?". Her mummy replied: "Don't worry sweetheart. All you have to do is put more juice in the machine and it will start printing again".
So the 3 year old girl went back to the printing machine and put more juice in it. Then, her picture started printing and she was very happy. She showed her mummy the printed picture and was so proud of her work.

One day, a colorful bird wanted to soar high in the sky. It was her birthday, and she wanted to fly higher than ever before. She flapped her wings and went up, up, up.
But then, she met a big cloud who said, "You can't fly here. This is my space." The bird was sad and didn't know what to do. She really wanted to soar on her birthday.
The bird had an idea. She asked the cloud, "Can I please fly here just for today? It's my birthday." The cloud thought about it and said, "Okay, just for today." So the bird soared high in the sky, happy on her birthday.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Anna. She was walking in the park one day and saw a big rock. She wanted to mix it with something, so she went and got a bucket of water. She mixed the water and the rock together and it made something really wet and slippery. She giggled and watched the rock move around in the water. Soon, it was all mixed together.
Then, she scooped up some of the wet mixture and spread it around until it was all over the ground. Anna mixed and mixed and mixed until the whole park looked wet and shiny. She was so proud of what she had created and danced around in the wet mix.
After she was done, Anna said goodbye to her wet creation and went home. From that day on, Anna always thought that mixing wet rocks was the most fun thing she had ever done.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lily. She liked to sort her toys and look at flowers. One day, she went to the park with her mom. They saw many pretty flowers. Lily was very happy.
In the park, Lily saw a dull flower. It was not like the other flowers. She felt sad for the dull flower. She wanted to help it. Lily asked her mom, "Can we help the dull flower?"
Her mom said, "Yes, let's sort the flowers." They took the dull flower and put it with other pretty flowers. The sun shined on the dull flower. It started to look better. Soon, the dull flower was pretty too. Lily and her mom were happy. They went home with smiles on their faces.

Once upon a time, there was a pirate. He had a big hat and a big ship. The pirate liked to dance.
One day, the pirate had a big party on his ship. All the pirates came to dance. They had so much fun. The pirate danced very well. He was so graceful.
All the pirates watched him dance. They were so happy. They clapped for him. They all said "applaud" and laughed. The pirate was the best dancer. And they all lived happily ever after.

One day, Jack and his Grandma took a walk in the park. Grandma smiled as Jack ran around, searching for something to do. On top of the hill, they saw a large structure that was made of tall wooden logs. Jack wanted to go play on it, but Grandma allowed him to go only after she checked it out.
The structure was very tall and noisy. When Grandma put her hand on the logs, they shifted and creaked. This made Jack so excited, but Grandma told him it was too risky to climb on. Jack begged and pleaded until finally, Grandma allowed him to go on it.
Jack rushed up the logs and started playing, but soon the logs started to creak and crack even more than before. Grandma looked worried, so Jack quickly jumped off and ran back to her.
Grandma hugged Jack and said, "You have to listen to your elders, Jack! This structure wouldn’t have been safe for you to play on. Next time, make sure to follow my instructions.”
Moral of the story: Always listen to your elders, because they are wiser and know what is best for you.

Once upon a time, in a small house, there lived a little girl named Lily. She loved to play outside in the snow. One day, she found a mitten on the ground. It was a big, red mitten. Lily was eager to find out who lost it.
Lily put on the mitten and went to find her friend, Bear. Bear was big and strong. He could protect Lily and help her look for the owner of the mitten. They walked and walked, asking all their friends if they lost a mitten.
Finally, they found a little bunny who was very cold. The bunny said, "I lost my mitten!" Lily gave the mitten to the bunny. Now, the bunny was warm again. Bear was happy to protect his friends, and Lily felt good for helping the bunny. They all played in the snow together, and had a fun day.

Once upon a time, there lived a strong man. He lived in a small village and had many friends. One day, he went out for a walk in the woods. He felt happy as the sun was shining brightly.
As he walked, he noticed something strange. A long shadow followed him wherever he went. The man stopped walking. He called out, "Who is there?".
The shadows said, "I am your shadow. I will follow you wherever you go". The man was frightened and said, "Go away! I don't want you following me ".
The shadows said, "If you don't let me follow you, I will make your life difficult". The man ignored the shadow and kept walking. The shadow followed him until he reached home.
When the man arrived home, his friends asked why he looked so scared. He told them about the shadow and how it followed him. His friends said, "The shadow will only get stronger if you ignore it. Don't let it follow you".
But it was too late. The shadow had become too strong for the man to resist. He was cursed by the shadow and it followed him wherever he went.
The moral of the story is to always listen to good advice. Don't let a bad situation become bigger than it already is.

Once upon a time, there was a clever seal named Sam. Sam lived in the ocean with his family. They had a lot of fun swimming and playing all day.
One day, Sam saw a little girl on the beach. She was skipping by the water. Sam wanted to play with her, so he swam close to the shore. He jumped out of the water and started to skip too.
The little girl saw Sam and laughed. They played and skipped together all day. Sam was so happy to make a new friend. From that day on, Sam and the little girl would skip and play together every time she came to the beach.

One day, a big, red ball and a small, blue ball went to play near a stream. The water in the stream was mild and clear. They were very happy.
The big, red ball said, "Let's spin and have fun!" So, they both started to spin around and around. They spun so fast that they made the water in the stream spin too.
As they spun, they saw a little fish in the stream. The fish said, "Hello, balls! Can I spin with you?" The big, red ball and the small, blue ball said, "Yes, come and spin with us!"
So, the little fish jumped out of the stream and started to spin with the balls. They all spun and played together near the mild stream. They had a fun day and became good friends.

One day, a young girl was playing with her friends at the park. She looked up and saw a very cheerful person coming towards her. He had a big smile and was carrying a big bag.
"Hello, I'm your uncle!" he said. "I heard you were at the park, so I thought I'd come to meet you."
The young girl's face lit up with joy. She ran towards him and embraced him tight.
"It's so nice to meet you, Uncle!" she said.
They went for a walk around the park and the young girl chatted with her uncle about her day. He was funny and kind and the young girl felt very happy.
When it was time to say goodbye, the young girl's uncle hugged her tight and gave her a big bag of funny toys.
"Take care, my dear," he said, and he was beaming with joy.
The young girl waved goodbye and ran off to play with her new toys, filled with happiness after meeting her uncle.

Tim, his mom, and his dad went outside to look at the comet. They saw it was getting bigger and bigger. They did not know why. They tried to add the comet to Tim's rock collection, but they could not.
The orange comet kept getting bigger and bigger. It was coming closer and closer to Tim's house. Then, the comet hit the ground near Tim's house. It was very loud and scary. Tim, his mom, and his dad were sad because their house was gone.

At the top, he met a girl named Sue. Sue said, "Be careful, it's slippery!" Tom smiled and said, "I will be okay."

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lily. She loved to play with her chalk. One day, she found a big box of chalk in many colors. Lily was so happy! She wanted to draw on the sidewalk right away. But her mom said, "Wait until the sun shines." Lily felt impatient.
The next day, the sun came out and shined brightly. Lily took her chalk and went outside. She drew a big rainbow on the sidewalk. The colors were so pretty! Lily's friends came to see her drawing. They all wanted to play with the chalk too. Lily shared her chalk with her friends. They all drew together, making the sidewalk colorful and fun.
Later, when it was time to go home, Lily looked at her rainbow drawing. She noticed that the sun made the colors shine even more. She remembered her mom telling her to wait for the sun. Lily was happy she listened to her mom. Now, she knew that the sun could make her drawings shine.

Once upon a time, there was a beautiful cow named Daisy. Daisy loved to dance in the green grass under the big, bright sun. One day, Daisy met a little girl named Lucy.
"Hi Daisy! Can I dance with you?" asked Lucy. Daisy nodded, and they danced together all day, laughing and having fun.
The next day, Lucy came back to dance with Daisy again. But this time, Daisy had a surprise. When they danced, Daisy made milk! Lucy was so happy, she clapped her hands and said, "Wow, Daisy! This is the best milk ever!" They danced and drank milk together, and they were the best of friends.

Once upon a time, there was an incredible octopus. He lived beneath the sea and was a very curious creature. He loved to explore and have adventures.
One day he decided to swim closer to the surface to see what he could find. Suddenly, he heard a voice that sounded like it was coming from the sky above.
The octopus was scared, he did not know what it was. After a few moments, he finally mustered up the courage to answer. He said hello and asked what was the voice was.
The voice was coming from a magical fairy who had been watching the octopus the whole time. She told him that she was from a secret world beneath the sun and wanted to show him something special. The octopus was so excited!
The fairy showed him a world full of colours and sounds he had never seen before. It was absolutely incredible! The octopus had so much fun and thanked the fairy for the amazing adventure. He promised her he would never forget it.

One day, a little boy named Tim saw a big leopard. The leopard was trying to climb a high tree. The leopard wanted to get the fruit at the top.
Tim wanted to help the leopard. He said, "Mr. Leopard, let me help you climb the tree." The leopard said, "No, Tim, it is too high for you. You may fall."
But Tim did not listen. He tried to climb the high tree too. He wanted to show the leopard that he was brave. But Tim fell from the tree and got hurt.
The leopard felt sad and said, "Tim, you should have listened to me. Now you are hurt." The moral of the story is to always listen to others when they try to help you.

One day, a pink cat and a blue dog saw a mirror in the park. The cat and dog did not like each other. They began to quarrel.
"Why do you want the mirror?" asked the pink cat. "I saw it first!"
"No, I saw it first!" said the blue dog. "I want to look at my face in the mirror."
The pink cat and the blue dog argued for a long time. They could not agree on who saw the mirror first.
Then, a wise bird came to the park. The bird saw the pink cat and the blue dog quarreling. "Stop fighting," said the bird. "Why don't you both use the mirror together?"
The pink cat and the blue dog thought about it. They decided to stop quarreling and share the mirror. They took turns looking at their faces in the mirror. Soon, they became friends and played together in the park.

Mum and Daddy had a big surprise for Lilly. They came back with bags and boxes full of stuff. Lilly was so excited that she jumped up and down. Mum put everything down in the living room. She said 'Lilly, let's unpack and see what we have here!'
Lilly's eyes lit up. There were pictures, books and toys! She was so happy she couldn't stop smiling. The first thing she pulled out was a strange looking man. He had a bald head and a big smile on his face. Lilly hugged him tightly.
Daddy said 'That's your new best friend, Baldy. He's here to keep you company!' Lilly couldn't believe her luck. She spent the rest of the day playing and laughing with Baldy. They had a great time exploring every corner of their home. Lilly was so glad that she had Baldy to keep her company.

Once upon a time, there was a mommy cat who had a little baby cat. They lived in a big, happy house. One day, mommy cat told baby cat it was time to bathe. Baby cat did not like to bathe, but mommy cat said it was important to be clean.
That day, mommy cat had to give a lecture to other cats. She was a successful cat and knew many things. Baby cat went with her and listened to her talk. While mommy cat was talking, baby cat saw a big bowl of water and decided to play in it.
Baby cat splashed and had fun in the water. When mommy cat finished her lecture, she saw baby cat all wet and clean. She was surprised and happy that baby cat learned to like bathing. Now, baby cat loved to bathe and be clean, and mommy cat was very proud.

Jolly is a young dog who likes to play with his friends. He likes to run, jump, bark and fetch. He is always happy and friendly.
One day, Jolly sees a new toy in his yard. It is a big ball with stripes and a rope. Jolly thinks it looks fun and runs to it. He tries to bite the rope and pull the ball.
But the ball is too heavy and hard. Jolly cannot move it or make it squeak. He tries harder and harder, but he only hurts his teeth and his mouth.
"Jolly, come here!" his owner calls. "That is not your toy. That is for the big cow. She likes to play with it. You have your own toys. Come and get them."
Jolly looks at his owner and then at the ball. He does not want to leave the ball. He wants to play with it. He thinks it is his toy. He refuses to go to his owner.
He growls and barks at the ball. He tries to scare it and make it move. He does not listen to his owner.
The big cow hears Jolly's noise. She comes to see what is happening. She sees Jolly and the ball. She is not happy. She wants her toy back. She moos and walks to Jolly.
Jolly sees the big cow. He is afraid. The big cow is much bigger and stronger than him. She can take the ball away from him. She can hurt him.
Jolly stops growling and barking. He lets go of the rope. He runs to his owner. He is sorry. He does not want to play with the ball anymore. He wants his own toys.
His owner hugs him and gives him a bone. He says, "Good boy, Jolly. You learned your lesson. The ball is not your toy. The ball is for the big cow. You have your own toys. They are better for you. They are fun and safe. Now, let's play with them."
Jolly wags his tail and licks his owner. He is happy again. He plays with his bone and his friends. He is a jolly young dog.

Once upon a time there was a furry little bunny. It was hopping around the garden when it saw something glinting in the sun. Excitedly, it hopped closer and saw it was a medal!
The bunny leaned in closer, excited to find out what the medal was for. Then suddenly it heard a voice. It was a boy! The bunny wanted to talk to him but it was too scared. The boy walked over, bent down and picked up the medal.
Confused, the bunny cocked its head to one side and shrugged. The boy smiled kindly, put the medal around the bunny's neck and said "this medal is for you! You're the bravest little bunny I know".
The bunny smiled, hopped around in circles and felt so proud! This was the best day ever. The boy waved goodbye and the bunny left the garden feeling so happy.

Once upon a time, there was a funny bunny named Bob. One day, Bob went out to wander in the woods. He hopped and hopped until he found a big tree. The tree had many pears on it. Bob liked pears a lot.
Bob saw a squirrel named Sam in the tree. Sam said, "Hi Bob! Do you want a pear?" Bob said, "Yes, please!" So, Sam threw a pear down to Bob. Bob ate the pear and it was very yummy.
Bob and Sam played and shared more pears. They had a great day together. The moral of the story is that sharing with friends is fun and kind.

Once upon a time, there was a messy little girl named Poppy. Poppy liked to play in the mud and make mud pies. She had a lot of fun, but she always got very dirty.
One sunny day, Poppy was playing with her friends in the park. They were all running and laughing. Poppy's mom called out, "Stop, Poppy! It's time to go home and get clean!" Poppy didn't want to stop playing, but she knew she had to listen to her mom.
When they got home, Poppy's mom filled the tub with warm water and bubbles. Poppy climbed in and washed all the mud off. She felt so clean and happy, and she promised her mom she would try not to be so messy next time.

Once upon a time, there was a young girl who lived in a big house. One day, the girl was exploring around the house when she found an old trunk in the corner of the room. She wanted to know what was inside, so she began to pull on the latch.
"Please, please open," she begged.
Suddenly, the lock clicked open and the girl gasped with delight. She opened the lid of the trunk to find all sorts of items, including a few dolls, which were all available to her. She was delighted and decided to take one of the dolls with her.
She grabbed the doll with two hands and began to pull. The girl was so happy to have something to play with, she couldn't contain her excitement!
"Yippee!" she yelled, as she pulled the doll close, happy to have something all for herself.

Once upon a time there was an adorable little crocodile. He lived in a big pond and often spent his days swimming around and playing in the sun.
One day, the little crocodile wanted to explore and decided to go for a walk. He went for a long way and suddenly he saw a girl. He was so excited and wanted to say hello.
He shouted: "Hello, I'm a crocodile. What's your name?"
The girl smiled and said, "My name is Alex. It's nice to meet you!"
The little crocodile was so happy. They both started playing together. They threw stones and ran around the pond. They laughed and smiled and it seemed like they would remain together forever.
But then, it was getting dark and the little crocodile knew he had to go back to his pond before it was too late. He said goodbye to Alex, gave her a big hug, and they both promised they would remain friends forever.
The little crocodile ran off and when he reached the pond, he stopped and looked back. Alex was waving at him and he waved back. He smiled and said to himself, "Alex is an adorable friend."

One day, a young nurse named Jane went to work. She had to care for a little boy named Tim. Tim was sad because he could not go out and play with his friends. Jane wanted to make Tim happy.
"Tim, let's play a game," said Jane. They played with toys and drew pictures. But Tim was still sad. He wanted to go outside. Jane thought hard about how to help him.
Then, something unexpected happened. A little bird flew into the room through the window. The bird was hurt and needed help. Jane and Tim worked together to care for the bird. They gave it food and a safe place to rest.
After a while, the bird got better and flew away. Tim was not sad anymore. He was happy because he helped the bird. Jane was happy too. They knew they could still have fun and help others, even if they could not go outside.

Once upon a time, there was a bright sun in the sky. It was a happy day. A little girl named Sue loved to play outside. She was very brave and liked to dare her friends to do fun things.
One day, Sue dared her friend Tim to make a big tower with spaghetti. They went to the kitchen and found a lot of spaghetti. They started to build the tower together. It was fun and the tower was getting taller and taller.
But then, a big wind came and blew the spaghetti tower away. Sue and Tim laughed and laughed. They had a great time, even if they didn't finish the tower. They knew they would dare each other to do more fun things another day.

Once upon a time, there was a helpful ant. The ant wanted to measure the earth. He started to walk and count his steps. He walked and walked for a very long time.
One day, the ant met a big bear. The bear was not nice. The bear said, "You cannot measure the earth. It is too big for a small ant like you." The ant felt sad but wanted to keep going.
The ant tried to walk around the bear, but the bear was too big. The bear did not let the ant go. The ant was very sad and could not measure the earth. The helpful ant's dream did not come true, and the story had a bad ending.

Anna and Ben were twins. They liked to play with their toys, but sometimes they fought. One day, they both wanted to play with the same toy. It was a big red car that could rock back and forth.
"I saw it first!" Anna said. She grabbed the car and hugged it to her chest.
"No, I did!" Ben said. He tried to pull the car away from Anna. They both pulled hard and shouted.
"Stop it, you two!" Mom said. She came into the room and saw the twins fighting. "You need to share the car. It is not nice to fight over toys."
"But I want it!" Anna said. She was stubborn and did not want to let go.
"So do I!" Ben said. He was stubborn too and did not want to give up.
Mom sighed. She knew the twins loved each other, but sometimes they forgot. She had an idea. She took a small pink heart from her pocket and showed it to the twins.
"Look, this is a special heart," she said. "It is a magic heart. It can make you happy and kind. But it only works if you share it with someone you love. Do you want to try it?"
Anna and Ben looked at the heart. It was shiny and pretty. They both wanted to touch it.
"OK, Mom," Anna said. She let go of the car and reached for the heart.
"OK, Mom," Ben said. He let go of the car too and reached for the heart.
Mom smiled. She gave the heart to Anna and said, "You can hold it first, but then you have to give it to Ben. And then he has to give it back to you. And so on. That is how you share the heart."
Anna nodded. She held the heart in her hand and felt a warm feeling in her chest. She looked at Ben and said, "I love you, Ben."
Ben smiled. He held the heart in his hand and felt a warm feeling too. He looked at Anna and said, "I love you, Anna."
The twins hugged each other and said sorry for fighting. Then they took turns rocking the car and playing with the heart. They were happy and kind. Mom was happy too. She watched them and thought, "That is the magic of the heart."

Once there was a little boy named Bob. He liked to play near the fireplace when it was cold outside. His mum warned him it was dangerous, but Bob replied that he was being careful.
One day when Bob was playing near the fireplace, he didn't listen to his mum and got too close. A spark flew out of the fireplace and lit Bob's shirt on fire! He was so scared and started to cry.
But then Bob's mum ran over and gave him a big hug. She quickly put out the fire and then the two of them sat together in the living room.
Bob's mum reminded him about how dangerous the fireplace could be. She said if he ever does anything dangerous he needs to tell someone first. So then he could stay safe. Bob replied that he would always remember to stay safe and not do anything dangerous.
The moral of the story is to be safe and always listen to your mum. If you do something dangerous tell someone to help you stay safe.

Anna and Ben were playing in the garden. They had a big bucket of water and some cups. They liked to pour water on the flowers and on each other.
"Look, Anna, I can make a big splash!" Ben said. He filled his cup with water and threw it in the air.
Anna laughed and did the same. "Me too, me too!" she said. The water sparkled in the sun.
But they did not see the giant who lived next door. He was very grumpy and did not like noise. He was trying to take a nap, but the sound of the water and the children's laughter woke him up.
He opened his eyes and saw the water flying over the fence. He got very angry and decided to teach them a lesson. He grabbed his kettle, which was full of hot tea, and poured it over the fence.
"Ouch, ouch, ouch!" Anna and Ben screamed. The hot tea burned their skin and clothes. They ran inside the house, crying and wet.
"Who did that? Who did that?" their mom asked. She saw the steam and the tea stains on the fence. She knew it was the giant. She was very mad and called the police.
The police came and knocked on the giant's door. He opened it and saw their angry faces. He knew he was in trouble. He tried to say sorry, but it was too late. The police took him away and locked him up.
Anna and Ben learned to be careful with water and to respect their neighbors. The giant learned to be nice and to share his tea. They all lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time, in a big factory, there was a wealthy man. He seemed very happy. The factory made toys for kids. The wealthy man liked to see the toys and the kids' happy faces.
One day, while the wealthy man was walking in the factory, he saw a big box. He did not know what was inside. He opened the box and something unexpected happened. Out of the box came a big, talking toy! The toy said, "Hello! I am your new friend."
The wealthy man was very surprised. He did not know that the factory made talking toys. He played with the toy all day. They became good friends. The wealthy man was even happier now. He learned that sometimes, unexpected things can make you happy.

Once upon a time, there was a little chair. It was a useful chair. Many people would sit on it and sigh. They felt happy when they sat on the chair. The chair had a big friend, the tree. They lived in a nice park.
One day, a big storm came. The tree told the chair, "I will keep you safe." The chair was scared but trusted the tree. The tree was very strong. The storm made a lot of noise, and the wind blew hard.
But the storm was too strong. The tree tried to hold on, but it fell down. The chair was very sad. The tree could not help the chair anymore. People came and took the tree away. The chair was all alone, and nobody sat on it again.

A little girl named Lea had a special radio. She loved listening to it every day. Especially when her parents had it set up to the music station. One day, Lea noticed something was wrong with her radio. She brought it to her dad and he said, "Let's see if we can improve it."
Lea's dad asked Lea to be brave and he used tools to open up the radio. He checked inside and said, "Ah, I see the problem." He pointed to something and Lea could see it was deaf.
Her dad quickly replaced the deaf part and then carefully closed the radio up again. He stepped back and said, "Now let's see if it works." Lea turned the radio on and was so excited to hear the music again.
He smiled and gave Lea a big hug. Her dad said, "Lea, you were so brave. It's great that we could improve your radio."
Lea was so happy and she hugged her dad. She thanked him for fixing her radio and said, "Thank you, Daddy."

Once upon a time, there was a funny cat named Sam. Sam loved to supply his friends with jokes to make them laugh. One day, Sam was worried because he could not think of a new joke.
Sam went to his friend, a little bird named Lily. He said, "Lily, I am worried. I can't think of a new joke to make our friends laugh. Can you help me?"
Lily thought for a moment and said, "Why don't we ask our friends to help us? Maybe they have a joke we can share."
Sam and Lily went to their friends, and they all shared their best jokes. Sam was no longer worried. Now, he had many jokes to supply and make everyone laugh. And they all had a fun and happy day together.

Sara loves to paint. She has many colors of paint and a big paper. She likes to make pictures of flowers, animals and rainbows. She is very happy when she paints.
One day, she sees a new boy in her class. His name is Ben. He looks sad and lonely. He does not have any paint or paper. He sits by himself and watches Sara paint.
Sara feels sorry for Ben. She is a compassionate girl. She wants to help him and make him happy. She decides to ask him a question.
"Hi, Ben. Do you want to paint with me?" Sara asks. She smiles and holds out her paint and paper.
Ben looks surprised and shy. He does not know Sara. He does not know how to paint. He shakes his head and says, "No, thank you."
Sara does not give up. She thinks of another question. She says, "Do you like flowers? I can show you how to paint a flower. It is easy and fun."
Ben looks curious and interested. He likes flowers. He nods and says, "Yes, I like flowers."
Sara is happy. She moves closer to Ben and gives him some paint and paper. She says, "Look, this is how you paint a flower. You make a circle for the center and some lines for the petals. Then you can choose any color you want."
Ben watches Sara and tries to copy her. He makes a circle and some lines. He chooses yellow and blue. He paints his flower. He smiles and says, "Look, I made a flower."
Sara looks at Ben's flower and claps her hands. She says, "Wow, you made a beautiful flower. You are a good painter. Do you want to paint more?"
Ben nods and says, "Yes, I want to paint more. Thank you, Sara. You are a nice friend."
Sara and Ben paint more flowers, animals and rainbows. They talk and laugh. They have fun. They become friends. They are happy.

John and his mom were in the kitchen. John saw a big bag of spaghetti. "Can I measure the spaghetti?" he asked.
His mom smiled. She gave him a ruler and said, "Yes, you can measure the spaghetti. See how long it is?"
John was very excited! He carefully placed the ruler next to the spaghetti and said, "It's very long! What can I do with it?"
His mom had a great plan. "Let's make an ancient spaghetti tower," she suggested.
John helped his mom measure out portions of the spaghetti and then stack them one on top of the other. They used the ruler to measure the length of each piece before stacking it.
When their tower was complete, they stepped back to admire their creation. "The ancient spaghetti tower is finished!" said John.
His mom smiled. "Yes, and it's going to be delicious!"

Once upon a time there were two little bunnies. One was called Original and the other was called Bandage. They were good friends who always loved to play together. They would race around the garden, play tag, and hop to the market.
One day, Original hurt his paw and started to feel sad. Bandage saw this and thought of a way to help. He fetched a bandage he found in the garden and carefully wrapped it around his friend's paw. Original felt much better and thanked Bandage for his support.
From then on, whenever Original was feeling down, Bandage was there to help. With a bandage and a few kind words, they became the best of friends!

One day, a little boy named Tim saw an incredible big aeroplane in the sky. He was so happy and excited. He ran to his mom and said, "Mom, look up! Big aeroplane!"
His mom looked up and smiled. She said, "Wow, that is a big aeroplane, Tim! It is flying very high in the sky."
Later, Tim went outside to play in the sand. He found a toy aeroplane and decided to bury it in the sand. He dug a hole and put the toy inside. Then, he covered it up with more sand. Tim was very proud of his work.
Suddenly, the sand started to shake and move. Tim was scared, but he watched as the toy aeroplane grew bigger and bigger. The toy turned into a real aeroplane, just like the one he saw in the sky! It was a big surprise.
Tim ran to his mom and said, "Mom, come see! My toy is now a big aeroplane!" His mom couldn't believe her eyes. They both laughed and played with the incredible aeroplane all day long.

One day, a little girl named Lucy went to the park with her mom. She brought her favorite bag with her. Inside the bag, she had her toys and snacks. She was very happy to play and share with her friends.
While Lucy was playing, she saw a big, tough dog. The dog looked scary, and it made her panic. She ran to her mom and told her about the dog. Her mom said not to worry, and that the dog was just playing too.
Lucy felt better and went back to play with her friends. She opened her bag and shared her snacks with everyone. The tough dog came over and wagged its tail. Lucy saw that the dog was friendly and not scary at all. She gave the dog a snack too, and they all played happily together.

Once there was a naughty little girl. She was very naughty and always misbehaving. One day she do something very naughty. She took her daddy’s tie and put it in the dirt. It began to rot away in the soil.
The little girl felt very bad about what she’d done, but it was too late. The tie was ruined and couldn’t be fixed.
The little girl’s daddy was very sad. He had worked very hard to buy that tie, and now it was gone. The little girl knew she had done something wrong and she was sorry.
The daddy gave the little girl a big, big hug and said it was alright. He said he still loved her and that she must never do such a naughty thing again.
The little girl was very happy and promised to never do anything naughty again. And with that, the tie was forgotten and the little girl was happy once more.

Once upon a time there was a nosy pilot. He liked to teach others about flying. One day, he met a girl who was only three years old. She was very small and very curious about flying.
The pilot decided to teach her. He showed her the different kinds of planes and taught her about the controls. He explained to her how to take off, how to fly and even how to land a plane.
The girl was so excited to learn. She asked a lot of questions and wanted to learn even more. The nosy pilot taught her everything he knew.
At the end of the day, the little girl was a pro at flying planes! She thanked the pilot for teaching her and he was very proud of how much she had learned.

Once upon a time, there were two logs. One log was big, and one log was small. The small log was different because it had stars carved into it.
The two logs were put near each other. Then, they were lit on fire. The logs became brighter and brighter until they both began to burn. The logs burned for a long time until nothing was left.
When the logs were no more, everyone was happy. The small log with stars carved in it was a special log - it made the fire look different! And that made everyone feel warm and happy.

Kitty and Doggy played with the red ball and the diamond toy. They shared and had lots of fun. From that day on, they became the best of friends.

Once upon a time, there was a shy cat named Tim. Tim liked to stir things. One day, Tim saw a big cup. Inside the cup, there was a measure of milk. Tim wanted to stir the milk. He thought it would be fun.
Tim saw a big dog named Max. Max was not shy. Max saw Tim and the milk. Max wanted the milk too. Max and Tim did not like to share. They both wanted the milk for themselves.
Tim tried to stir the milk, but Max got angry. Max and Tim fought over the milk. They pushed and pulled. In the end, the cup fell down. The milk spilled all over the floor. No one got the milk. Tim and Max were sad.

Once upon a time there was a very tidy hotel. Everything was always just so neat and clean. But one day there was a problem - a big problem. At night, the water began to rise in the hotel! It started to fill the room and it was hot too.
Everyone was scared, but the hotel manager knew just what to do. He got everyone busy and had them cleaning up the hotel. Each person had a task and everyone worked together to make it tidy again.
By morning, the water had stopped rising, and the hotel was all fixed up. Everyone was so relieved, and the manager was praised for his quick thinking. From that day on, everyone admired the beauty and tidiness of the hotel.

Once upon a time, there was a little dog named Spot. Spot loved to play with his ball all day. One day, Spot's ball rolled into a dark cave. Spot was scared, but he wanted his ball back.
Spot went into the cave and saw a small spark. He realized that the spark was a firefly. The firefly was sad and helpless because she was lost. Spot wanted to help the firefly find her way out of the cave.
Spot and the firefly worked together to find the way out. They became good friends. When they got out of the cave, Spot found his ball and the firefly was happy to be home. The moral of the story is: when you help others, you help yourself too.

Once upon a time there was a little girl called Amy who liked to explore. One day she was roaming around the house and she noticed a big wardrobe in the corner of the room. She walked up to it and tried to reach the handle. Suddenly, the wardrobe seemed very tall! She was desperate to open it, when suddenly she saw a chair. She took the chair, stood on it and covered the handle with her hands. With some struggle she was able to open the wardrobe.
Now inside the wardrobe there were two paths, a rough and a smooth one. She looked at both and noticed that the rough path seemed fun and exciting. Without thinking too much, she started walking on it.
The path seemed to be never ending. She walked for what seemed like hours. Her tiny legs got tired and she started crying. Then suddenly, her mom appeared with a big smile on her face. She hugged Amy and said "You see my darling, when you take the rough path, you can get lost. Always take the smooth path and you’ll never get lost".
Amy smiled and hugged her mom. From that day onwards, whenever Amy needed to choose a path she always remembered her mom's advice. She took the smooth path and kept away from any rough roads.

Once upon a time, there was a jolly man named Tom. Tom had a big gear. One day, he wanted to mix colors to paint his gear. He went to the store to buy paint.
Tom started to mix red and blue paint. He was very happy. He painted his gear with the new color. The gear looked very pretty. Tom showed his gear to his friends. They liked it too.
But, Tom did not clean up the paint. He left the paint cans open. A little dog came and knocked over the paint. The paint spilled all over the floor, and the dog got dirty too. Tom was sad. His mom was not happy. The moral of the story is always clean up after you are done playing.

One day, a boy and his dog went to play. It was a high hill. The boy said, "I love this season. It is warm and fun." The dog wagged his tail.
They ran and played. The boy saw a big tree. He kneeled down to look at it. The dog barked. The boy said, "What is it, dog?"
Then, a little bird came out of the tree. It talked! The bird said, "Hello, boy and dog. I am a magic bird. I can make one wish come true." The boy and the dog looked at each other. They were happy and surprised.
The boy thought and said, "I wish for more days like this to play with my dog." The magic bird nodded and flew away. The boy and the dog played all season long.

Jack was playing at the park with his friends when Ben came up to him. Ben had a bottle of shampoo with him and he started to tease Jack about it.
Jack said, "Why do you have shampoo?"
Ben replied, "I found it by the slide and I wanted to show you. It smells really good!"
Jack was a little upset but he said, "That's nice. Can I see it?"
Ben handed Jack the bottle of shampoo and Jack opened it to take a sniff. He smiled and said, "It smells great!"
Ben smiled and said, "See - there's nothing to be upset about. Let's go back and play!"
And so Jack and Ben went back to the park and played together until the sun went down.

Sue's mom went to get a towel to dry the dog, but when she came back, she saw that the dog had eaten the toast! Sue was sad at first, but then she saw that the dog was not trembling anymore. She was glad that the dog was warm and happy. Sue's mom made more toast for Sue and the dog, and they all had breakfast together.

Once upon a time, there was a generous leopard who loved to help others. He used to help many animals in the forest and they loved him very much.
One day, the leopard was walking through the forest when he noticed a small rabbit who was stuck in a bush. So he jumped up and asked the rabbit, "How I can help you?"
The rabbit replied, "I'm stuck and can't get out."
The leopard happily replied, "Don't worry, I can help you!" He quickly used his claws to break the branches and set the rabbit free.
The rabbit thanked the leopard and hopped away. But as soon as he was gone, a big lion appeared with a scary roar. The lion was angry and asked the leopard, "Why did you help the rabbit?"
The leopard was scared, but he stayed calm and said, "Because I wanted to help him."
The lion didn't believe him, and he angrily attacked the leopard. The leopard tried to fight back, but the lion was too strong and he eventually died.
The moral of the story is to always be careful who you help!

Tim looked at all the insects again. He thought about what his mom said. He chose the small green bug because it looked nice and did not bite. Tim was happy with his new friend. Later, he found out that the green bug could change colors! It was the most interesting insect in the park.

Once upon a time there was a happy family. The family had a mom, a dad and two children, Sammy and Sue.
Sue loved to pick flowers in the garden and give them to her mom and dad. She also loved to pick flowers for Sammy to give to him.
One day Sammy said, "Sue, I want to marry a flower". Sue thought it was a funny idea and said, "Yes, let's marry a flower". So they took one of Sue's flowers and said, "We marry you".
That night Sammy had a dream that their flower flew away, and he and Sue were left without a flower. He was sad and he told Sue about it in the morning.
Sue said, "I know what happened, Sammy. We thought it was funny to marry a flower, but it isn't serious." Sammy nodded and they decided to be more careful with their jokes and find serious things to do.
The moral of the story is that it's important to be serious and not joke about things that are important.

Lily and Ben are twins. They like to study nature. They go to the park with Mom and Dad. They see many things: trees, flowers, birds, bugs.
"Look, Lily, a big butterfly!" Ben says. He runs after it. He wants to catch it.
"No, Ben, don't!" Lily says. She follows him. She knows butterflies are not toys. They are alive and need to be free.
Ben reaches out his hand. He touches the butterfly's wing. The butterfly falls to the ground. It does not fly anymore. It is helpless.
"Ben, you hurt it!" Lily cries. She picks up the butterfly. She is gentle. She tries to make it fly again. But it does not move.
"I'm sorry, Lily. I'm sorry, butterfly." Ben says. He is sad. He did not mean to hurt it.
Mom and Dad come. They see what happened. They hug Lily and Ben.
"It's okay, kids. You made a mistake. But you can learn from it." Mom says. She takes the butterfly. She puts it on a flower.
"Maybe it will get better. Maybe it will fly again. But you have to be careful with nature. Nature is beautiful and precious. You have to respect it and protect it." Dad says.
Lily and Ben nod. They understand. They say sorry to the butterfly. They hope it will be okay. They study nature with their eyes, not their hands. They learn to love it more.

One day, Jack and his mom went to the park. Jack took out a small matchbox from his pocket and opened it. His mom said “No Jack, don’t burn the leaves!” But Jack was too excited and lit a match. The leaves started burning and it was very loud.
Jack’s mom started crying and said “Jack, you made a very bad choice. The fire is too big and dangerous!” Jack felt sad and quickly grabbed an old jug of water he found and tried to put out the fire. The water was too little and the fire kept burning.
Jack’s mom was very scared but she knew what to do. She ran to the nearest store and asked the clerk for some help. The clerk ran back to the park with a fire extinguisher and put out the fire.
Jack and his mom thanked the clerk. Jack learned his lesson and knew to never play with matches again.

As Tim settled down, he saw that the meat was in a trap! Tim was scared and knew that he had to save Bob from the trap. He quickly flew up to the trap and pushed it open with his beak. Bob was surprised and said, "Thank you, Tim. You saved my life!" From that day on, Tim and Bob became best friends and always helped each other.

"Wow, what is this?" Tom asked.

They decided to take the egg home and keep it as a pet. They put it in a box and covered it with a blanket. They gave it some water and bread. They waited for it to hatch.
The next day, they heard a loud crack. They ran to the box and saw a baby dragon. It had scales, wings, and a long tail. It looked at them and made a noise.
"Hello, little dragon!" Lila said. "We are your friends. We will take care of you."
The dragon did not seem to like them. It snapped at them and scratched the box. It tried to escape.
"Hey, stop that!" Tom said. "We are nice to you. You should accept us."
The dragon did not listen. It broke the box and flew out of the window. It roared and breathed fire. It burned the house and the trees. It scared the people and the animals.
Lila and Tom were very sad and scared. They realized they made a big mistake. They should not have taken the egg. They should have left it alone. They were not lucky. They were in trouble.

"Hi, jellyfish! Can you whistle?" Tom asked. The jellyfish did not say anything. Tom was sad, but he tried to whistle for the jellyfish. He tried and tried, but he could not whistle.

Once upon a time there was a dolphin who loved to swim in the ocean. One day, the dolphin felt lonely, so it decided to explore. It swam for a very long time, until it saw a distant island.
The dolphin started swimming to the island, but it was tired and couldn't move anymore. It shouted for help, but no one was around. Then suddenly, a kind voice answered.
"Don't worry," said the voice from the sky. "I will forgive you and help you." The dolphin looked up and saw a tiny bird flying above him.
The bird offered to fly the dolphin to the island. The dolphin agreed and flew away with the bird. As they flew, they talked and became best friends.
When they reached the island, the dolphin learned that it was a magical place. Dolphins were free to roam and explore as far as they wanted. The dolphin was so happy, it waved goodbye to its new friend and started to explore.
The dolphin returned to the ocean with a smile, knowing that it was safe and happy. That day, the dolphin learned that it was important to forgive and be kind to others, even when they seem distant.

Tom and Anna are friends. They like to play in the park. They have a big bucket and a small shovel. They dig and make mud pies. They are happy.
But Sam is jealous. He does not have a bucket or a shovel. He wants to play with Tom and Anna. He asks them, "Can I play with you?"
Tom and Anna say, "Yes, you can play with us. We have enough mud for everyone. You can use our bucket and shovel."
Sam is happy. He says, "Thank you, Tom and Anna. You are very nice."
But Sam is not nice. He does not share. He takes the bucket and the shovel and runs away. He says, "Ha ha, they are mine now. I have the best toys."
Tom and Anna are sad. They say, "Sam is mean. He took our bucket and shovel. He did not ask. He did not say sorry. He is not our friend."
Sam runs to the other side of the park. He tries to make mud pies. But he is not happy. He feels lonely. He sees Tom and Anna playing with other children. They have sticks and stones and leaves. They make a castle and a bridge and a boat. They have fun.
Sam wants to join them. He says, "Can I play with you?"
Tom and Anna say, "No, you cannot play with us. You are not nice. You took our bucket and shovel. You did not ask. You did not say sorry. You are not our friend."
Sam is sad. He says, "I am sorry, Tom and Anna. I was wrong. I was jealous. I did not share. I want to be your friend."
Tom and Anna say, "You can be our friend, but you have to give back our bucket and shovel. You have to say sorry. You have to share."
Sam says, "I will give back your bucket and shovel. I will say sorry. I will share."
He carries the bucket and the shovel to Tom and Anna. He says, "I am sorry, Tom and Anna. Here are your bucket and shovel. Can I play with you?"
Tom and Anna say, "Yes, you can play with us. We forgive you. We are happy."
They all play together. They make mud pies and castles and bridges and boats. They share and help and laugh. They are friends.
The moral of the story is: Do not be jealous. Do not take what is not yours. Do not be mean. Be nice. Share and say sorry. Be a good friend.

Once upon a time there lived a bear in a small, tidy house. Every morning he would look in the mirror and pretend to be a bear-king. He would talk to his reflection in his most regal voice and act like he was travelling to mysterious lands. On one particular morning, he decided he needed a passport to make his adventures more real.
He opened his drawer and rummaged through old toys, until he found a small card with a big stamp on the back. It was a passport! Instantly, he knew his stories would be even more vivid.
He said to himself, “Now I must find a palace to stay in during my travels.” He created a beautiful palace out of his messy pillow and cushion covers. Then he jumped into the throne, embraced his passport tight and imagined bumpy flights over the sea.
He said to his reflection, “Wear your kingly crown and let’s go on an adventure!” His passport was clutched in one paw and the other held his crown firmly in place. He said to his reflection, “Let’s go explore the world!” and he set off on his imaginary journey.

Once upon a time there was a brave little girl named Jane. One day, Jane was out playing in the garden and came across a small white rabbit that was stuck in a bush. Jane noticed that the rabbit was scared and so she decided to rescue him.
Jane tried to think of a way to rescue the rabbit and decided to use some celery sticks to make a bridge for him to walk on. She placed the celery sticks next to the bush, forming a bridge.
The rabbit sniffed the celery and started to nibble on it. He was so excited to have something to eat that he forgot to be scared, and walked across the celery bridge and hopped away. Suddenly, a stupid fox appeared and started to chase the rabbit!
Jane was worried and said, "I must rescue it!" She grabbed the remaining celery sticks and threw them one by one at the fox. The fox got so distracted by the celery that he ran away and the rabbit was safe again.
Jane had saved the rabbit! She hugged the rabbit and said goodbye before letting him go. Jane felt so happy that she was able to rescue the poor rabbit.

The brave fisherman could not swim. He tried to call for help, but no one was near. The brave fisherman was very sad and scared.
The moral of the story is: be careful when you help others, as they may not always be nice.

After a while, the caterpillar felt tired. He found a nice, soft leaf to rest on. He fell asleep and had sweet dreams. When he woke up, he was not a caterpillar anymore. He was a beautiful butterfly!

Anna and Ben were playing with their toys in the living room. They liked to pretend they were pirates and explorers, sailing on a big ship. They had a map, a telescope, a sword and a hat.
"Look, Ben, I see an island!" Anna said, pointing with her telescope. "Maybe there is treasure there!"
"Let's go, Anna!" Ben said, grabbing his sword and hat. "But we have to be careful, there might be enemies or traps!"
They ran to the couch and climbed on it, pretending it was their ship. They made noises like the wind and the waves, and sang pirate songs.
Suddenly, they heard a loud boom outside. They looked out the window and saw dark clouds and lightning. It was a storm!
"Uh-oh, Anna, we have to go back!" Ben said, scared. "The storm is too big and dangerous!"
"But what about the treasure, Ben?" Anna asked, still curious.
"We can find it another day, Anna," Ben said, trying to be brave. "The treasure is not important, we are important. We have to be safe and humble, like Mommy and Daddy always say."
"Okay, Ben, you are right," Anna said, nodding. "Let's go back to our room and wait for the storm to pass."
They took their toys and ran to their room, holding hands. They hugged each other and prayed for the storm to go away. They did not mention the island or the treasure anymore. They were happy to have each other.

One day, the girl found a key. She was excited and held it in her hand.
But then, the girl realized that the key was special. With it, she could restore things.
So, the girl went on a journey. She used the key to restore things that made her feel happy and brave.
In the end, the girl was no longer shy or ashamed. She had restored her courage and was now filled with joy.

Once upon a time, there was a farm with many animals. The animals liked to play and have fun. One day, a little dog named Max came to the farm. Max wanted to play with the animals, but he was very small.
"Stay safe, Max," said the big cow. "The farm can be a big place for a little dog like you." Max listened and stayed close to the cow. They played together and had fun.
One day, while they were playing, they heard a loud noise. The other animals were scared, but Max was brave. He went to see what it was. It was a big machine that was lost. Max barked and showed the machine the way out of the farm. The animals were so happy and thanked Max for keeping them safe. From that day on, Max was a hero on the farm and everyone loved him.

Once there was a mom and dad who had a special little girl named Samantha. She was the most curious three-year-old ever!  She was always exploring and trying to learn new things.
One day, Samantha found a really sharp pair of scissors and she wanted to play with them. Mom said, "No, sweetheart, those scissors have a sharp blade so they could cut you! We must be careful!"
But Samantha wanted to just keep playing. She took the scissors and started to cut the sofa in the living room.
Mom saw what happened and got really mad.  She shouted, "Samantha! How could you do this?! You have been naughty!"
The couch was now all covered in ugly ashes. Mom was so sad!
Finally, Samantha realized how wrong she was and she cried as she apologized to her mom. Mom hugged her and said, "Don't do wrong things, sweetheart. Be careful and always listen to mommy."
And ever since that day, Samantha looked both ways before crossing the street, said please and thank you, and was extra mindful of the things she played with.

When Tim got there, he said, "Look, Sam! I found this big rod." Sam looked at the rod and said, "Wow! That's impressive. Let's play with it." They played with the rod all day, having a lot of fun.
Suddenly, a dog came and took the rod from them. Tim and Sam were sad. They tried to get the rod back, but the dog was too fast. Then, Tim said, "Let's quit. We can find another rod." But Sam said, "No, we can't quit. That's our rod." So, they kept trying to get the rod back. At last, they got the rod back and were very happy. The dog was not bad, he just wanted to play too. So, they all played together and had a great day.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl called Sarah. She was three years old, and her mom and dad said that she was perfect.
Each day, Sarah saw posts in the back yard of her house, with flowers around them. Every week, they got new posts. Sarah asked her mom what they were, and her mom said that soon Sarah would find out.
One sunny day, the posts got flowers again, but this time they had something special. Sarah's mom said that the posts were for her, and that she had to forgive something.
Sarah didn't know what she had to forgive, but she hugged all the posts with the flowers around them.
That night Sarah and her family had dinner together. They ate with special hearts and forgave each other. After dinner, Sarah understood why the posts were there - they were for her to show that mom and dad loved her, and that it was ok to forgive.
Her mom and dad were so proud of how perfect Sarah was, and how she could forgive so easily.  From that day on, Sarah and her family always forgave each other. The posts in the garden were always a reminder of that.

When it was time to go home, Tim thanked the yellow bus for the fun day. The bus said, "You're welcome, Tim. I'm glad I could help." From that day on, Tim and the yellow bus were best friends.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim had a plan. He wanted to clean up all the junk in his room. Tim's mom was not happy with the mess. She felt miserable every time she saw the junk.
Tim started to pick up his toys and put them away. But then, he found a big box. Tim did not know what was inside. He opened the box and found a magic wand. Tim was very surprised.
Tim waved the magic wand at the junk. All of a sudden, the junk started to dance and sing. The junk cleaned itself up! Tim's mom came into the room and saw the clean room. One day, a hungry cat named Tom went for a walk. Tom saw a big melon on the ground. He wanted to eat it, but the melon was too big for him. Tom saw a little bird named Sue.
"Hi Sue, can you help me?" asked Tom. "I want to feed on this big melon, but it's too big for me."
Sue thought for a moment and said, "Okay, I will help you. Let's push the melon together."
Tom and Sue pushed the big melon. It started to roll down a hill. They ran after it, trying to catch it. The melon went faster and faster.
At the bottom of the hill, the melon hit a rock and broke open. But inside the melon, there were many little fish! Tom and Sue were very surprised. They did not expect to find fish in a melon.
Tom and Sue were happy. They shared the little fish and ate them together. They were not hungry anymore. And they became very good friends.
