When he was happy that the fire was gone, he returned the hose and drove back in his fire truck. All the people cheered for Bill - the brave fireman!

Tim and Sam tried to hold on, but they were too small. The bird flew away with the rope and the ants. And that was the end of Tim and Sam's playtime.

The bell answered, "I am here to make music for everyone."
Tim wanted to taste the bell, so he climbed the tree. When he got to the top, he found a big, red apple next to the bell. He tasted the apple, and it was the best apple he had ever eaten.

Once upon a time, there was a restless little bee named Buzz. Buzz loved to fly around and look for pretty flowers. One day, he saw a big sunflower in a field. Buzz thought the sunflower was very pretty, so he flew over to say hello.
Buzz and the sunflower became very good friends. They would laugh and play together every day. The sunflower would dance in the wind, and Buzz would buzz around her. But one day, the sunflower stopped dancing. She was sad because she couldn't see the other flowers in the field.
Buzz wanted to help his friend, so he had an idea. He asked all the other bees to help him move the sunflower to a new spot. The bees worked together, and soon they moved the sunflower to a place where she could see all the other flowers. The sunflower was happy again, and she started to dance and laugh with Buzz.
The other flowers saw how happy the sunflower was, and they wanted to be friends too. Soon, all the flowers in the field were friends, and they all laughed and played together. The moral of the story is that helping a friend can bring happiness to everyone.

"Who is he?" Anna asks.
"I don't know," Ben says.

"Yes, please," Sam says.

"You're welcome," Anna and Ben say.

"Here, this is for you," Anna says.
"It's beautiful," Sam says.
He puts on the wrap and feels cozy and loved. He smiles and says thank you again.
Anna and Ben feel happy and humble. They realize they have more than they need. They realize they can share and make new friends. They realize they can be kind and generous. They realize they are good kids.

She thought that it was so interesting that she had to pick it up. She could not believe how heavy it was. She put her arms around it and dragged it along.
She went over to a nearby bench and sat down. She continued to stare at the stick. She thought about all the things she could do with it. She could make it into a long boss, use it to paint with or just play around with it. She was sure that it could keep her busy for hours.
The more she stared at it, the more interesting it became. After thinking about it for a while, she decided to take it home with her. She said goodbye to the park and started walking home with her new stick. She was sure that she was going to have a lot of fun with it.

Once upon a time, there was a young boy named Tim. Tim loved to play outside with his dog, Spot. They would run and jump all day long. One day, Tim found a big sheet in his room. He had an idea.
Tim took the sheet outside and said to Spot, "Let's play save the day!" He tied the sheet around his neck like a cape. Tim and Spot ran around, pretending to save their friends from danger.
As they played, a little girl named Sue came over. She was sad because her cat was stuck in a tree. "Can you save my cat, Tim?" Sue asked. Tim and Spot looked up at the tree and saw the cat. With the sheet still tied around his neck, Tim climbed the tree and saved the cat. Sue was so happy and thanked Tim and Spot. From that day on, Tim, Spot, and Sue became best friends and played together every day.

One day, a boy named Tim went to the park with his mom. They played on the swings and had lots of fun. After a while, Tim's mom said it was time to leave. Tim was sad, but he knew they would come back another day.
On their way home, they saw an ice cream truck. Tim's mom bought him a cone with his favorite ice cream. He was so happy! But then, a bird flew by and dropped something on his ice cream. It was disgusting! Tim didn't want to eat it anymore.
Tim's mom saw how sad he was and got him a new cone. This time, they sat under a tree to eat it. Tim enjoyed his ice cream and forgot about the disgusting thing that happened. He was happy again, and they went home with smiles on their faces.

Tom opened the box and found a little bird. The bird was scared, but Tom was kind. They became friends and played together. The bird could sing beautiful songs. Tom and the bird had lots of fun in the attic. And that's how Tom found a new friend in the most unexpected place.

Once upon a time, there was a big black dog named Buddy. Buddy lived in a house with a nice family. Buddy liked to play and run all day long.
One day, Buddy went outside to play. The sun was very harsh, but Buddy didn't mind. He played with a ball, ran around, and barked. After a while, Buddy felt very tired. He found a tree with cool shade and said to himself, "I need to rest."
Buddy lay down under the tree and closed his eyes. He felt the cool grass on his fur and listened to the birds sing. Soon, Buddy's friend, a little girl named Lily, came outside. She saw Buddy resting and said, "Buddy, are you okay?"
Buddy opened his eyes and wagged his tail. He knew he needed to rest, but he was happy to see his friend. "Yes, Lily, I am just resting," he said. Lily smiled and sat down next to Buddy, giving him a big hug. They both rested under the tree, enjoying the shade and each other's company.

Once upon a time there was a boy called James. He was very eager to go on an adventure. One day, he decided to explore the forest near his house. James hopped on his bicycle and off he went.
As he weaved his way through the trees, the path got twistier. He couldn’t believe his eyes when he came across an old chair. James stopped, got off his bicycle and sat on the seat.
Suddenly, a rabbit appeared.
James was astonished to see the rabbit and asked, “What are you doing here?”
The rabbit replied, “I’m looking for some yummy carrots.”
James was so happy that he decided to help the rabbit. He hopped off the seat and the two of them searched the forest together. After a few moments, the rabbit found a few juicy carrots.
James smiled and said, “Let’s go home.” And he was eager to tell his family all about his adventure!

Once upon a time, in a bright sunny day, a yellow bird named Sunny lived in a big tree. Sunny loved to sing and play with his friends. They liked to celebrate every time they found a new friend.
One day, Sunny met a big, scary cat. The cat said, "Sunny, let's be friends and celebrate." Sunny thought, "Maybe the cat is not so bad." So, they became friends and played together.
But the cat was not a good friend. One day, when Sunny was singing, the cat jumped and caught Sunny. The cat was happy, but Sunny was sad. The other birds saw what happened and flew away.
The moral of the story is to be careful when choosing friends. Not everyone who wants to be your friend is good for you.

Once upon a time, there was an ordinary boy named Tim. Tim liked to play outside. One day, he found a hole in his yard. He wanted to see how big the hole was. Tim decided to count how many steps it took to walk around the hole.
He started to count. One, two, three, four, five. Tim walked around the hole until he counted ten steps. He was happy to know how big the hole was. Tim thought it was a special hole.
Tim told his friends about the hole. They came to see it too. They all counted the steps around the hole together. One, two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight, nine, ten. They all agreed it was a special hole. They played around the hole every day, and they had lots of fun.

One day, a boy named Tim and his dog, Spot, went to the park. Tim wanted to record a video of Spot doing a fun trick. He wanted to show his friends how strong Spot's muscles were.
At the park, Tim said, "Spot, I want to see you jump high and clean!" Spot ran and jumped over a big log. Tim recorded it with his camera. They were both happy.
But then, a big wind came and blew Tim's camera away! "Oh no!" said Tim. Spot ran fast and caught the camera before it fell. Tim was so happy and said, "Good job, Spot! You saved our video!" They went home to show their friends the video of Spot's strong muscles and the big jump.

Lily and Ben were twins who liked to play together. They had many toys, but their favorite was a big box. They pretended the box was a spaceship, a castle, a boat, and many other things.
One day, their mom said she had to mail some letters. She asked Lily and Ben to help her put the letters in the box. Lily and Ben were happy to help. They took the letters and put them in the box. But then, they had an idea.
"Let's mail ourselves!" Lily said.
"Yeah, let's go on an adventure!" Ben agreed.
They climbed into the box and closed the lid. They giggled and whispered to each other.
"Where do you think we will go?" Lily asked.
"Maybe to the moon, or the jungle, or the sea!" Ben said.
They waited and waited, but nothing happened. They started to feel hot and bored.
"Mom, we're ready to go!" Lily shouted.
"Mom, open the box!" Ben yelled.
But mom did not hear them. She had gone to the kitchen to make some tea. She did not know that Lily and Ben were in the box.
Soon, dad came home from work. He saw the box and the letters on the table.
"Looks like mom has some mail to send," he said. He picked up the box and carried it to the car. He did not know that Lily and Ben were in the box.
Lily and Ben felt the box move. They were scared and confused.
"Dad, stop!" Lily cried.
"Dad, we're in here!" Ben screamed.
But dad did not hear them. He had turned on the radio and was singing along. He did not know that Lily and Ben were in the box.
He drove to the post office and parked the car. He got out and took the box to the counter. He did not know that Lily and Ben were in the box.
"Hi, I have some mail to send," he said to the lady.
"Sure, let me weigh it for you," the lady said. She put the box on the scale. She was shocked. The box was very heavy.
"What's in here?" she asked.
"Just some letters," dad said.
The lady looked at him suspiciously. She shook the box gently. She heard some noises.
"Help!" Lily and Ben shouted.
The lady opened the box. She was shocked. She saw Lily and Ben in the box.
"What are you doing here?" she asked.
"We wanted to mail ourselves," Lily and Ben said.
The lady looked at dad. He was shocked. He saw Lily and Ben in the box.
"How did you get in there?" he asked.
"We put ourselves in," Lily and Ben said.
Dad was angry. He took Lily and Ben out of the box. He apologized to the lady. He took the letters and the box and went back to the car. He did not know what to say.
Lily and Ben were sorry. They realized they had made a big mistake. They did not want to mail themselves anymore. They wanted to go home.
"Mom, we're sorry!" Lily and Ben said.
"Mom, we love you!" Lily and Ben said.
Mom was shocked. She saw Lily and Ben in the car. She hugged them and kissed them. She was glad they were safe.
"What happened?" she asked.
"We'll tell you later," dad said.
He drove them home. He explained what had happened. He was not happy. He said they had to be more careful and listen to mom. He said they had to share their toys and play nicely. He said they had to be fair.
Lily and Ben nodded. They agreed. They learned their lesson. They did not want to mail themselves again. They wanted to stay with mom and dad. They loved them very much.

They started to pass the cherry back and forth, but then the cherry fell on the ground. Sue and Tim were sad because they could not eat the cherry anymore. At lunchtime, Tim couldn't find his lunch bag again. He was sad and hungry. But then he remembered the smart ant. He looked around and saw the ant waving at him. The ant had found Tim's lunch bag! Tim followed the ant and found his lunch. He was so happy and thanked the smart ant. From that day on, Tim never lost his lunch again.

Tommy loves to play with water. He likes to splash in the pool, fill up buckets and pour them out, and make bubbles with soap. But his favorite thing to do is to use the hose. The hose can make water go very far and very fast. It can make water go up in the air or down on the ground. It can make water go in different shapes and colors.
One day, Tommy's mom said he could use the hose in the backyard. Tommy was very excited. He ran to the hose and turned it on. He felt the water come out and spray his hand. He laughed and pointed the hose at the flowers. He saw the water make the flowers wet and shiny. He moved the hose to the grass and made a big puddle. He jumped in the puddle and splashed his feet.
Then he saw something else. He saw a big red ball on the other side of the fence. It was his neighbor's ball. He wanted to play with it. He had an idea. He aimed the hose at the ball and squeezed it hard. He saw the water push the ball over the fence and into his yard. He was very happy. He turned off the hose and ran to the ball. He picked it up and threw it in the air. He caught it and threw it again.
He heard a voice. It was his neighbor. He was a boy named Sam. He was looking for his ball. He saw Tommy with it. He was not angry. He was enthusiastic. He liked to play with water too. He asked Tommy if he could join him. Tommy said yes. He gave him the ball and turned on the hose again. They played together with the hose and the ball. They had a lot of fun. They became friends.

Once upon a time, there was a kind girl named Poppy. She had a pet cat named Fluffy. One day, Poppy and Fluffy went for a walk in the woods. They saw a big tree with a bird on a branch. The bird said, "Hello, Poppy and Fluffy!"
Poppy answered, "Hello, bird! How are you today?" The bird said, "I am sad. I lost my baby bird. Can you help me find her?" Poppy and Fluffy wanted to help their new friend. They looked high and low for the baby bird. They looked under rocks and behind trees.
At last, they found the baby bird near a big hole. But, they did not see the hole and fell in. Poppy, Fluffy, and the baby bird were stuck in the hole. They called for help, but no one heard them. They stayed in the hole for a long time.
Finally, a big bear heard their cries and helped them out of the hole. But the bear was not kind. He wanted to eat Poppy, Fluffy, and the baby bird. They ran away as fast as they could, but the bear caught them.
The moral of the story is to always be careful when helping others. Sometimes, it can lead to danger.

Once upon a time, there was a little fish named Fin. Fin was a very selfish fish. He did not like to share his toys with other fish. One day, Fin's friend, Bubbles, asked if he could play with one of Fin's toys.
"Can I play with your toy, please?" Bubbles asked. Fin did not want to share, but he remembered his mom encourage him to be nice. So, he said, "Okay, you can play with my toy."
While they were playing, a big fish swam by. The big fish was not feeling well. He had a hurt lung. Fin and Bubbles saw the big fish and wanted to help. They swam up to the big fish and asked, "Can we help you feel better?"
The big fish was happy for their help. Fin and Bubbles took turns bringing the big fish food and helping him swim. Soon, the big fish's lung felt better. He thanked Fin and Bubbles for their help.
Fin learned that it was good to share and help others. He was not selfish anymore. He and Bubbles played with all the fish in the sea, and they all had a great time.

Lila and Ben are playing in the garden. They have a big cloth and some sticks. They want to make a tent.
"Look, Lila, I have a good stick. It is long and strong. It can stand by itself." Ben says.
"Wow, Ben, that is a good stick. I have a good cloth. It is big and soft. It can cover the stick." Lila says.
They put the cloth over the stick and make a tent. They crawl inside and pretend they are camping.
"Hello, Lila, hello, Ben. What are you doing?" Mommy asks.
"We are camping, Mommy. We have a tent. It is cozy and fun." Lila and Ben say.
Mommy smiles and says, "That is a nice tent. Can I see?"
She bends down and looks inside. She sees the cloth and the stick. She also sees a hole in the cloth.
"Oh, no, Lila, Ben. This is a bad cloth. It has a hole. It can rip more. It can let the rain in." Mommy says.
Lila and Ben look at the hole. They feel sad.
"Sorry, Mommy. We did not see the hole. We did not mean to use a bad cloth." Lila and Ben say.
Mommy hugs them and says, "It's okay, Lila, Ben. You did not know. It was an accident. I have a good cloth. It is new and bright. It has no holes. It can make a better tent. Do you want to try?"
Lila and Ben nod and say, "Yes, please, Mommy. Thank you, Mommy."
Mommy helps them take off the bad cloth and put on the good cloth. They make a new tent. It is bigger and prettier. They crawl inside and smile.
"We love our new tent, Mommy. It is cozy and fun. And it has no holes." Lila and Ben say.
Mommy smiles and says, "I love your new tent, too. You are very clever and creative. You can play here as long as you want. But remember to be careful with the cloth. It is good, but it can still rip. And if it rains, come inside. Okay?"
Lila and Ben say, "Okay, Mommy. We will be careful. And we will come inside if it rains. We love you, Mommy."
Mommy says, "I love you, too, Lila, Ben. Have fun camping."

Once upon a time there was a very deaf rabbit named Bob. Bob was always hopping around everywhere. His family often warned him not to travel too far away. But Bob was curious and so one day he hopped away and started his journey.
On his travels, he suddenly encountered a big yellow comb in the middle of the forest. The comb looked shiny and pretty, so Bob couldn't help but stop and take a closer look at it. As he went to pick it up, he heard a voice say: "That comb is mine!"
Bob looked around and saw a tall and old tree. He realized it was the tree that was talking to him. The tree spoke again: "You can take the comb with you, but be careful. You must never use your comb in a dark and scary place or something bad will happen!"
Bob was scared and he promised he would never use his comb in a dark and scary place. He thanked the tree and continued on his journey. Bob went back home and was safe. But he never forgot the lessons he had learned and he always made sure to use his comb only in places he knew were safe.

Once upon a time, there was a big cook named Tom. He loved to cook yummy food for everyone. One day, Tom was going to the store to buy food. He saw many cars and people. It was traffic. Tom thought it was boring.
Tom walked and walked until he saw a big tree. He thought it would be fun to climb it. He climbed up and up. When he was at the top, he saw something strange. It was a big, red button.
Tom wanted to see what the button did. He pushed it. Suddenly, all the cars and people in the traffic started to dance! The cars danced, the people danced, and even the dogs danced. The boring traffic was now fun!
Everyone laughed and clapped. Tom climbed down the tree and went to the store. He bought food and cooked a big meal for all his new friends. And from that day, Tom and his friends would always remember the day the boring traffic turned into a dance party.

Once upon a time, there was a friendly cloud named Fluffy. Fluffy loved to rain on the town below. The town had a big fork in the middle of it. The fork was a special place where people met and played.
One day, Fluffy saw a little girl named Lily. Lily was sad because it was too hot to play. Fluffy wanted to help Lily. So, Fluffy rained on the fork. The rain made it cooler for Lily to play.
Lily looked up at Fluffy and said, "Thank you, Fluffy! Now I can play with my friends." Fluffy was happy to help. From that day on, Fluffy and Lily were best friends. They played together at the fork every day.

Once upon a time, there was a little bug. The little bug loved to crawl on the ground. One day, the bug went on a search for a big, healthy leaf to eat.
As the bug crawled, it met a tiny ant. The ant said, "Hi, bug! What are you doing?" The bug said, "I am on a search for a big, healthy leaf to eat. Will you help me?" The ant said, "Yes, let's go find the leaf!"
The bug and the ant crawled and crawled. They found a big, healthy leaf. They were so happy! But then, something unexpected happened. A big bird came and took the leaf away. The bug and the ant were sad. They had to start their search again, but they knew it would not be easy.

Tommy was a big boy. He did not like his crib. He wanted to sleep in a big bed like his sister. One night, he tried to climb out of his crib. He put his leg over the rail and pushed with his arms. But he slipped and fell on the floor. He hit his head and felt dizzy. He started to cry.
Mommy and Daddy heard him and ran to his room. They saw him on the floor and picked him up. They hugged him and kissed him. They asked him what happened.
"I wanted to sleep in a big bed," Tommy said. "But I fell and hurt my head."
Mommy and Daddy looked at each other. They smiled and said, "You are a brave boy, Tommy. But you are not ready for a big bed yet. You need to stay in your crib until you grow more. Your crib is safe and cozy. We love you very much."
Tommy felt embarrassed. He knew he did something silly. He said sorry to Mommy and Daddy. They said it was okay and tucked him back in his crib. They gave him his teddy bear and sang him a lullaby. Tommy felt better and closed his eyes. He dreamed of sleeping in a big bed someday.

Once upon a time, there lived an ordinary green frog who lived in an ordinary pond. Every day, he would poke his head out of the pond and croak away. One morning, he heard a loud ROAR coming from the meeting of the animals in the forest near the pond. He thought it was an ordinary roar, and he paid it no mind.
The frog soon found out that the roar was not ordinary at all. A Royal Tiger was on the prowl in their part of the forest, and he was causing all kinds of trouble. He roared at all the animals and scared them so much they all ran away. He even caused birds to fly out of nests and scared the fish out of their ponds.
The frog was scared of the Tiger and trying to think of what to do. Then he had an idea. He hopped out of the pond and croaked as loudly as he could. The Tiger thought the frog was so brave and he stopped his mighty ROAR.
The Tiger explained why he was so angry and they had a long chat. After a while, the Tiger said he was no longer angry and the animals could come back. The frog was happy his plan worked and the day ended with good news for the animals in the forest.

Once upon a time, there was a hill. It was a very tidy hill. The grass was short and the trees were neatly trimmed. Every day the sun shined brightly on the hill. Then one day, it began to rain. The rain made everything very wet. All of the grass and trees on the hill were covered in water. The gentle sound of the rain made the hill sound happy. After a while, the rain stopped and the sun came back out again. The hill looked even tidier than before. Everything glistened and sparkled in the bright daylight. The hill was so beautiful that everyone walking past would stop and admire it. From that day forward, the hill always stayed neat and tidy, no matter if it rained or shone.

Once upon a time, there was a happy girl named May. She loved to play in the park and swing on the swings. One day, May was feeling a little bit worried because she had never seen a star in the sky before.
May asked her mother, “Mama, can I see a star somewhere?” Her mother replied, “Of course you can. The stars come up at night and they look beautiful.”
May was so happy that she ran outside to the park. She lay on the ground and looked up in the sky. “I don’t believe I can see a star!” she said.
But then, something magical happened! A star began to appear! May was so excited that she jumped up and down and shouted, “A star! I can see a star!”
May looked up at the sky every night from then on. She wanted to be the first to spot a star and she was never worried about not finding one. Every night, she would start counting as soon as the sun said goodbye. And every night, she was sure to find a star. May loved believing in the stars!

Once upon a time, there was a boy named Tim. He loved to watch films on his TV. One day, Tim found a lucky coin on the floor.
Tim was so happy with his lucky coin. He held it tight and wished to be in a film. Suddenly, something unexpected happened! Tim was now inside the TV, in his favorite film.
But, Tim didn't want to stay in the film forever. He held his lucky coin again and wished to go back home. With a twist, Tim was back in his room, safe and sound. From that day on, Tim knew that his lucky coin was very special.

John had a very humble home. Every day he would pack his blocks away. He had to be careful though because his blocks were very special.
One day he put his blocks in his bag and went outside. He found some friends and they had great fun with the blocks. They all built a tall tower, and it was the tallest tower anyone had ever seen.
John and his friends had so much fun playing together with the blocks. When it was time to go home, John packed his blocks away again. He said goodbye to his friends and thanked them for a wonderful day.
John was so happy that he had been able to share his blocks with his friends. He went inside feeling very humble. He couldn't wait to play with his blocks again the next day.

One day, a nice boy named Tim found a big toy rocket in his room. He was very happy and wanted to play with it. Tim took the rocket outside to show his friends.
When he got to the park, Tim saw his friends playing with their own toys. They all laughed and had fun together. Tim showed them his new rocket and they all wanted to play with it too.
They took turns flying the rocket high in the sky. The rocket went up, up, up and then came back down. They all laughed and clapped as they watched it fly. They played with the rocket until it was time to go home. Tim and his friends had a great day with the nice rocket.

Once upon a time, there was a long road. On the road, there was a pigeon. The pigeon was sad. It had no friends.
One day, a boy saw the pigeon. The boy said, "Hi, pigeon! Do you want to play with me?" The pigeon was happy. They played all day long. But then, the boy saw a big, shiny toy. He wanted the toy.
The boy left the pigeon to get the toy. He did not look at the road. A big car came fast. The boy did not see the car. The car hit the boy. The boy was hurt. The pigeon was sad. The fun day had a bad end.
The story teaches us to look and be careful when we are near a road. We must always watch out for cars, so we do not get hurt.

Once upon a time, there was a poor bear. He lived in a small cave in the woods. One day, he found a toy that could record sounds. He was very happy and wanted to show it to his friend, the rabbit.
The poor bear went to the rabbit's home and said, "Look at my new toy! It can record sounds. Let's play with it." The rabbit was very excited and they started to record their voices. They laughed and had a lot of fun.
Later, they heard a loud noise in the woods. They were scared but the poor bear had an idea. He said, "Let's use my toy to record the loud noise. Maybe we can find out what it is." They recorded the noise and played it back. It was just a big bird singing. They laughed and were not scared anymore. The poor bear and the rabbit were very happy that they had found the toy and they played with it all day.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Bobby. He was three years old and full of life. He loved playing outside with his friends and going to nursery school.
One day, his teacher Mrs. Graham called him aside. She had a special task for Bobby. She asked him to encourage his friends in school, by teaching them new things.
Bobby was a bit shy at first, but he was also very cheerful. He loved teaching his friends things like counting, humming and jumping. He also taught them to name all the animals in the zoo.
After a few days, Bobby noticed that his friends were learning quickly and becoming more confident. Seeing this, he was filled with joy.
Mrs. Graham praised Bobby for his hard work. She said, "Well done Bobby, you are doing a great job of encouraging and teaching your friends. You should be very proud of yourself!"
Bobby smiled with pride and went back to teaching his friends more fun things. He was learning as much as he was teaching and having lots of fun along the way.

One day, a cat and a dog were playing near a big tree. The cat saw a spike on the ground and was confused. The dog looked at the spike and said, "What is that?"
The cat replied, "I don't know. Let's go find out." They walked closer to the spike, but then the cat stepped on it. "Ouch!" the cat yelled. The dog was scared and didn't know what to do.
Just then, a big bird flew down from the tree. The bird picked up the spike in its beak and flew away. The cat and the dog were surprised and happy. They said "thank you" to the bird and went back to playing. The cat was careful not to step on any more spikes.

Once upon a time, there was a huge bear. It was an enormous bear with big, fluffy fur! One day, the bear started to feel cold. He had an idea. He decided that he would go and find his friend Jack. Jack was a small mouse. He was sure that Jack could help him stay warm.
The bear went off to find Jack. He travelled through the woods and eventually came to Jack's house. He knocked on the door and called out, "Jack, it's me! Can I come in?"
Jack opened the door. He said, "Oh, it's you bear! What can I do for you?"
The bear asked, "Can I come in and stay warm? It's very cold outside."
Jack agreed, "Of course! Come in bear. I can help you stay warm."
So, the bear stepped into Jack's house. They talked and laughed for hours. Eventually, the bear was nice and warm.
The next morning, the bear said goodbye to Jack and went on his way. He was feeling warm and happy, thanks to his enormous friend Jack.

Once upon a time, there was a nosy alligator named Ally. Ally liked to fix things. One day, Ally saw a broken toy car. Ally wanted to help, so she went to fix the toy car.
Ally worked hard to fix the toy car. She put the wheels back on and made it look new again. The little boy who owned the toy car was very happy. He said, "Thank you, Ally! You fixed my toy car!"
From that day on, Ally the nosy alligator helped fix many things for her friends. Everyone loved Ally because she was kind and helpful. And Ally was happy because she liked to fix things and make her friends smile.

Lily and Max were playing soldiers in the garden. They had sticks for swords and pots for helmets. They marched around the grass, pretending to fight enemies. "We are brave and strong!" Max shouted. "We will win the war!"
But then they saw a big dog on the other side of the fence. The dog was barking loudly and showing its teeth. Lily and Max got scared and ran back to the house. They dropped their sticks and pots and hid behind the sofa. "The dog is mean and scary!" Lily cried. "We will lose the war!"
Their mom came in and saw them upset. She hugged them and said, "Don't worry, my little soldiers. The dog can't hurt you. He is just protecting his home. He doesn't know you are playing. Maybe you can make friends with him later."
She gave them some cookies and milk and said, "You did a good job marching and playing. You can have a victory party now. How about that?"
Lily and Max smiled and nodded. They forgot about the dog and enjoyed their cookies and milk. They felt brave and strong again. They said, "Thank you, mom. You are the best!"

Ben and Mia are twins. They like to play and have fun. But sometimes they do not mind Mom and Dad. They make a mess and a loud noise.
One day, Mom and Dad are making a meal for the family. They tell Ben and Mia to sit at the table and wait. But Ben and Mia do not want to wait. They want to play with their toys and run around the house.
"Ben, Mia, mind us and sit down!" Mom says. "The meal is almost ready and it is hot and yummy."
But Ben and Mia do not listen. They keep playing and running. They knock over a lamp and a vase. They make a loud crash and a loud bang.
"Ben, Mia, stop!" Dad says. "You are making a big mess and a loud noise. You are not being good and you are not minding us."
But Ben and Mia do not stop. They laugh and giggle. They think it is fun to make a mess and a loud noise.
Mom and Dad are not happy. They are sad and mad. They turn off the stove and go to the living room. They see Ben and Mia and the mess and the noise.
"Ben, Mia, look what you have done!" Mom says. "You have broken the lamp and the vase. You have made a mess and a loud noise. You have not minded us and you have not been good. You have spoiled the meal and the day."
Ben and Mia stop playing and running. They see Mom and Dad and the mess and the noise. They feel bad and sorry. They know they have done wrong and they have not minded Mom and Dad.
"Mom, Dad, we are sorry!" Ben and Mia say. "We did not mean to make a mess and a loud noise. We did not mean to spoil the meal and the day. We did not mind you and we were not good. Please forgive us and please love us."
Mom and Dad look at Ben and Mia. They see their faces and their eyes. They feel love and pity. They know Ben and Mia are sorry and they want to be good. They hug Ben and Mia and kiss them.
"Ben, Mia, we forgive you and we love you!" Mom and Dad say. "But you have to mind us and be good. You have to clean up the mess and the noise. You have to sit at the table and eat the meal. You have to say thank you and be happy."
Ben and Mia nod and smile. They say sorry and thank you. They help Mom and Dad clean up the mess and the noise. They sit at the table and eat the meal. They say it is hot and yummy. They are happy and good. They mind Mom and Dad and they have fun.

Once upon a time, in a small town, there was a very old tree. People said it was an ancient tree. It was big and strong. All the young kids, or youth, loved to play near the tree. They liked to run around it and climb its branches.
One day, a little boy named Tim wanted to play near the tree. But, he saw a big gate that was close to the tree. The gate was shut. Tim was sad. He wanted to play with his friends near the tree.
Tim asked his mom about the gate. His mom told him, "The gate is there to keep the tree safe. We must not hurt the ancient tree. We must care for it." Tim understood. He played with his friends away from the tree. They still had fun.
The moral value of the story is to respect and care for old things, like the ancient tree, and to find other ways to have fun without causing harm.

Once upon a time, there was a shy fish named Timmy. Timmy was not like other fish. He was scared of many things. One day, Timmy saw a big shark. The shark was hungry and wanted to eat Timmy.
Timmy was very scared of the shark. He did not want to be eaten. Timmy thought, "I must surrender to the shark. Then maybe he will not eat me." So, Timmy swam to the shark and said, "I give up. Please do not eat me."
The big shark looked at the shy fish and said, "I will not eat you, Timmy. I am also shy and scared. Let us be friends." Timmy was very happy. He and the shark became best friends, and they played together every day.

Once upon a time, there was a little brown dog named Spot. Spot loved to play with his favorite toy, a red brick. One sunny day, Spot went to the park to play with his brick.
At the park, Spot met a new friend, a cute cat named Fluffy. Fluffy said, "Hi Spot, what do you have there?" Spot replied, "This is my red brick. I love to play with it." Fluffy asked, "Can I play with your brick too?" Spot said, "Yes, let's play together!"
As they played, Spot and Fluffy became very good friends. When it was time to go home, Spot gave Fluffy a big kiss on the cheek. Fluffy smiled and said, "Thank you, Spot. I had so much fun today. Let's play again soon!" Spot wagged his tail and said, "Yes, let's play together every day!" And from that day on, Spot and Fluffy were the best of friends.

Once upon a time, there was a lazy cat named Tom. Tom loved to sit in the sun all day. One day, Tom heard a loud alarm. It went "beep beep beep!" Tom jumped up and looked around.
Tom saw a little bird named Sue. Sue was sitting on a tree branch. She said, "Tom, help me! The alarm is scaring me!" Tom wanted to help his friend Sue. So, he walked to the tree and tried to find the alarm.
Tom found the alarm near the tree. He used his paw to turn it off. The alarm stopped beeping. Sue said, "Thank you, Tom! You are a good friend." Tom felt happy that he could help Sue. Now, Tom and Sue could sit in the sun and enjoy the quiet day together.

One day, a small caterpillar named Tim was in the garden. He was looking for leaves to eat. He saw many leaves, but he wanted to sort them. Tim liked to eat only the best leaves.
Tim saw a big, green leaf. He thought it looked good to eat. But then, he saw a dangerous bug on the leaf. The bug was very big and had sharp teeth. Tim was scared.
"Hello, bug. Can I eat this leaf?" Tim asked the dangerous bug. The bug looked at Tim and said, "No, this is my leaf. You can't eat it." Tim was sad, but he didn't want to make the bug angry.
Tim went to find another leaf. He found a small, tasty leaf and ate it. Even though he couldn't eat the big leaf, he was still happy. The dangerous bug didn't bother Tim, and he stayed safe in the garden.

Once upon a time, in a small village, there lived a boy named Tim. Tim had a big shield that he loved to play with. One day, he saw a hurt bird on the ground.
Tim paused and said, "Bird, why are you hurt?" The bird replied, "I fell from the tree and cannot fly." Tim wanted to help the bird feel better.
So, Tim used his shield to protect the bird from the sun and rain. He took care of the bird until it could fly again. The bird thanked Tim and flew away happy. The moral of the story is: always help those who are hurt.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Sue. Sue had a favorite sock that she loved very much. One day, the sock went missing, and Sue felt sad. She looked everywhere in her home but could not find her sock. Sue's mom told her to never give up and to keep looking.
Sue went outside to look for her sock. It was a cold and icy day. She saw a small bird shivering in the cold. Sue felt bad for the bird and wanted to help it. She thought of her missing sock and had an idea.
Sue went back inside and found another sock. She put the sock on the bird to keep it warm. The bird was happy, and Sue felt happy too. Then, the bird flew away and came back with Sue's missing sock. Sue was surprised and happy. She learned that if you help others, good things can happen to you. Sue and the bird became best friends, and they both succeeded in finding warmth and happiness.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim liked to play outside in the sun. He loved to run, jump, and laugh with his friends. One day, it rained all day and Tim could not play outside. He was sad.
The next day, the rain stopped and the sky was clear. Tim went outside to play. The grass was wet, but the sun was shining and it started to dry everything. Tim was happy to feel the warm sun on his face.
Tim saw a quiet place under a big tree. He sat down and listened to the birds sing. He felt peaceful and calm. Tim loved the quiet spot and decided to come back every day. And so, he did.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim loved pizza more than anything else. One day, his mom made a big, yummy pizza for dinner. Tim was very happy and could not wait to eat it.
Before they could eat, Tim's mom said, "Tim, please seat at the table." Tim sat down and looked at the pizza. It was still hot, so he had to wait for it to get cold. While he waited, Tim's friend, Sam, came to the door. "Can I have some pizza?" Sam asked. Tim thought about it and said, "Yes, you can have some. Let's share."
Tim and Sam ate the pizza together, and it was so good. They were both very happy. The moral of the story is that sharing with friends can make you even happier than having something all to yourself.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim was not normal. He could become a spirit. When he was a spirit, he could fly and go through walls.
One day, Tim met a girl named Sue. Sue saw Tim become a spirit and said, "Wow, you can fly! Can you teach me how to do that?" Tim tried to teach Sue, but she could not become a spirit. Sue was sad and said, "I wish I could be like you."
Tim wanted to help Sue feel better. He thought, "If I can become a spirit, maybe I can help Sue become one too." So, Tim and Sue went on a journey to find a way for Sue to become a spirit. They met a wise old man who told them, "To become a spirit, you must be kind and brave."
Sue and Tim worked together to be kind and brave. They helped people and animals in need. One day, Sue became a spirit too! Sue and Tim were happy. They could now fly and go through walls together. And they lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time, there was a loud bird. The bird lived in a tree near a spring. The bird liked to sing all day long. All the animals in the forest could hear the bird.
One day, the loud bird quit singing. The animals were very confused. They went to the spring to see what happened. They saw the bird sitting quietly on a branch.
Then, they saw a tiny mouse singing near the spring. The mouse had the most beautiful voice. The loud bird was happy to listen to the mouse sing. The animals were surprised and happy. Now, they all enjoyed listening to the tiny mouse sing by the spring.

One day, Tom went to the park with his mom. They saw a big pond with ducks and a dry boat. Tom said, "Mom, can you tell me a story about the boat?"
Mom smiled and started to tell the story. "Once upon a time, there was a boat that lived in the pond. The boat was very sad because it was dry and could not float. It wanted to play with the ducks in the water."
One day, a kind girl came to the park. She saw the dry boat and said, "Why are you so sad, boat?" The boat told her, "I am dry and cannot float. I want to play with the ducks." The girl thought for a moment and then ran to get a big bucket of water.
She poured the water on the boat, and it became wet and happy. The boat could now float and play with the ducks. The boat thanked the girl and they all had fun together in the pond.
Tom loved the story and asked his mom if they could help the dry boat too. Mom said, "Yes, Tom, we can help the boat and make it wet so it can play with the ducks." They found a bucket, filled it with water, and poured it on the boat. The boat floated, and Tom was very happy.

Once upon a time, there was a selfish frog named Fred. He lived in a swamp. Fred did not like to share. He wanted the whole swamp for himself.
One day, a nice duck named Daisy came to the swamp. Daisy asked Fred, "Can I play in the swamp too?" Fred said, "No, this is my swamp. I don't want to share." Daisy felt sad and left.
Fred was happy to have the swamp all to himself. But soon, the swamp started to change. The water got dirty and the plants died. Fred did not know how to make the swamp nice again. He needed help, but no one wanted to help him because he was selfish. In the end, Fred was all alone in his dirty swamp.

Once upon a time there was a little bird named Sarah. She was a very curious bird, always looking for fun and adventure.
One day, Sarah saw a parade on the street. All of the people were smiling, clapping and cheering. She wanted to join in the fun so she flew through the air and landed in the parade.
While Sarah was in the parade, she noticed a tasty looking strawberry pie. She could not resist, and so she decided to have a little nibble.
Just then, an old wise man appeared and said to Sarah: "If you remain in the parade and do not take the pie, you will be rewarded".
Sarah thought for a moment, and then decided to stay in the parade and admire the delicious pie from a distance.
Sure enough, as Sarah remained in the parade, she was rewarded with a delicious strawberry pie from the wise man. She was so happy and enjoyed every bite!
The moral of the story is that if you remain true to your values, you will be rewarded.

Lily saw a boy with a messy sandcastle. The boy was sad. "Why are you sad?" Lily asked. "My sandcastle is messy and I can't fix it," the boy said. Lily wanted to help, so she said, "Let's fix it together!"
Lily and the boy worked hard to fix the messy sandcastle. They made it bigger and better. The boy was so happy and said, "Thank you, Lily!" They played together all day and became good friends.

Once upon a time, there was a kind hippo named Bobo. Bobo lived in a big pond with his friends. He liked to play with them all day long. One day, Bobo and his friends decided to have a fun game. They wanted to see who could weigh the most.
They took turns standing on a big rock to see how much they weighed. The rock would sink into the water when they stood on it. The more the rock sank, the more they weighed. Bobo was very big, so he thought he would win the game.
But when it was Bobo's turn to weigh, the rock sank so much that it broke. Bobo fell into the water and got hurt. His friends tried to help, but they couldn't. Bobo was too big and heavy for them. They all felt sad because their fun game had a bad ending.

Tom and Sue are friends. They like to play in the park. They have a ball, a bike and a kite. They have fun in the sun.
But then, Tom hears a sound. It is his mom. She calls him. She says it is time to go home. Tom is sad. He does not want to leave. He wants to play more.
He tells Sue he has to leave. Sue is sad too. She likes to play with Tom. She gives him a hug. She says bye-bye. She hopes to see him soon.
Tom runs to his mom. She smiles at him. She says she has a surprise for him. She has a tasty cake. It is his favorite. It has chocolate and cream. Tom is happy. He loves cake. He says thank you to his mom. He says he had a good day.
He thinks of Sue. He misses her. He hopes she has a tasty cake too. He thinks of the park. He hopes to go back soon. He thinks of the ball, the bike and the kite. He hopes to play with them again. He smiles. He is a lucky boy.

Once upon a time, there was a big yellow bird. This bird could fly very high in the sky. The bird's name was Sunny. Sunny liked to fly and play with his friends. But sometimes, Sunny felt a little boring.
One day, Sunny was flying high in the sky, looking for something fun to do. He saw a big tree with a hole in it. Sunny thought, "Maybe there is something fun in there!" So, he flew down to the tree and looked inside the hole.
When Sunny looked inside, he saw a little mouse. The mouse was sad because he couldn't fly like Sunny. Sunny had an idea! He picked up the little mouse with his feet and they started to fly together. The mouse was so happy! They flew all day and had a lot of fun. From that day on, Sunny and the mouse were best friends, and Sunny never felt boring again.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Mia. She was three years old and very brave. One day, Mia and her mommy were walking in their backyard when Mia saw something in the corner of her eye. She asked her mommy what it was.
Mia's mommy replied, "I'm not sure what it is. Let's go take a look".
So Mia and her mommy walked closer and closer until they saw an old, empty shed. Mia was scared and her hands began to tremble. She asked her mommy if it was safe.
Mia's mommy smiled and said, “Yes, it looks safe. I think something very special is hiding inside".
So Mia bravely opened the door and peeked inside. What she saw made her tremble with excitement. The shed was full of toys - all waiting just for her! Mia hugged her mommy and ran inside to play.
Now whenever Mia and her mommy look at the shed, they smile. They know that Mia's special sight of the shed brought them happiness and joy.

One day, a rich man named Bob was walking down the street. He saw a little girl named Sue who was sad. Sue lost her toy. Bob wanted to help her find it.
Bob said, "Sue, let's look for your toy together." Sue said, "No, I can find it myself." Bob insisted, "I want to help you, please let me." Sue thought about it and said, "Okay, you can help me."
They looked and looked for Sue's toy. They went to a big event where people were playing games and eating food. Finally, they found the toy under a table. Sue was very happy and said, "Thank you, Bob, for helping me find my toy!"

Once upon a time, there was a thin tree. The tree was very nice. The tree had a friend, a big rock. The rock was strong and could support the tree when it was windy.
One hot day, the sun was very bright. The tree gave shade to the rock. The rock was happy to have shade. They liked to help each other.
Every day, the tree and the rock played together. They were the best of friends. The tree and the rock lived happily ever after.

Tom and Lily were playing in the park. It was cold and windy, but they did not care. They liked to run and slide and swing. They had fun.
But then, Lily saw a big dog. The dog was not friendly. It barked and growled and ran towards them. Lily was scared. She ran to Tom and hugged him.
"Help, Tom! The dog is bad!" she cried.
Tom was brave. He saw a stick on the ground. He picked it up and waved it at the dog. He shouted, "Go away, dog! Leave us alone!"
The dog did not listen. It jumped at Tom and tried to bite him. Tom hit the dog with the stick. The dog yelped and ran away.
Tom and Lily were safe. They hugged each other and smiled.
"Thank you, Tom! You saved me!" Lily said.
"You're welcome, Lily! You are my friend!" Tom said.
They heard a voice behind them. It was their big sister, Anna. She was bossy. She wore a red coat and a hat. She had been looking for them.
"Tom! Lily! What are you doing? You should not play with bad dogs! You could get hurt! Come here, now!" she said.
Tom and Lily rolled their eyes. They did not like Anna's voice. They walked to her slowly.
"Sorry, Anna. We were just playing. We did not see the dog until it was too late. But Tom saved me. He is brave!" Lily said.
Anna looked at Tom. She saw the stick in his hand and the scratch on his arm. She felt sorry for him. She gave him a hug and a kiss.
"Wow, Tom. You are brave. You saved Lily. I am proud of you. But you should be careful. Here, take my coat. It will keep you warm." she said.
Tom smiled. He took Anna's coat and put it on. It was big and soft and red. He felt warm and happy.
"Thank you, Anna. You are nice. You are my sister!" he said.
Anna smiled. She took Tom and Lily by the hand. She led them to their home. They had hot chocolate and cookies. They told their mom and dad what happened. They were proud of Tom. They hugged him and Lily. They were a family. They loved each other.

The refrigerator became angry. It did not like that Tim was pulling on its door. But Tim did not know that the refrigerator was angry. He just wanted some food. So, he pulled and pulled again. This time, the door opened! Tim was very happy. He found some yummy food inside and ate it all up.
From that day on, Tim and the refrigerator became good friends. Tim learned how to open the door without making the refrigerator angry. And the refrigerator was always full of yummy food for Tim to eat. They lived happily ever after.

Arriving in the living room, he noticed something new - there was a big, beautiful fireplace. He ran over and started to touch it. His mom called him over, and said, “It looks so special, what do you think it can do?”
Peter said, “Maybe it can give us silly heat!” His mom laughed and said, “That’s not exactly how it works, but it can give off warmth!”
Peter smiled and said, “Let’s make a fire and get silly and warm!” His mom lit a fire, and the two of them snuggled up together in front of the fireplace and watched the flames dance and bloom.

Lily was a curious girl. She liked to look at everything and ask questions. One day, she saw a big hole in the land near her house. She wanted to know what was inside. She ran to the hole and peered over the edge.
"Hello, is anyone there?" she shouted.
She heard a loud roar from the hole. She jumped back and saw a big green head with sharp teeth and yellow eyes. It was a dragon!
"Who are you?" the dragon asked.
"I'm Lily. Who are you?" Lily asked.
"I'm Rex. This is my home. You should not be here. You are too small and tasty. I might eat you," Rex said.
Lily was scared, but also curious. She wanted to talk to the dragon more. She thought he might be lonely.
"Please don't eat me. I just wanted to say hello. Do you have any friends?" Lily asked.
Rex shook his head. He said he had no friends. He said everyone was afraid of him and ran away. He said he was sad and angry.
Lily felt sorry for Rex. She said she would be his friend if he was nice to her. She said she liked dragons and wanted to learn more about them.
Rex smiled. He said he liked Lily and wanted to be her friend too. He said he would not eat her or hurt her. He said he would teach her about dragons and show her his treasures.
Lily was happy. She said she would visit Rex again soon. She said she would bring him some cookies and books. She said she would tell her mom and dad about him.
Rex was happy too. He said he would wait for Lily and be good to her. He said he would protect her from any danger. He said he would share his treasures with her.
Lily and Rex became friends. They talked and played and learned from each other. They were curious and kind. They were not afraid of each other.
But one day, Lily's mom and dad found out about Rex. They were angry and scared. They said Lily was naughty and foolish. They said Rex was dangerous and evil. They said Lily could not see Rex anymore. They said they would punish her if she did.
Lily was sad and angry. She said Rex was her friend and not bad. She said she wanted to see him and help him. She said her mom and dad were wrong and mean.
Lily and Rex missed each other. They wanted to be together. They wanted to be happy. They wanted to be free.

The cop smiled and said, "Hello, Tom. My coat is too hot for this sunny day. But I like watching you spring. It makes me happy."
Tom had an idea. He said, "I can help you, Mr. Cop! Let's take off your coat and put it under that tree. Then you can watch me spring, and you won't be hot anymore."

Once there was a little boy named Tom. Tom was only 3 years old and loved to play all day long. On this particular day he found an old jar of cream in his backyard. He was so excited, he ran back inside with it in hand.
When he opened the jar, his face lit up with joy. It was filled with a clear, sticky cream. He could not wait to eat it.
Tom struggled to open the jar but eventually he was able to get it open. He dug his finger into the jar and scooped up a big spoonful. But as he began to eat it, he realised it was bitter and sour. He quickly spat it out.
Tom was so sad, he didn't want to play anymore. He had wasted the jar of cream and he never got to taste it. He put the jar outside and left it there, never wanting to see it again. And that was the bad ending.

Once upon a time, there was a thick tree in a big forest. Many animals lived near the tree. They were all good friends. One day, they found a big apple under the tree. It was very red and looked tasty. They all wanted to eat the apple.
A little mouse said, "Let's use a spell to cut the apple into small parts. Then, we can all eat it." The animals thought this was a good idea. They all said the spell together. But something unexpected happened. The apple did not cut into small parts. Instead, it started to rot.
The animals were very sad. They did not want to eat a rot apple. Then, a wise old owl came to help. The owl said, "You said the spell wrong. Let's try it again." The animals said the spell again, but this time with the owl's help. Suddenly, the rot apple turned back into a big, red, and tasty apple.
The animals were so happy. They thanked the wise owl and shared the apple with all their friends. From that day on, they learned to be more careful with spells. And they all lived happily ever after.

One day, their mom took them to the toy store to buy a new toy. Lily and Ben saw many things they liked, but they both wanted the same thing: a fierce dinosaur that could roar and bite. They both grabbed the dinosaur and pulled hard.
"Mine!" Lily shouted.
"No, mine!" Ben shouted.
"Stop, stop!" their mom said. "You can't both have the dinosaur. You have to share or choose something else."
But Lily and Ben did not want to share or choose something else. They wanted the dinosaur. They kept pulling and shouting.
The mom sighed. She looked around the toy store and saw something that made her smile. She walked over to a shelf and picked up a small blue vehicle that looked like the zoom-zoom car, but smaller. She brought it to Lily and Ben.
"Look, I found another zoom-zoom car!" she said. "Now you can each have one. How about we buy this one and the dinosaur, and you can take turns playing with them?"
Lily and Ben looked at the small blue vehicle. It was cute and shiny and made the same noises as the big red one. They let go of the dinosaur and reached for the vehicle.
"Wow, thank you, mom!" they said.
They hugged their mom and smiled. They were happy to have a new toy and to play with their old one. They decided to share the dinosaur and the zoom-zoom cars. They put them in a basket and went to the cashier. They had a fun day at the toy store.

The monster was very scary and the little girl was very scared. She was about to run away when the monster said to her, "Please don't be afraid. If you can jump through my hoop, I will close the ground again."
The little girl was brave and decided to try again. She jumped and jumped, but still didn't make it. The monster said, "Let me help you," and he waved his hand and the hoop got bigger. The little girl was able to jump through it and the monster closed the ground again.
The little girl was so happy she thanked the monster and ran back home. She never had to worry about the monster again.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl called Mary. Mary had a special present - a beautiful shiny copper box with a shimmering lid.
She was so excited she ran straight over to show her mum. "Look mummy! I have a copper box! What's inside?"
Her mum smiled and said, "You'll have to cover the box to find out, sweetheart."
Mary was confused. "Cover? What's that?"
"It means you need to put the lid on the box - like this," her mum said as she put the lid in place.
Mary did the same and, as the lid clicked into place, a bright light shone from the box, making Mary gasp in awe. "Mummy, what is it?"
"Impressive, isn't it?" her mum said. "Open the box and find out!"
And that's what Mary did. Inside the box was a magical surprise - a special gift only for her!

Sam ran to him and saw what happened. He felt sorry for his friend. He said, "Don't cry, Tim. It's okay. I will comfort you. Here, take my laser. You can play with it. I don't need it. I have you."
Tim looked at Sam and smiled. He said, "Thank you, Sam. You are very kind. You are a good friend. I'm sorry I broke my laser. I will share mine with you when I get a new one."
Sam hugged Tim and said, "You're welcome, Tim. You don't have to be sorry. It was an accident. Lasers are just toys. Friends are more important."
The moral value of the story is: Friendship is more valuable than material things. Friends should comfort each other when they are sad or hurt. Friends should share and be generous with each other.

Once upon a time there was a little girl called Daisy. Daisy was very persistent – no matter how hard something was, she would never give up. One day, Daisy was playing in her garden when she discovered something special. It was a gem! Daisy was so excited when she found the gem; she wanted to sort it and see what it looked like.
"It's pretty, isn’t it?" said her mum with a big smile on her face.
"Yes, it is," Daisy replied. She was very pleased with her find.
Daisy and her mum spent the afternoon sorting the gem. Daisy picked it up and held it in her hands, turning it over to examine it more carefully. Finally, they were able to make out the shape of a star. Daisy was so excited – she had never seen such a beautiful gem before.
Daisy thanked her mum for helping her. Then, with a smile on her face, she put the gem away in a safe place and promised to keep it forever. Daisy's persistence had paid off – now she had a beautiful gem to treasure.

Once upon a time, in a big jungle, there was a big lion. The lion had a dirty mane. He was not happy about it. He wanted to be clean and shiny. One day, he met a little bird. The bird was holding an arrow.
"Hello, bird," the lion said. "Why do you have an arrow?"
"I found it on the ground," the bird replied. "I want to use it to help my friends."
The lion had an idea. He asked the bird, "Can you help me? I want to be clean. Can you use the arrow to clean my dirty mane?"
The bird agreed to help the lion. She used the arrow to pick out the dirt from the lion's mane. It took a long time, but the lion was very patient. When the bird was done, the lion's mane was clean and shiny.
The lion was so happy, he let out a big roar. He thanked the bird for her help. From that day on, the lion and the bird became the best of friends.

Sara liked to feed the birds in the park. She had a bag of bread and seeds that she broke into small pieces. She threw them on the grass and watched the birds come and eat. She saw sparrows, pigeons, ducks and a big black crow.
One day, a woman came to the park. She was old and wore a long coat and a hat. She had a big stick that she used to walk. She looked angry and bitter. She did not like the birds or the children. She shouted at Sara and said, "Stop feeding those dirty birds! They make a mess and they are noisy. Go away and leave me alone!"
Sara was scared and sad. She did not understand why the woman was so mean. She loved the birds and wanted to make them happy. She said, "But the birds are hungry and they are my friends. They do not hurt anyone. Why are you so bitter?"
The woman snorted and said, "You are a silly girl. You do not know anything. The birds are pests and they spread diseases. They are not your friends. They only want your food. You should not waste your bread and seeds on them. You should give them to me. I am hungry and I have no money."
Sara felt sorry for the woman. She thought maybe the woman was bitter because she was hungry and lonely. She said, "I have some bread and seeds left. You can have some if you want. But please do not be mean to the birds. They are also hungry and lonely. Maybe they can be your friends too."
The woman was surprised and confused. She did not expect Sara to be so kind and generous. She took some bread and seeds from Sara's bag and ate them. They were stale and dry, but they filled her stomach. She looked at the birds and saw them eating too. They were colorful and lively, and they made cheerful sounds. She felt a little warmth in her heart. She said, "Thank you, Sara. You are a good girl. Maybe the birds are not so bad after all. Maybe we can share this park and be friends."

This made the dog sad. He didn't want to fight with the cat, he just wanted to play. So the dog sat down and started to cry. The cat saw the dog was sad and felt bad for teasing him.
"Sorry, dog," said the cat. "I didn't mean to make you sad. Let's play together and be friends." The dog stopped crying and wagged his tail. They played together all day and became the best of friends.

Once upon a time there was a twig. It was an ordinary twig, just lying on the ground. Suddenly, something amazing happened. The twig started to slip! First it slid a little bit then it started to move faster and faster. It slid and bounced across the ground until it disappeared from sight.
Everyone was so surprised and amazed that an ordinary twig could move like that. They couldn't figure out why it had started to slip. It was a big mystery!
Finally, they discovered the answer. It turned out that a friendly hedgehog had been pushing the twig with his tiny feet! He had been trying to have some fun sliding the twig around and he was so happy that it had worked.
From then on, the twig was known as the "twig that slipped", and its story was told to everyone!

Once there was a girl who liked to put things in her room. She liked her room to be neat and organized. She kept all of her favorite things in there, and it was very special to her.
One day, she went to play at a park and she found a rock. She wanted to keep it, so she put it in her room. But it was rough, and she got a little bit of scratch.
The rock was too rough for her room. She wanted to keep it, so she decided to put it outside her house. But the next morning the rock was gone! Someone had taken it away.
The girl was very sad that someone had taken something that was hers. She learned that even though it is not nice to take things without permission, sometimes it is even worse to keep something that doesn't belong to you.
Moral of the story: Respect other people's things, and always remember to put back what isn't yours.

Once upon a time, there was a big building with an elevator. The elevator went up and down, taking people to different floors. One day, a little boy named Tim went inside the elevator with his mom. Tim was very light and small.
As the elevator went up, Tim started to jump. His mom said, "Tim, please stop jumping in the elevator." But Tim did not listen. He kept jumping, and the elevator stopped working. Tim and his mom were stuck inside.
A kind man named Sam heard them call for help. He knew how to save them. Sam pressed a button on the outside of the elevator, and it started working again. The elevator door opened, and Tim and his mom were free. Tim said, "Thank you, Sam!" From that day on, Tim never jumped in the elevator again.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Sue. Sue loved to play with her toy car. One day, her toy car broke. Sue was sad. She wanted to repair her toy car.
Sue went to her dad for help. Her dad said they needed a special mineral to fix the toy car. They went outside to look for the mineral. They were very alert, looking all around.
Then, something unexpected happened. A little squirrel came by with the special mineral in its mouth! The squirrel gave the mineral to Sue. Sue and her dad used it to repair the toy car. Sue was so happy, and she played with her toy car all day long.

Once upon a time, there was a little pink flower. It wanted to bloom, but it was too shy. It was friends with a big tree who said, "Wait for Tuesday, it will be a good day to bloom."
The pink flower was excited for Tuesday. It knew that it would be a special day. The big tree told the pink flower stories about other flowers that bloomed on Tuesday. The pink flower could not wait for Tuesday to come.
Finally, Tuesday came. The sun was shining, and the pink flower felt brave. It bloomed into a beautiful pink flower. The big tree was right; Tuesday was the perfect day to bloom. The pink flower was so happy it bloomed and felt proud of itself.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim liked to pretend he was a big strong lion. One day, Tim saw a big tree and wanted to climb it.
Tim asked his mom, "Can I climb the big tree?" His mom said, "No, Tim. It is not safe. You might fall." Tim felt sad but he listened to his mom. He went to his room and put on his comfortable lion costume.
Later, Tim went outside and saw the big tree again. He thought, "I can pretend to be a big strong lion and climb the tree." So, Tim started to climb the tree. He felt happy and brave. But then, he fell from the tree and got hurt. Tim cried and wished he had listened to his mom.

One day, a boy named Tim had an idea. He wanted to visit his friend Max who lived in the big house down the road. Tim thought it would be fun to play with Max's toys and eat snacks together. So, he put on his shoes and started to walk to Max's house.
On the way, Tim saw a big dog. The dog was very big and had a terrible smell. Tim was scared of the dog, but he wanted to be brave. So, he tried to walk past the dog, hoping it would not see him. But the dog saw Tim and started to follow him.
Tim ran as fast as he could to Max's house. When he got there, he knocked on the door. Max opened the door and Tim went inside. They played with toys and ate snacks. But then, the big dog came to the door. Max's mom opened the door and the dog went inside. The dog was very hungry and ate all the snacks. Tim and Max were sad because they had no more snacks to eat.

Tom and Anna like to play in the park. They like to swing, slide and run. One day, they see a big sign in the park. The sign says: "Number Game. Find the numbers in the park and win a prize."
Tom and Anna want to play the game. They look for numbers on the trees, the benches and the flowers. They find one, two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight, nine and ten. They are very happy.
They go to the man with the sign. He has a box of prizes. He says: "Well done, kids. You found all the numbers. You can choose one prize each."
Tom and Anna look at the prizes. There are balls, books, dolls, cars and stickers. Tom likes the ball. He says: "I want the ball. It is red and round and bouncy."
Anna likes the stickers. She says: "I want the stickers. They are shiny and pretty and fun."
But there is a problem. There is only one ball and one pack of stickers. The man says: "Sorry, kids. There is only one of each. You have to share or choose something else."
Tom and Anna are sad and angry. They both want the same prize. They start to argue. They say: "I saw it first. It is mine. Give it to me."
The man says: "Stop, kids. Don't fight. You worked together to find the numbers. You should be proud of yourselves. Why don't you share the prizes? You can take turns playing with the ball and the stickers. That way, you can both have fun and be friends."
Tom and Anna think about it. They realize the man is right. They say: "OK, we will share. Thank you, mister. You are very nice."
They take the ball and the stickers and go back to the park. They play with the ball and the stickers. They have fun and they are friends. They are proud of themselves. They are happy.

One day, a boy went for a walk in the park. He looked around and saw a squirrel up in a tree. "Wow," the boy exclaimed, "It's so high!"
The boy decided to try and guess how high the squirrel was. He carefully looked up in the sky, trying to figure it out.
Suddenly, the squirrel jumped down from the tree and sat near the boy. The boy was startled. "Oh, hello," he said.
The squirrel nodded its head and said, "Guess how high I can jump?"
The boy was intrigued. He took a step back and measured the distance from the squirrel to the ground. He thought for a moment and said, "I guess you can jump very high!"
The squirrel nodded its head and said, "You guessed right!" Then, it jumped back in the tree and scurried away.
The boy smiled and continued walking. He had a feeling he'd be seeing the squirrel again.

Once upon a time, in a small house, there lived a lazy cat. The cat liked to sleep all day long. One day, the cat heard a voice from the kitchen. It was a little bird who was stuck in a big pot.
The bird said, "Help me, please!" The lazy cat did not want to help, but the voice was so sad. The cat went to the kitchen and saw the bird in the pot. The cat tried to stir the pot to help the bird get out.
But the cat was too lazy and did not stir the pot well. The bird was still stuck. The cat went back to sleep, leaving the bird alone. The bird tried to get out, but it could not. The bird was sad and stayed in the pot forever.
