One day, Tim met a girl named Sue. Sue saw Tim's coal and asked, "What is that?" Tim said, "This is my coal toy. I like to pretend it is a car, a plane or a boat." Sue said, "Can I play with you?" Tim said, "Yes, let's pretend together!"
Tim and Sue played with the coal. They pretended it was a car, a plane, and a boat. They had so much fun with the unusual toy. They became best friends and played pretend every day.

One day, a little girl named Lily was sad. Her favorite bathrobe was lost. She looked everywhere in her room, but she could not find it. She asked her mom, "Mom, can you help me find my bathrobe?"
Her mom said, "Let's look together." They looked in the closet and under the bed, but the bathrobe was not there. Lily felt even more sad.
Then, her little brother Tommy came into her room. He was wearing her bathrobe! He said, "I found it! I wanted to surprise you." Lily was happy and gave him a big hug. They both laughed, and Lily was not sad anymore.

Once upon a time, there was a cute little dog named Spot. Spot loved to play and run all day. He had many friends in the park.
One day, Spot saw a big box in his house. It was a new TV! Spot's family was very happy. They turned it on and watched funny shows together. Spot liked to sit close and watch the colors on the screen.
Spot's favorite show was about a big, friendly bear who loved to hug. When the bear hugged his friends, Spot would hug his family too. They all laughed and hugged each other. Spot loved his new TV and the happy times it brought.

One day, a little monkey went to play outside. He was a fine monkey who loved to have fun. The sun was bright and the sky was blue. The monkey was very happy.
As the monkey played, he saw a big tree. He wanted to climb it. Up, up, up he went. At the top of the tree, he saw a bird. The bird was singing a pretty song. The monkey wanted to clap for the bird.
The monkey clapped his hands and the bird stopped singing. The bird looked at the monkey and they both smiled. The monkey and the bird became friends. They played together all day long. At the end of the day, the monkey and the bird went back to their homes. They were very happy to have a new friend.

Once upon a time, in a land of hot summer days, there lived a little boy named Tim. Tim loved to play outside with his friends. They would run, jump, and laugh all day long. But one day, the summer was terrible. It was so hot that Tim and his friends could not play outside.
Tim was sad. He wanted to find a way to make the hot summer days fun again. He thought and thought, and then he had an idea. Tim would supply cold treats for everyone. He asked his mom for help. She said yes and helped him make ice pops for all his friends.
The next day, Tim gave the ice pops to his friends. They were so happy! They could play outside and cool down with the ice pops. The terrible summer was not so terrible anymore. Tim and his friends played and laughed all day long, and they were happy again.

Anna loved to play on the computer. She liked to watch funny videos of animals and draw pictures with colors. But her mom did not permit her to use the computer all the time. She said Anna had to do other things, like read books, play outside, and help with chores.
One day, Anna was very bored. She had read all her books, it was raining outside, and she had no chores to do. She asked her mom if she could use the computer for a little while. Her mom said no, she had to save the computer for later, when her dad came home from work. He had a big project to finish and he needed the computer.
Anna was miserable. She did not understand why her dad needed the computer so much. She thought he could finish his project on paper, like she did with her homework. She decided to sneak into the office and use the computer when her mom was not looking. She thought she could watch one video and then turn it off.
She tiptoed into the office and turned on the computer. She saw a lot of words and numbers on the screen. She did not know what they meant. She clicked on a button that said "delete". She thought it would make the words and numbers go away and let her watch a video.
But instead, the computer made a loud beep and the screen went black. Anna was scared. She had broken the computer. She heard her mom coming. She ran out of the office and hid under her bed.
Her mom saw the black screen and the delete button. She knew what had happened. She was very angry and sad. She called her dad and told him the bad news. He was very angry and sad too. He said he had to start his project all over again and he might lose his job.
Anna heard her mom and dad talking. She felt very sorry and guilty. She came out of her hiding place and said she was sorry. She said she did not mean to break the computer. She said she just wanted to watch a video.
Her mom and dad hugged her and said they loved her. They said they were sorry too. They said they should have explained to her why the computer was important and why she could not use it. They said they would try to fix the computer and finish the project. They said they would also try to spend more time with her and play with her.
Anna was happy. She said she loved them too. She said she would not touch the computer again without their permission. She said she would do other things, like read books, play outside, and help with chores. She said she would also make a picture for her dad to cheer him up.
The end.

Once upon a time, there was a magic ball. A boy named Tim found the ball in his yard. Tim was very happy. He wanted to play with his new ball.
He called his friend, Sam. "Sam, come see my magic ball!" Tim said. Sam came running over. They both played with the magic ball. They would kick the ball and laugh. They had so much fun.
One day, Tim felt worried. He did not want to lose his magic ball. So, he hid it in a box. But when he opened the box later, the magic ball was gone. Tim was very sad. He never found his magic ball again.

Once upon a time, there was a girl named Mia. Mia liked to draw pictures. One day, she drew a picture of a big tree with many leaves. She showed it to her friend Sam.
"Look at my picture, Sam!" said Mia. Sam looked at the picture and smiled. "I see it, Mia! It is a big tree, and it looks very nice," said Sam.
Mia and Sam liked to play under the tree. They knew the tree was reliable because it gave them shade on hot days. They liked to sit and draw more pictures under the tree.
One day, a new kid named Tim came to play with them. Tim didn't know if the tree was reliable. Mia and Sam told him, "Don't worry, Tim. This tree is very good. It keeps us cool on hot days."
Tim played with Mia and Sam under the tree. They all drew pictures together. Tim learned that the tree was reliable, just like his new friends Mia and Sam. They all learned that it is good to trust and be kind to each other.

Once upon a time, there was a little white pig named Pippa. Pippa lived on a farm with many other animals. She had a lot of friends, like the cow, the sheep, and the chicken.
One day, Pippa wanted to play with her ball. But she could not find it. She went to her friend, the cow, and asked, "Can I borrow your ball, please?" The cow said, "Of course, Pippa! Here you go." Pippa thanked the cow and went to play with the ball.
Pippa had a lot of fun playing with the ball. She kicked it and rolled it all around the farm. Her friends joined her and they all played together. After a while, Pippa found her own ball, and she returned the borrowed ball to the cow. They all played together until the sun went down, and then they went to sleep, happy and tired from their fun day.

Once there was a little girl named Sarah who liked to take walks in the park. She was reliable and she liked to look at the leaves. Today the sun was shining and there were lots of leaves on the ground.
Sarah took a deep breath and chsaoged her eyes around, but she couldn't find a leaf to pick up. All of a sudden, she heard snoring coming from a nearby tree. She couldn't believe it! Peering around the tree, she saw a little bird yawning away.
Sarah said "Hello! What are you doing?"
The bird scratched its head and said, "I'm so tired! I'm just taking a little nap under this leaf".
Sarah said "That's a good idea! Your leaf looks really comfortable."
The bird smiled and said, "It sure is! Thank you for noticing".
From that day on, Sarah and the bird were reliable friends. Sometimes she would bring the bird some of her favorite leaves, and the bird always thanked her with a gentle yawn.

Once upon a time, a little boy named Tim went for a walk. He wanted to collect rocks on a trail. He put on his shoes and went out of the house.
On the trail, Tim met a big dog. The dog said, "I am lost. Can you help me find my way back home?" Tim said, "Yes, I will help you. Let's go together." So, Tim and the dog walked down the trail to find the dog's home.
They walked and walked, and then they saw a big tree. The tree was dead. It was very scary. Suddenly, the dog said, "I am not a dog. I am a wolf!" The wolf was very bad. Tim was very scared. The wolf ran away, and Tim was all alone on the trail.

Tom and Sam are brothers. They like to play in the garden. One day, they find a big red pepper. They do not know what it is.
"Look, Sam, a red ball!" Tom says.
"No, Tom, it is not a ball. It is food. I saw Mom put it in the salad," Sam says.
"Food? Can we taste it?" Tom asks.
"Sure, why not? Let's try it," Sam says.
They each take a bite of the pepper. They make a funny face. The pepper is very spicy. It burns their mouth and tongue. They run to the kitchen. They need water.
"Mom, Mom, help! We ate a spicy sock!" Tom cries.
"A spicy sock? What do you mean?" Mom asks.
She sees the pepper in their hands. She laughs.
"That is not a sock, boys. That is a pepper. It is very spicy. You should not eat it like that. Here, drink some water. It will make you feel better," Mom says.
She gives them a glass of water. They drink it. They feel better.
"Sorry, Mom. We did not know. It looked like a ball," Tom says.
"It's okay, boys. Next time, ask me before you taste something new. And please, do not eat socks. They are not food. They are for your feet," Mom says.
She hugs them. They hug her back.
They learned a new word today. Spicy. They do not like spicy food. They like sweet food. Like cookies. Mom gives them a cookie. They smile. They taste the cookie. It is yummy. They are happy.

Once upon a time, in a distant land, there was a little boy named Tim. Tim liked to play outside and enjoy the sun. He loved to run and jump in the grass.
One day, Tim found a shiny thing in the grass. It was a razor. Tim didn't know what it was, but he thought it was pretty. He decided to take it home and show his mom.
When Tim showed his mom the razor, she got worried. She told him that it was not safe to play with. Tim learned that some things can look pretty but be dangerous. The moral of the story is to be careful with things you don't know and always ask for help from a grown-up.

Lily and Ben are friends. They like to play together. Today they have paint and paper. They want to make art.
Lily says, "I will paint a flower. Flowers are beautiful. What will you paint, Ben?"
Ben says, "I will paint a face. Faces are beautiful too. I will paint your face, Lily."
Lily says, "Okay, but be careful. Don't make me look funny."
Ben dips his brush in the paint. He paints Lily's face on the paper. He paints her eyes, her nose, her mouth and her hair. He uses different colors. He thinks Lily is very beautiful.
Lily looks at the paper. She sees her face. She smiles. She says, "Thank you, Ben. You are a good painter. You made me look beautiful."
Ben says, "You're welcome, Lily. You are a good friend. You make me happy."
They hug and laugh. They are happy. They have paint and paper. They have art. They have each other.

One day, a little girl named Lucy felt sick. She had a bad cough and her head hurt. Her mom saw that Lucy was not feeling well, so she called the doctor. The doctor was a nice man who helped make people feel better.
The doctor came to Lucy's house and looked at her. He said, "Lucy, you have a serious cough. But don't worry, I will give you some medicine to help you improve." Lucy's mom gave her the medicine, and Lucy started to feel better.
The next day, Lucy's friend Tom came to play. They played with toys and ran in the yard. Tom said, "I'm glad the doctor helped you improve, Lucy. Now we can have fun together again!" Lucy was happy that she was feeling better, and she thanked the doctor for helping her.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. He liked to play in the yard with his rake. The rake was rough, but Tim did not mind. He would tell his mom all about how he made big piles of leaves with it.
One day, while Tim was playing, he saw a big, pretty bird. The bird was sitting on the fence. Tim wanted to tell his mom about the bird, so he ran inside. When he was gone, the bird saw the rake and thought it was a stick.
The bird picked up the rake with its beak and flew away. Tim came back outside and saw that his rake was gone. He was very sad. He could not make big piles of leaves anymore. The bird had taken his rake, and Tim never found it again.

Once upon a time, there was a bulb. It was very delicate and tiny. It was so small that you could barely see it. But the bulb was special, for it had the power to grow.
One day, a special friend found the bulb. When he saw the bulb, he smiled, for he knew its potential. With love, he wrapped the delicate bulb in a cloth and planted it in his garden.
He watered the bulb everyday and soon, it started to grow. Every morning, the bulb grew a little bit bigger and it also started to change colour. First, it was yellow, and then it became pink.
Pretty soon, the bulb had fully bloomed! All of its friends were amazed at how pretty it was. The bulb was now a beautiful flower, delicate and strong.
The bulb and its special friend were very happy and celebrated the day, for they both knew that with love and care, even the smallest things can grow!

Once upon a time, there was a brave bird named Bob. Bob loved to soar high up in the sky. Every day, he would fly and play with his friends.
One day, while flying, Bob saw a big box on the ground. He was curious and flew down to see what was inside. To his surprise, the box was full of soap! Bob didn't know what soap was, but he thought it looked fun.
Bob decided to play with the soap. He picked up a bar of soap in his beak and flew up high. Then, something unexpected happened. The soap started to make bubbles! Bob was amazed and so happy. He and his friends played with the bubbles all day, soaring through the sky with their new, bubbly toy. And that's how Bob the brave bird found a new way to have fun in the sky.

Sally saw her friend, Timmy, at the party. They played games and had a lot of fun. Sally told Timmy, "I am so excited to eat the tasty cake!" Timmy was excited too. They both knew they needed to use their napkins when they ate.
After playing, they sat down to eat the cake. Sally and Timmy remembered what Sally's mom said about using the napkin. They used their napkins to clean their hands and face after eating the tasty cake. They were happy that they listened to Sally's mom. The party was a lot of fun and they had a great time.

Sam and Mia like to play on the beach. They make sand castles and look for shells. They are happy and lively.
One day, they see a big wave coming. It is the tide. The tide is the water that goes up and down on the beach. Sometimes it is low and sometimes it is high.
"Look, Sam, the tide is high!" Mia says. "It is coming to our sand castles!"
"Oh no, Mia, we have to save them!" Sam says. "The tide will take them away!"
They run to their sand castles and try to move them to a higher place. But the tide is faster than them. It reaches their sand castles and washes them away.
"Bye bye, sand castles!" Mia says. "We will make new ones tomorrow!"
Sam and Mia are sad, but they are also brave. They hug each other and watch the tide go back. They see some shells and stones that the tide left behind.
"Look, Mia, the tide gave us some gifts!" Sam says. "Let's take them and make a necklace!"
"OK, Sam, that sounds fun!" Mia says. "The tide is not so bad after all!"
They pick up the shells and stones and make a necklace. They wear it and smile. They are happy and lively again.

Spot went to Sue and said, "Look, I have a heel. Do you want to be my friend?" Sue looked at the heel and said, "Yes, I like how organized it is. Let's be friends!" They played together all day at the park.
Then, something unexpected happened. The heel started to move! It was not just a heel, but a baby turtle! The turtle said, "Thank you for finding me. Now, we can all be friends!" Spot, Sue, and the turtle became the best of friends and had many more fun days at the park.

Once upon a time, there was a kind family who lived on a farm. They had a chicken named Chicky and every day the father from the family would weigh Chicky.
One day, when the father was about to weigh Chicky, she asked a question: "Why do you weigh me every day?". The father replied in a gentle, kind voice: "I weigh you so I know how healthy you are and how much food you need for the day".
Chicky thought about this for a moment and was happy and content again. The father then weighed Chicky and found that she was big and strong, perfect for a happy little chicken. And so, the kind family went back to their daily farm routines.

Once upon a time, there was a lazy cat named Tom. Tom liked to sleep all day in the sun. He did not like to play or run.
One day, a big red car came down the street. The car made a loud noise that woke Tom up. Tom did not like the noise, so he decided to run after the car.
Tom ran and ran, but the car was too fast. He stopped to rest under a tree. As he lay there, he saw other cats playing and running. Tom thought it was fun and joined them. From that day on, Tom was not a lazy cat anymore. He loved to run and play with his new friends.

Once upon a time, there was a sleepy cat named Tom. He lived in a small house with his friend, a little girl named Sue. Sue loved to play with Tom, but sometimes Tom was too sleepy to play.
One day, Sue was making sauce for her lunch. She put the sauce in a bowl and went to get a spoon. While she was gone, Tom woke up and saw the sauce. He was very hungry, so he went to the bowl to eat the sauce. But the sauce was hot! Tom jumped back and knocked the bowl over. The sauce spilled all over the floor.
Sue came back and saw the mess. She was not mad at Tom. Instead, she laughed and said, "Let's clean this up and go play outside!" So they cleaned up the sauce and went outside to play. Tom was not sleepy anymore, and they played all day. Tom learned to speed around the yard, chasing butterflies and having fun with Sue. And they lived happily ever after.

Once upon a time, in a small town, there was a law that said no one could release their pet outside. A little boy named Tim had a pet bird named Blue. Tim thought this law was difficult because he knew Blue wanted to fly outside.
One sunny day, Tim decided to take Blue to the park. He wanted to let Blue fly and be happy. Tim looked around to make sure no one was watching. Then, he opened the cage and released Blue into the sky. Blue flew up and down, and Tim was happy to see his friend enjoying the outside.
After playing in the park, Tim and Blue went back home. Tim knew he broke the law, but he also knew that Blue needed to fly outside. From that day on, Tim and Blue would go to the park to play together. They knew it was difficult to follow the law, but they also knew that sometimes, it was important to do what was best for their friends.

Once upon a time, there was a mysterious pilot. He flew a big plane in the sky. One day, he saw a big cloud. The cloud was so big and fluffy. The pilot wanted to see what was inside the cloud.
He flew his plane into the cloud. Inside, he saw a big, shiny star. The star was stuck in the cloud. The pilot wanted to help the star. He got a long rope and tied it to the star. Then, he tried to pull the star out of the cloud.
As he pulled and pulled, the star began to move. Suddenly, the cloud opened up and the star was free! But, the star was not a star at all. It was a big, shiny balloon! The pilot laughed and took the balloon with him. He showed it to all the kids on the ground, and they were so happy to play with the big, shiny balloon. The mysterious pilot became a hero to the kids, and they all loved him.

One day, Tom and Sue saw a big box. It was shaking and making noise. Tom and Sue were scared. They did not know what was in the box. Tom started to tremble. Sue told Tom to open the box. Tom did not want to, but Sue was bossy.
Tom opened the box. It was not scary at all. It was just a little bird. The bird was stuck in the box. Tom and Sue helped the bird get out. The bird was happy. Tom, Sue, and the bird became friends. They played together every day.

Lucy saw the melon and she stared at it. She thought it was the biggest melon she had ever seen! She really wanted to take it home. She asked her mom if they could buy the melon. Her mom said yes.
They took the melon home and Lucy was very happy. She showed the melon to her dad and her little brother. They all ate the melon together. It was sweet and juicy. They all liked it very much.

Lily and her mom went to a big party at her aunt's house. There were many people, some she knew and some she did not. Lily saw her cousin Jake, who was four years old. He was playing with a toy car on the floor.
"Hi, Jake!" Lily said. "Can I play with you?"
"Sure, Lily. This is my new car. It goes very fast. Look!" Jake said. He pushed the car across the carpet and made a loud vroom sound.
Lily smiled and picked up another car. She and Jake raced their cars and had fun.
Lily's mom came to check on them. She was holding a glass of wine. Wine was a drink for grown-ups, Lily knew. It was red and smelled funny.
"Are you having a good time, sweetie?" Lily's mom asked.
"Yes, mom. Jake and I are playing with cars. They are very cool," Lily said.
"That's nice. Do you want some juice?" Lily's mom asked.
"Yes, please. Thank you, mom," Lily said.
Lily's mom gave her a cup of juice and kissed her on the cheek. She looked around the room and saw a lady in a blue dress. She was graceful, Lily thought. She moved like a dancer.
"Who is that lady, mom?" Lily asked.
"That lady is your aunt's friend. She is a ballet teacher. She teaches people how to dance," Lily's mom said.
"Wow. She is very pretty. Can she teach me how to dance?" Lily asked.
"Maybe. Why don't you go and say hello to her? She might like to meet you," Lily's mom said.
Lily nodded and put down her car. She walked over to the lady in the blue dress. She was smiling and talking to another lady.
"Excuse me, miss. I'm Lily. I'm Jake's cousin. You are very graceful. Can you teach me how to dance?" Lily said.
The lady in the blue dress looked down at Lily and smiled. She recognized her as her friend's niece. She was very cute and polite.
"Hello, Lily. I'm Miss Anna. I'm your aunt's friend. You are very sweet. I can teach you some dance moves, if you like. Do you want to try?" Miss Anna said.
"Yes, please. I love to dance," Lily said.
Miss Anna took Lily's hand and led her to a corner of the room. She showed her how to stand on her toes, how to spin, and how to jump. Lily followed her and tried to copy her. She felt happy and proud. She was learning how to dance like a graceful lady.

Once upon a time, there was a colorful cat. The cat had lots of fur. It could change its fur. One day, the cat met a dog. The dog had brown fur. The cat wanted to be friends with the dog.
"Hi, dog! Let's play!" said the cat. The dog looked at the cat and smiled. "Yes, let's play, cat!" said the dog. They played and had lots of fun. The cat changed its fur to brown like the dog.
The next day, the cat and the dog met a bird. The bird had blue feathers. The cat changed its fur to blue like the bird. "Hi, bird! Can we be friends?" asked the cat. The bird said, "Yes, we can be friends!"
Now, the cat, the dog, and the bird were all friends. They played together every day. The cat liked to change its fur to match its friends. They were very happy and had lots of fun.

Once upon a time, there was a girl named Lily. She loved to play with her dog, Spot, in the park. One day, they found a soft ball near a tree. Lily picked it up and said, "Let's play, Spot!"
Lily threw the ball far away, and Spot ran to catch it. But when he came back, he had a big cone in his mouth. Lily laughed and said, "Silly Spot, that's not the ball!" She encouraged Spot to find the ball by saying, "Go, Spot, go! Find the ball!"
Spot ran off again and this time, he found the soft ball. He brought it back to Lily, and they played all day long. At the end of the day, Lily and Spot took the cone home, too. It became a special toy for them to play with together.

Once upon a time, there was a big chair that everyone in the house loved to seat on. It was so soft and comfy. The chair was very proud and always told the other chairs, "I am the best seat in the house!" The other chairs were jealous because they wanted to be the best seat too.
One day, a little girl was reading a book with many pages. She liked to turn the pages and look at the pretty pictures. She sat on the big chair to read her book. The jealous chairs watched her and became very sad. They wanted the little girl to sit on them too.
The jealous chairs thought of a plan. They pushed the big chair away when no one was looking. Then, the little girl came back to read her book. She looked for the big chair, but it was gone. She had to sit on the hard floor, and her book fell. The pages ripped and the little girl cried. The chairs felt bad for what they did. They learned that being jealous and doing mean things can hurt others.

Once upon a time, there was a lively little girl named Mia. Mia loved to play with her puzzle. One day, Mia saw a big box in her room. She did not know what was inside. Mia felt something would happen.
Mia opened the big box. Inside the box, she found a new puzzle. The puzzle had a picture of a pretty garden. Mia was very happy. She started to put the puzzle together. She knew something good was going to happen when she finished it.
Mia worked hard on the puzzle. She put all the pieces together. When she finished, a pretty flower from the puzzle came to life! The flower jumped out of the puzzle and danced around her room. Mia was so happy. She knew something special would happen, and it did!

Tommy was a very obedient three year old. His mummy was very proud of him. Tommy loved playing with his friends in the park.
One day, Tommy and his friends were playing in the park. Suddenly, they heard a loud scream. It sounded like someone was in trouble!
Tommy got scared and tried to run back to his mummy. But one of his friends grabbed him and said, "Tommy, we have to help whoever is screaming."
Tommy nodded and they all ran towards the source of the scream.
The friends were shocked to find a little boy stuck inside a toilet. He was screaming and crying.
Tommy and his friends were brave and decided to help. They worked together to find a way to open the toilet and soon, the little boy was freed.
The friends all cheered and thanked Tommy for being so brave and obedient. Mummy was very proud to hear about it.

Mia liked to play with clay. She had many colors of clay and she made animals and flowers and houses with them. She put her clay in a big box and kept it in her room.
One day, Mia went to visit her grandma with her mom and dad. She was happy to see her grandma, who gave her hugs and cookies and read her stories. But she also missed her clay. She wanted to make something for her grandma with her clay.
She asked her mom if she could bring her clay box, but her mom said no. She said the clay box was too heavy and they had no space in the car. She said Mia could play with other toys at grandma's house. Mia felt frustrated. She did not want to play with other toys. She wanted to play with her clay.
She tried to have fun at grandma's house, but she could not stop thinking about her clay. She looked for clay in grandma's drawers and closets, but she only found old papers and buttons and yarn. She asked grandma if she had any clay, but grandma said no. She said she had no clay, but she had some dough. She said Mia could play with the dough and make something with it.
Mia was curious. She had never played with dough before. She followed grandma to the kitchen and saw a big bowl of white dough. Grandma gave her some dough and showed her how to roll it and shape it. Mia felt the dough in her hands. It was soft and sticky and warm. It was different from clay, but it was also fun. She started to make something with the dough. She made a round ball for the head, a long snake for the body, four small balls for the legs, and two ears. She made a dog with the dough.
She showed her dog to grandma, who smiled and praised her. She said Mia was very clever and creative. She said she liked her dog very much. She said she would bake the dog in the oven and then Mia could paint it and keep it. Mia felt happy. She still missed her clay, but she also liked her dough dog. She hugged grandma and thanked her. She decided to make more things with the dough. She made a cat, a bird, a flower, and a house. She had fun with the dough. She did not feel frustrated anymore.

Anna and Ben like to play hockey with their friends. They have sticks and a ball and two goals. They wear orange shirts and helmets. They have fun and try to score.
One day, a new boy comes to play with them. His name is Max. He has a big stick and a blue shirt. He says he is very good at hockey. He wants to play with Anna and Ben.
"Can I join your team?" he asks.
"Sure, you can," Anna says. "We are the orange team. You can wear this orange helmet."
"No, I don't want to wear orange. I like blue. Blue is better," Max says. He frowns and crosses his arms.
"But we are the orange team. We have to wear the same color," Ben says.
"I don't care. I want to wear blue. And I want to score all the goals. You have to pass me the ball," Max says. He frowns more and stamps his feet.
Anna and Ben look at each other. They don't like Max. He is not nice. He is not fun. He is bossy and rude.
"Sorry, Max. You can't play with us. You don't follow the rules. You don't share. You don't smile. You only frown. You are not a good friend," Anna says.
"Yeah, Max. You can't play with us. You can play by yourself. Or you can find another team. But not the orange team. We don't want you," Ben says.
Anna and Ben turn away from Max. They take their sticks and their ball and their helmets. They go to play with their other friends. They have fun and try to score. They smile and laugh. They are happy. They are the orange team.

Once upon a time, there was a beautiful girl named Lily. She loved to eat dinner with her family every day. Her mom made yummy food for them to eat. Lily's favorite was when they had pizza for dinner.
One day, Lily saw an apple on the table. It was not a good apple. It was old and starting to rot. Lily did not want to eat the apple. She told her mom about the bad apple.
Her mom took the bad apple away and gave Lily a fresh, new apple. Lily was happy to eat the new apple. It was sweet and crunchy. She finished her dinner and went to play with her toys.

One day, a grumpy cat named Tom went for a walk. He saw a big tree and decided to climb it. While he was up in the tree, he met a happy bird. The bird said, "Hi, I'm Sam. I love to sing!"
Tom said, "I'm grumpy. I don't like to sing. I just want to sit and get some air." Sam knew that Tom meant he needed oxygen. Sam wanted to help Tom feel better.
Sam said, "Let's play a game! If you can catch me, I will help you feel better." Tom agreed and they played the game. Tom caught Sam and they both laughed. They became good friends and Tom wasn't grumpy anymore. He learned that love and oxygen can make you happy.

Once upon a time there was a brave kitty called Daisy. Daisy lived near a river and loved to play in the river. One day, while she was happily playing in the river, she heard a noise that made her very scared. She looked around but couldn't see anything, so Daisy crept out of the river and waited to see what was disturbing her.
Suddenly, she saw that a big bird had knocked over a bottle of shampoo. It was making a big mess and some of the shampoo had even gone into the river. Daisy was so scared that she ran away, weaving and dodging through the bushes.
When Daisy got to a safe distance, she stopped to catch her breath. That's when she heard a voice. It said, "why are you so scared? My name is Big Bird and I'm just trying to clean up the mess."
Daisy wasn't so scared anymore, so she came closer and helped Big Bird clean up the shampoo. When it was all tidied up, Big Bird thanked Daisy for her help and flew away. She watched it fly away until it disappeared into the clear blue sky. Daisy was no longer scared and the river was clean again!

Lila and Ben like to play in the park. They see many pigeons on the grass. They want to feed them some bread. They break the bread into small pieces and throw them to the pigeons.
"Look, Ben, the pigeons are happy!" Lila says.
"Yes, they are. They make funny noises. Coo, coo!" Ben says.
They watch the pigeons eat the bread. One pigeon is different from the others. It has a shiny ring on its leg. Lila thinks it is important.
"Ben, do you see that pigeon? It has a ring. Maybe it is a special pigeon. Maybe it has a name." Lila says.
"Maybe. Let's try to catch it. Maybe it can tell us something." Ben says.
They run after the pigeon with the ring. The pigeon sees them and flies away. It lands on a fountain. Lila and Ben follow it. They try to reach it, but it is too high. They see the water in the fountain. It is spinning and sparkling.
"Wow, look at the water, Lila. It is spinning. It is pretty." Ben says.
"Yes, it is. Maybe the pigeon likes the water. Maybe it wants to spin too." Lila says.
They watch the pigeon on the fountain. It flaps its wings and makes a sound. It looks at them and then at the water. It jumps into the water and starts to spin. Lila and Ben laugh and clap their hands.
"Look, Ben, the pigeon is spinning. It is having fun. It is a happy pigeon." Lila says.
"Yes, it is. It is a special pigeon. It has a ring and it can spin. It is an important pigeon." Ben says.
They smile and wave at the pigeon. The pigeon spins and waves back. They are all happy.

One day, they saw a big oak tree with a hole in its trunk. They wondered what was inside. They decided to test it out.
"Let's play hide and seek," Tim said. "You hide in the hole and I'll count to ten."
"OK," Lily said. She ran to the hole and crawled inside. It was dark and cozy. She heard Tim counting outside.
"One, two, three..."
Lily waited for Tim to find her. She smiled. She was good at hiding.
But then, she heard a growl. She felt something furry and wet touch her leg. She screamed.
"Help! Help! There's a dog in here!"
Tim heard Lily's scream. He ran to the hole and looked inside. He saw a playful dog wagging its tail and licking Lily's face. He laughed.
"Don't be scared, Lily. It's just a dog. He wants to play with you."
Lily calmed down. She saw the dog's friendly eyes and soft ears. She petted the dog and laughed too.
"Hi, dog. You're nice. What's your name?"
The dog barked. He liked Tim and Lily. He wanted to be their friend.
The end.

Lila and Tom were playing in the garden. They liked to look at the flowers and smell them. Lila saw a big red flower that she had never seen before. It was very pretty and bright.
"Look, Tom, look!" she said. "This flower is new. It is red like a flame. Do you know its name?"
Tom came closer and looked at the flower. He shook his head. He did not know its name either. He touched one of the petals. It was soft and warm.
"I don't know its name, Lila. It is unknown to me. Maybe it is a magic flower. Maybe it can grant wishes. What do you wish for, Lila?"
Lila thought for a moment. She had many wishes, but she wanted to share one with Tom. She smiled and said:
"I wish for more flowers like this one. I wish they would bloom all over the garden and make it more beautiful. What do you wish for, Tom?"
Tom thought for a moment too. He had many wishes, but he wanted to share one with Lila. He smiled and said:
"I wish for more fun and games with you, Lila. I wish we would always be friends and play together. Do you think the flower will hear us, Lila?"
Lila nodded. She hoped the flower would hear them and make their wishes come true. She and Tom held hands and closed their eyes. They said their wishes out loud and thanked the flower.
Then they opened their eyes and gasped. The garden was full of red flowers like the one they had found. They were blooming everywhere, making the garden look like a firework. Lila and Tom laughed and clapped. They ran around and picked some flowers. They gave them to each other and to their parents. They had a lot of fun and games that day. They were very happy and grateful. They never forgot the unknown flower that granted their wishes.

Once upon a time, in a jolly little town, there lived a group of animals. There was a dog, a cat, a bird, and a fish. They were all friends, but they never played together. They always took turns to play with each other.
One day, a big storm came to the town. The wind blew, and the rain fell. The animals were scared. They did not know what to do. They each ran to their own homes to hide from the storm.
Then, the dog had an idea. He thought, "Why don't we all unite and face the storm together?" So, he went to the cat, the bird, and the fish, and they all agreed. They came together and faced the storm as one.
As they stood together, the storm seemed less scary. They were not alone, and they felt strong. The storm soon went away, and the sun came out. The animals were happy and knew that being united made them stronger.
From that day on, the dog, the cat, the bird, and the fish always played together. They took turns to play games and have fun. They were a united, jolly group of friends, and they lived happily ever after.

One day, Mark met a little bird who was not scared of him. The bird said, "I like your loud sounds, they are fun!" Mark was happy that someone liked his loud noises. From that day on, Mark and the little bird became best friends. They played together and made loud noises all day long.

Once upon a time, there was a playful bear who loved a good listen. Every day, he would sit, listen, and dream. One day, his toe started to tingle, so he just had to take a look. When he finally peered down at his toes, he saw something beyond his wildest dreams - a glimmering key! He just knew it was the key to something special, so he listened for any clues.
The wind began to whisper, and the bear heard something about a magical door. The bear's toes began to tingle once more, and he just had to investigate further. The bear listened to the wind and followed it to an ancient tree with a door covered in vines. The bear knew he could use the key to unlock this door - and with a gleam in his eyes, he grabbed the key and began to turn it.
As the door slowly opened, the bear was met with a magical new world. Trails of mist swirled around him, and the cheerful sound of laughter filled the air. Then, suddenly, the bear noticed a sign that said, "Come play in our magical world." The bear quickly realized that his toe really did know something special - something that only the most playful of bears can find.

Tim and Sam are brothers. They like to play outside in the yard. They have a big tree with many branches. They use the branches to make forts and swords and other things.
One day, Tim finds a branch that is very long and strong. He thinks it is perfect for his fort. He takes it to his corner of the yard and starts to build. Sam sees the branch and wants it too. He thinks it is perfect for his sword. He runs to Tim and tries to take the branch.
"Stop!" Tim says. "This is my branch. I found it first. Go find your own branch."
"No!" Sam says. "I want this branch. It is the best branch. Give it to me now."
They pull and tug on the branch. They yell and cry. They are very angry. They do not share. They do not listen.
The branch is not happy. It does not like to be pulled and tugged. It does not like to be yelled and cried. It does not like to be used for forts and swords. It wants to be free. It wants to be on the tree.
The branch snaps. It breaks in two. It falls to the ground. It is destroyed.
Tim and Sam stop. They look at the branch. They look at each other. They are very sad. They have no branch. They have no fort. They have no sword. They have no fun.
They hear a sound. It is a bird. It is on the tree. It is very small and cute. It is adorable. It sings a song. It sounds happy. It has a nest. It has a home. It has a family.
Tim and Sam look at the bird. They look at the tree. They look at the branch. They understand. They are sorry. They say sorry to the branch. They say sorry to the bird. They say sorry to each other.
They hug. They smile. They are friends. They go to the tree. They find another branch. It is not very long or strong. It is not perfect for forts or swords. But it is perfect for them. They use it to play together. They have fun. They are happy. The end.

Kitty said, "I wanted to see what was up here in the sun!" The bird and Kitty became friends. They played together in the sun all day. And they lived happily ever after.

One day, a big red bus was driving down the street. The bus was very tight because it had many people inside. The bus driver was a nice man who liked to count the number of people who got on his bus.
A little girl named Amy got on the bus with her mom. Amy saw the bus driver counting the people. She asked, "What are you doing?"
The bus driver replied, "I am counting the number of people on my bus. Would you like to help me?"
Amy was excited and said, "Yes!" So, Amy and the bus driver counted the people together. They counted one, two, three, four, and so on. They had a fun time counting together. At the end of the day, Amy and her mom got off the bus and said goodbye to the bus driver. The bus driver was happy he had a helper and continued to count the number of people on his bus every day.

There was a big button. It was red and shiny. Everyone wanted to touch it. They were jealous of the button.
A girl named Lily asked her mom, "Can I touch the button?" Her mom said, "No, I will not permit you to touch the button." Lily was sad.
One day, Lily's mom was not looking. Lily touched the button. It made a fun sound. Lily was happy.

One day, a jolly frog named Bob hopped near a small volcano. He liked to play and jump around the volcano. The volcano was not hot, so it was safe for Bob.
Bob met a bird named Sally. Sally said, "Bob, let's play a game near the water." Bob agreed, and they went to the water to play. They splashed and had fun. Then, they decided to soak in the water to cool down.
While they were soaking, the small volcano started to shake. Bob and Sally were scared. They ran away from the volcano as fast as they could. The volcano did not get hot, but it made a big noise. Bob and Sally learned that it is good to be careful and not play too close to things that can be dangerous.

Once upon a time, there was a loud dancer named Lily. She loved to dance and play with her friends. One day, she met a new friend named Tim.
Tim was shy and didn't like loud noises. Lily wanted to play with Tim, so she asked, "Do you want to dance with me?" Tim was scared of the loud music, so he said, "No, thank you."
Lily had an idea. She started to poke Tim gently and said, "Let's play a quiet game instead." They played and had fun together. Soon, Tim wasn't shy anymore.
The other kids saw Lily and Tim playing and wanted to join. They all played together and had a great time. In the end, they learned that it's important to be kind and think about how others feel.

Lily and Ben are friends. They like to play in the park. One day, they see a bird in a tree. The bird is singing a song. Lily and Ben listen to the song.
"Hello, bird!" Lily says. "You have a nice song."
The bird stops singing. It looks at Lily and Ben. It says, "Thank you, children. You are very kind."
Lily and Ben are surprised. They did not know that the bird can talk.
"Wow, you can talk!" Ben says. "You are a clever bird."
The bird smiles. It says, "Yes, I can talk. I learned from other birds and people. I like to learn new words and songs."
Lily and Ben are curious. They ask the bird, "Can you teach us a new song?"
The bird nods. It says, "Of course. I know many songs. I will teach you one that I learned from a friend. It is a song about friendship."
Lily and Ben are happy. They say, "Yes, please. We want to learn your song."
The bird says, "Okay. Listen carefully. I will sing it first, and then you can repeat after me."
The bird sings a song. It is a simple and sweet song. Lily and Ben like the song. They try to sing along. The bird helps them with the words. Soon, they can sing the song together.
The bird says, "You are doing great. You are fast learners. You are clever children."
Lily and Ben say, "Thank you, bird. You are a good teacher. You are a friendly bird."
The bird says, "You are welcome, children. You are good students. You are nice friends."
Lily and Ben smile. They say, "We are friends. We like your song. We like you, bird."
The bird smiles. It says, "I like you, too, children. You make me happy. You are my friends, too."
They hug the bird. They sing the song again. They are happy. They are friends.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Amy. She had a dream that she could fly. Every day, she would sit in her backyard and look up at the sky, wishing she could join the birds up high.
One day, as Amy sat in her yard, a kind bird flew down to her. The bird said, "I can help you fly, but you must wait one year." Amy was so happy and promised to wait. She told her mom and dad about her new friend and the dream that would come true.
A year went by, and Amy never forgot her dream. She was so excited when the bird came back. The bird touched her with its wing, and suddenly, Amy could fly! She soared through the sky, laughing and playing with her bird friend. The little girl's dream had come true, and she was the happiest child in the world.

Once upon a time, there was a pirate named Bob. Bob was a selfish pirate. He did not like to share his toys with others. One day, Bob found a big treasure chest.
Bob said, "This treasure is mine!" He did not want to share it with his pirate friends. His friends were sad, but they knew Bob would remain selfish.
One of his friends, Tim, decided to talk to Bob. Tim said, "Bob, we should share the treasure. We are friends." Bob thought about it and knew Tim was right. He decided to share the treasure with his friends. They were all very happy.

Once upon a time, there was a dog. The dog had a name. The name was Spot. Spot was an alert dog. He liked to play and run.
One day, Spot saw a cat. The cat needed help. Spot was a good dog, so he helped the cat. They became friends. The cat's name was Fluffy.
But, guess what? Fluffy was not a cat! Fluffy was a dog too! Fluffy just looked like a cat. Spot and Fluffy were happy to be dog friends. They played and ran together every day.

Anna and Ben were playing in the park. They saw a big hill with many rocks and trees. They wanted to climb the hill and see what was on the other side.
"Let's go, Ben!" Anna said. "Maybe we can find something fun!"
"OK, Anna!" Ben said. "But be careful, the rocks are sharp and the trees are prickly."
They started to climb the hill, holding hands and helping each other. They saw some birds and bugs and flowers on the way. They were happy and curious.
But when they reached the top of the hill, they saw something scary. There was a big hole in the ground, and inside the hole there was a huge snake. The snake was ancient, with scales and fangs and a long tail. It was sleeping, but it looked angry and hungry.
"Anna, look!" Ben whispered. "It's a snake! A big, big snake!"
"Shh, Ben!" Anna whispered back. "Don't wake it up! Let's go back, now!"
But it was too late. The snake heard them and opened its eyes. It saw the two children and hissed. It was hungry and wanted to eat them.
"Run, Anna, run!" Ben shouted. "The snake is coming!"
They tried to run, but the snake was faster. It slithered out of the hole and chased them down the hill. It caught up with them and wrapped its tail around them. They screamed and cried, but no one heard them. The snake squeezed them and bit them. They stopped screaming and crying. They stopped moving and breathing. The snake ate them and went back to the hole. It lied down and slept again. It was full and happy.

Once upon a time, there was a big, enormous dog named Buddy. Buddy had a small friend named Tim. Tim was a tiny bird. They liked to play in the park.
One sunny day, Buddy and Tim were playing a game. Buddy said, "Let's number our toes!" So, Buddy and Tim started counting their toes. Buddy had four toes on each foot. Tim had three toes on each foot.
As they were counting, a little girl named Sue came up to them. Sue said, "Hi Buddy and Tim! Can I play too?" Buddy and Tim said, "Yes, let's all number our toes together!" So, Sue counted her toes too. She had five toes on each foot.
They all laughed and had fun counting their toes. Buddy, Tim, and Sue played in the park until the sun went down. They were very happy to be friends.

Little Jimmy wanted to wear a costume. So one day, he asked his mom for a costume. His mom said yes, so they went to the store. Jimmy was so excited he couldn't sit still!
They looked at lots of costumes, and Jimmy found one he liked. It was blue with wings and a mask. His mom said it was perfect, and bought it for him.
When they got home, Jimmy wanted to put it on right away. But his mom said he had to tie the wings so he wouldn't trip. So Jimmy had to do it himself.
Jimmy was so serious. He really wanted to do it right. He carefully tied the wings one time, then he tied them twice. When he was done, he proudly showed it to his mom.
His mom was so proud that Jimmy had done such a good job. Jimmy proudly put on his costume and twirled around the room. He was ready for some serious costume fun!

Once upon a time, in a big forest, there was a light green tree. This tree was very special. It had a secret. The tree could bring peace to all the animals in the forest.
One day, a big lion came to the forest. He was very loud. He would roar all day and all night. All the animals were scared of the lion. They did not know what to do.
The light green tree had an idea. It wanted to help the animals. The tree told the animals to come and touch its leaves. When the animals touched the leaves, they felt peace.
The lion heard about the tree and went to see it. He touched the leaves too. Suddenly, the lion stopped roaring. He felt happy and calm. The plot twist was that the lion was not mean, he just needed peace.
Now, all the animals in the forest lived together in peace. The lion became friends with everyone. The light green tree was very happy to see the animals happy. And they all lived happily ever after.

One day, a charming little boat wanted to sail on the big sea. The boat was happy and excited to go on an adventure. It knew that it would be a fun trip. The boat had a friend who was a cop. The cop said, "Be careful on your trip, little boat. The sea can be tricky."
The little boat started to sail on the big sea. The sun was shining, and the waves were calm. The boat was having a great time. It remembered what the cop friend had said, so it was careful not to go too far from the shore.
Then, the sky became dark, and the sea became rough. The little boat was scared. But it remembered what the cop friend said about the sea being tricky. The boat knew what to do. It sailed back to the shore safely. The cop friend was waiting and was happy to see the little boat safe. They both knew that being careful and listening to friends was very important.

Once upon a time, there was a little girl named Lily. She loved to push her toy truck around her house. One sunny day, Lily saw the mailman come to her house. He had a big bag full of mail.
The mailman had an unusual letter for Lily. It was colorful and had her name on it. Lily was very excited. She ran to her mom and said, "Mommy, look! I got mail!"
Her mom smiled and helped her open the letter. Inside was a picture of a beautiful butterfly. Lily's friend, Max, had sent her the picture. Lily was so happy. She decided to push her toy truck to Max's house to say thank you. And they played together all day long.

Lily and Tom were friends who liked to play outside. One day, they wanted to search for the sun, because it was cold and gray. They put on their hats and coats and went to the park.
"Where is the sun?" Lily asked. "Maybe it is hiding behind the trees."
They ran to the trees and looked up, but they did not see the sun. They only saw leaves and branches.
"Maybe it is hiding behind the clouds," Tom said. "Let's climb the hill and see."
They climbed the hill and looked at the sky, but they did not see the sun. They only saw clouds and wind.
"Maybe it is hiding behind the moon," Lily said. "Let's wait until night and see."
They waited until night and looked at the stars, but they did not see the sun. They only saw the moon and the dark.
They felt sad and cold. They missed the sun and its warmth and light.
"Maybe the sun is not hiding," Tom said. "Maybe it is just sleeping. Maybe it will wake up tomorrow and shine again."
"Maybe you are right," Lily said. "Let's go home and sleep too. Maybe we will see the sun in our dreams."
They went home and hugged their moms and dads. They said good night and went to bed. They dreamed of the sun and its smile.
The next day, they woke up and saw the sun shining through their windows. They felt happy and warm. They ran outside and played with the sun.
They learned that the sun does not always hide. Sometimes it just needs to rest. And they learned to be patient and hopeful. Because the sun always comes back.

One day, a bird named Blue made a nest in a big tree. The tree had many flowers that would bloom. Blue loved to watch the flowers open. They were pretty and smelled nice.
Blue had a friend named Red. Red was a squirrel. Red liked to eat delicious nuts from the tree. One day, Red said, "Blue, I found a big nut! Let's share it!" Blue was happy and flew down to eat the nut with Red.
But when they opened the nut, they found a tiny baby bird inside! It was a surprise. Blue said, "Oh! A baby bird! We need to take care of it." So, Blue and Red took care of the baby bird in the nest. They became a happy family in the tree with the blooming flowers.

Once upon a time, there was a little car named Zoomy. Zoomy was red and shiny. He loved to zoom fast down the street. One day, Zoomy saw a big wrap on the ground. It was a perfect wrap with pretty colors.
Zoomy went to his friend, a blue truck named Trucky. "Trucky, look at this perfect wrap I found!" Zoomy said. Trucky looked at the wrap and said, "Wow, that is a perfect wrap! What are you going to do with it?"
Zoomy thought for a moment and said, "I want to wear it like a cape and zoom even faster!" So, Zoomy put the wrap around his neck like a cape. He felt very special.
Zoomy started to zoom with his new cape. He went faster and faster. But then, the wrap got caught on a tree. Zoomy couldn't stop! He zoomed right into a big wall. The wrap was torn and Zoomy was sad. He had to go home with a broken cape and a big bump.

Once upon a time, there was a little boy named Tim. He had a brother named Sam. Tim was popular at school because he always behaved well. Sam wanted to be popular too, but he did not behave well.
One day, Sam asked Tim, "How can I be popular like you?" Tim said, "You need to behave well. Be kind to others and share your toys."
Sam started to behave well, and soon he became popular too. All the kids at school liked him. Sam was happy and thanked his brother for the advice.
The moral of the story is: if you want to be popular, you should behave well and be kind to others.

Once upon a time, there was a big blue ball. The blue ball was very sad. It had a task to do. The task was to stop a little girl from falling down.
One day, the little girl was playing near a hill. The blue ball saw her and knew it had to help. The blue ball rolled to the girl and said, "You must not go up the hill. I am here to prevent you from falling."
The little girl did not listen to the blue ball. She went up the hill anyway. The blue ball tried to follow her, but it was too slow. The girl was too fast for the blue ball.
At the top of the hill, the little girl tripped and fell down. The blue ball could not prevent her fall. The girl was hurt and cried. The blue ball was sad because it could not do its task. The bad ending made the blue ball feel like it had failed.

Once upon a time, in a big tree, there lived a gray monkey. The gray monkey was very happy. He liked to jump and play all day long. One day, while the gray monkey was playing, he saw a little boy. The little boy was sad. The gray monkey wanted to help.
"Hi, little boy!" said the gray monkey. "Why are you sad? Do you want to play with me?"
The little boy looked up and saw the gray monkey. "Yes, I want to play," said the little boy. "But I can't find my ball. Can you help me find it?"
The gray monkey was happy to help. He used his mind to think where the ball might be. He looked high and low. Finally, he found the ball under a bush. The gray monkey brought the ball to the little boy.
"Here is your ball!" said the gray monkey. The little boy was very happy. They played together all day long. From that day on, the gray monkey and the little boy were best friends. They always had fun and helped each other.
